### [100] [Earth as a transducer for ultralight bosonic dark-matter detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16342)
*Saarik Kalia,Ibrahim A. Sulai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了超轻玻色暗物质（UBDM）通过"地球换能器"效应在地球表面产生振荡磁场信号的原理与探测前景。该效应利用地球半径（R）作为信号放大尺度，对质量≤3×10⁻¹⁴ eV的UBDM具有极强的直接探测能力，并具有高时空相干性、对大气模型不敏感等优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用地球作为天然换能器来探测超轻玻色暗物质，弥补传统实验室实验在极低质量暗物质探测上的不足。

Method: 理论推导了多种UBDM模型下的地球换能器效应及其特性，分析了信号的空间/时间相干性、对大气模型的鲁棒性等性质。

Result: 地球换能器信号与地球半径R成正比，在质量m_DM≲1/R∼3×10⁻¹⁴ eV范围内是最强的直接探测手段之一，信号具有高空间/时间相干性且不依赖大气建模。

Conclusion: 综述了现有和未来探测地球换能器效应的前景，指出该效应是探测极低质量UBDM的重要途径。

Abstract: Ultralight bosonic dark matter (UBDM) that couples to electromagnetism can generate an oscillating magnetic-field signal at the Earth's surface. This is referred to as the "Earth transducer" effect, as the Earth converts UBDM into a detectable magnetic field. Similar DM-induced fields in laboratory experiments typically scale with the size $L$ of the experiment. Because the Earth transducer signal instead scales with the large radius of the Earth, $R$, it is one of the most powerful direct probes of UBDM with masses $m_\mathrm{DM}\lesssim1/R\sim3\times10^{-14}\,\mathrm{eV}$. It has many other favorable properties, such as high spatial and temporal coherence and robustness to atmospheric modeling. In this review, we derive the Earth transducer effect and its properties for multiple UBDM models, and discuss current and future prospects to detect it.

</details>


### [101] [Transverse Charge Distribution as a Probe of Nucleon Transversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16392)
*Wanchen Li,Xiaohui Liu,Ding Yu Shao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出横向电荷分布作为碎片化和核子层析成像的自旋敏感电荷流探针，通过测量碎裂夸克周围净电荷的角度分布，完全依赖带电粒子方向与电荷符号的径迹追踪，无需量热能量测量。领头阶下分布分解为无极化电荷单极和手征奇性横向电荷偶极，其中偶极受Collins效应支配并直接耦合横向自旋分布。应用于RHIC横向极化p↑p碰撞，电荷加权可抑制无极化单极背景，使相反电荷强子的自旋相关偶极相干叠加，显著增强方位角不对称性，为提取横向自旋分布建立了理论上干净、实验上精确的纯径迹途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核子层析成像工具（如横向动量依赖分布）依赖于量热能量测量和强子身份识别，实验上复杂且受系统误差限制。本文旨在设计一种仅依赖带电粒子径迹（方向和电荷符号）的探针，避免量热测量的需求，同时实现对横向自旋分布（transversity）的敏感探测，从而简化实验并提高精度。

Method: (1) 定义横向电荷分布作为可观测，测量碎裂夸克周围净电荷的角分布；(2) 在领头扭度下将其分解为无极化电荷单极（领头阶）和手征奇性电荷偶极（子领头阶）；(3) 建立算符乘积展开（OPE），将分布与电荷加权的共线矩关联，证明单极由电荷守恒固定，偶极由Collins碎裂函数支配并与transversity直接耦合；(4) 应用于RHIC横向极化p↑p碰撞，展示电荷加权机制如何抑制无极化背景并使相反电荷贡献相干增强。

Result: 在横向极化p↑p碰撞中，电荷加权有效抑制了无极化单极背景，并使来自正负电荷强子的自旋依赖偶极贡献相干叠加，大幅增强了净方位角不对称性。该信号理论上纯净且仅需径迹信息（带电粒子方向和电荷符号），无需量热测量或强子类型识别。

Conclusion: 横向电荷分布为提取横向自旋分布提供了一种理论上干净、实验上精确的新途径，完全基于带电粒子径迹追踪，规避了量热能量测量的复杂性和系统误差。该可观测在RHIC实验条件下具有显著的可实现性和灵敏度。

Abstract: We introduce the transverse charge distribution as a spin-sensitive charge-flow probe for fragmentation and nucleon tomography. By measuring the angular distribution of net electric charge around a fragmenting quark, this observable relies entirely on the tracking of charged-particle directions and charge signs, strictly bypassing the need for calorimetric energy measurements. At leading twist, the distribution decomposes into an unpolarized charge monopole and a chiral-odd transverse charge dipole. We derive the operator product expansion of these distributions onto charge-weighted collinear moments: the monopole is fixed by charge conservation, while the dipole is governed by the Collins effect and couples directly to transversity. Applying this formalism to transversely polarized $p^\uparrow p$ collisions at RHIC, we show that charge weighting suppresses the unpolarized monopole background and causes the spin-dependent dipoles from oppositely charged hadrons to add coherently. This coherence strongly enhances the resulting azimuthal asymmetries, establishing a theoretically clean and experimentally precise track-only avenue for extracting transversity.

</details>


### [102] [Attractodynamics in 0+1D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16393)
*Jean F. Du Plessis,Bruno Scheihing-Hitschfeld,Rachel Steinhorst*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"吸引子动力学"(attractodynamics)，作为远离平衡态吸引子附近动力学的宏观理论框架，并在0+1D模型中通过精确已知的各向异性吸引子解进行验证，展示了理想吸引子动力学及其瞬态修正的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体力学是围绕局域热平衡的长波长动力学宏观理论，但远离平衡态的吸引子附近动力学缺乏类似的系统框架。本文旨在发展吸引子动力学，系统组织远离平衡态吸引子附近的非流体力学微观信息。

Method: 从0+1D模型出发，利用一个精确已知的各向异性远离平衡吸引子解作为基准，构建理想吸引子动力学方程及其领先阶瞬态残余扩展，并与完整动力学演化进行直接比较。

Result: 理想吸引子动力学在吸引子流形附近捕捉到了正确的演化行为，而保留领先阶偏离吸引子的矩则扩展了有效描述范围，直至有限截断失效。

Conclusion: 该0+1D示例识别了构建局域3+1D吸引子动力学以及不依赖于底层动力学描述的宏观吸引子动力学理论所需的关键要素。

Abstract: Hydrodynamics is a macroscopic theory of long-wavelength dynamics around local thermal equilibrium. We develop attractodynamics, the analogous construction around a far-from-equilibrium attractor. Dynamics near a far-from-equilibrium attractor retains some non-hydrodynamic microscopic information, which attractodynamics systematically organizes. We move towards this general structure by starting in 0+1D, and consider a model for which an anisotropic far-from-equilibrium attractor solution is exactly known. This setting provides a clean benchmark in which the ideal attractodynamic equations and a leading transient residual extension can be compared directly with the full kinetic evolution. The resulting hierarchy gives an improvable description of near-attractor dynamics: the ideal theory captures evolution close to the attracting manifold, while retaining the leading off-attractor moments extends the regime of agreement until the finite truncation breaks down. This example identifies the ingredients needed for local 3+1D attractodynamics and for macroscopic attractodynamic theories not derived from an underlying kinetic description.

</details>


### [103] [Particle productions during collisions of highly boosted bubble walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16398)
*Haipeng An,Hongyi Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇宙学一阶相变中高度洛伦兹收缩的泡泡壁碰撞产生远超特征尺度重粒子的机制。通过标量序参量场，推导了弹性与非弹性碰撞下傅里叶空间轮廓的紫外行为，发现谱密度普遍标度为$F(χ)\propto [V^\prime(2v_φ)]^2χ^{-4}$，与势能导数有关。利用高精度数值解验证了该行为，并推导了重粒子产生率、费米子对产生率及宇宙学数密度。扩展到(3+1)维并考虑有限泡泡半径后，发现相比平行壁近似存在量级为1的压低。该结果修正了先前处理的紫外标度，对超重暗物质产生和泡泡碰撞重子生成有直接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶相变中泡泡壁碰撞是高能粒子产生的重要机制，但此前对远超特征尺度重粒子的产生研究不足，紫外标度行为存在争议，需要建立普适理论框架。

Method: 利用标量序参量场推导傅里叶空间轮廓的紫外渐进行为（弹性与非弹性碰撞）；采用高精度数值求解 trapping 方程并抑制有限积分域谱泄漏；解析推导重粒子产生率与费米子对产生率；扩展至(3+1)维并引入有限泡泡半径修正。

Result: 在$χ\gg M_h^2$区域得到普适结果$\tildeφ(χ) = -2V^\prime(2v_φ)χ^{-2}+O(χ^{-3})$，谱密度$F(χ)\propto [V^\prime(2v_φ)]^2χ^{-4}$；数值验证与解析结果在广泛紫外范围一致；有限泡泡半径效应导致约O(1)的压低因子。

Conclusion: 重粒子产生定域于碰撞瞬间，领头阶仅依赖标量势在$V^\prime(2v_φ)$处的导数；该结果修正了先前处理的紫外标度，对超重暗物质与重子生成有重要理论意义。

Abstract: We investigate the production of particles much heavier than the characteristic scale of a cosmological first-order phase transition through collisions of highly boosted bubble walls. Using the scalar order-parameter field, we derive the ultraviolet behavior of its Fourier-space profile for both elastic and inelastic collisions. In the regime $χ\equivω^2-\mathbf{k}^2\gg M_h^2$, we find the universal result $\tildeφ(χ) = -2V^\prime(2v_φ)χ^{-2}+O(χ^{-3}),$ implying that the spectral density scales as $F(χ)\propto [V^\prime(2v_φ)]^2χ^{-4}$. Thus, heavy-particle production is localized near the instant of collision and, at leading order, depends on the scalar potential only through $V^\prime(2v_φ)$. We verify this behavior using high-precision numerical solutions of the trapping equation, carefully suppressing spectral leakage from the finite integration domain, and obtain agreement over a broad ultraviolet range. We then derive analytical production rates for general heavy-particle thresholds and for fermion pairs, together with their cosmological number density and yield. Finally, we extend the analysis to $(3+1)$ dimensions and incorporate the finite bubble radius, finding an order-one suppression relative to the parallel-wall approximation. Our results revise the ultraviolet scaling used in previous treatments and have direct implications for superheavy dark-matter production and baryogenesis from bubble collisions.

</details>


### [104] [Long-Lived Dark Hadrons at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16400)
*Hooman Davoudiasl,Hongkai Liu,Ethan T. Neil*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有GeV尺度禁闭的暗非阿贝尔规范 secto，该暗扇区与标准模型仅有微弱耦合，其低能谱可能包含长寿命的味对角暗π介子。这些态的信号特别适合电子-离子对撞机（EIC），因为EIC没有硬触发要求且能记录软末态粒子，可互补探测暗强子化动力学。论文提出了基准门户构建，讨论了轴子-like mediator与暗π介子之间的混合，并识别了由此产生的位移衰变信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗非阿贝尔规范扇区在GeV尺度禁闭下的低能物理，利用电子-离子对撞机（EIC）的独特能力来探测长寿命暗π介子的位移衰变信号，从而为暗物质和暗扇区提供互补的探测途径。

Method: 构建基准门户模型，引入一个与暗扇区和标准模型弱耦合的轴子-like mediator，研究该mediator与暗π介子之间的混合机制，分析在EIC上产生的位移衰变信号特征。

Result: 识别出暗π介子在EIC上的位移衰变信号，说明EIC由于无硬触发要求和能记录软粒子，在探测暗强子化动力学方面具有互补优势。

Conclusion: 电子-离子对撞机（EIC）能够有效探测长寿命暗π介子的位移衰变信号，为暗扇区的实验搜索提供了新的互补平台。

Abstract: We study a dark non-Abelian gauge sector with GeV-scale confinement. The dark sector is assumed to couple only feebly to the Standard Model, while its low-energy spectrum may contain long-lived flavor-diagonal dark pions. Signals of these states are particularly well suited to the Electron-Ion Collider (EIC), where the absence of a hard trigger requirement and the capability to record soft final-state particles offer a complementary probe of dark hadronization dynamics. We present a benchmark portal construction, discuss the mixing between an axion-like mediator and dark pions, and identify the resulting displaced-decay signature.

</details>


### [105] [Light-front diagnostics in the 't Hooft model: II. Boundary cancellations, ERBL spectral sums, and analyticity of the EMT form factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16426)
*Arkadiy I. Syamtomov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在大Nc't Hooft模型中完成了光前对角重叠的第二GPD矩的ERBL贡献，证明了两支撑区域的结合使EMT形状因子在曲率阶上解析，并提取了稳定曲率、激发态估计和纵向迹半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对角光前重叠包含近前向能动张量信息但本身不是局域矩阵元，需要完成ERBL贡献以得到完整的EMT形状因子，并研究两支撑区域如何结合以及边界指数产生的分数幂和共振对数结构如何消除。

Method: 在大Nc't Hooft模型中，通过完成第二GPD矩的ERBL贡献，分析两支撑区域的结合机制，研究边界指数产生的分数幂和β=1/2处的共振对数，证明这些结构的消除以及领头和次指征族的干涉。

Result: 共振处双对数和单对数消除产生逆质量平方残差求和规则；共振上方两区域结合为有限四阶系数；EMT形状因子在曲率阶上解析；ERBL扇区不改变斜率但对曲率和高阶导数至关重要；提取了稳定规范曲率和重曲率、第二和第三轻激发的系统包络估计以及纵向迹半径；中间态分解展示了组分质量和激发如何重新分配极点强度并产生可移除极点和干涉零点。

Conclusion: 完整的EMT形状因子在曲率阶上是解析的，ERBL扇区对曲率和高阶导数贡献不可或缺，共振处的对数结构完全消除，提取的曲率和激发估计具有系统性。

Abstract: A diagonal light-front overlap contains near-forward energy-momentum-tensor (EMT) information but is not itself a local matrix element. In the large-$N_c$ 't Hooft model we complete the second GPD moment with its ERBL contribution and follow how the two support regions combine. Boundary exponents generate fractional powers and, at $β=1/2$, resonant logarithms in the separate terms. We show that these structures cancel, including the interference of the leading and next indicial families. At the resonance, cancellation of the double and single logarithms also yields an inverse-mass-squared residue sum rule; above it, the two regions combine into a finite fourth-order coefficient. The complete EMT form factor is therefore analytic through curvature order. The ERBL sector leaves the slope unchanged but is essential for curvature and higher derivatives. We extract stable canonical and heavy curvatures, estimates with systematic envelopes for the second and third light excitations, and the longitudinal trace radius. The intermediate-state decomposition further shows how constituent mass and excitation redistribute pole strength and generate removable poles and interference zeros.

</details>


### [106] [Exact constraints on family-separated seesaw relations and their phenomenological consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16658)
*Jianlong Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在树级水平下严格求解了家族分离跷跷板（family-separated seesaw）ansatz，发现满足约束条件的精确解导致所有标准非共振单圈衰变不对称性在非简并微扰区为零，表明所提出的低能CP破坏与这些衰变不对称性之间的关联并不成立。同时修正了重质量重建公式，并澄清了对无中微子双β衰变、Yukawa标度、参数计数及轻-重质量排序的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Z.-z. Xing提出的家族分离跷跷板ansatz（arXiv:2605.27049v2）在树级、共同重整化标度下的严格解，验证其声称的低能CP破坏与轻子生成衰变不对称性之间的关联是否成立。

Method: 在树级水平、共同重整化标度下，对固定轻-重配对施加约束条件m_i U_{αi}U_{βi}+M_i R_{αi}R_{βi}=0及UU^†+RR^†=1，推导出精确解析解U=VD^{-1/2}和R=iVE diag(√(r_i/(1+r_i)))，进而获得完全幺正补全下的M_R和Y_ν表达式，并系统分析各衰变不对称性及CP不变量的行为。

Result: 精确解导致U^†U和R^†R对角化且不同列严格正交；在M_i>m_i的规范区域，未破缺相衰变描述中的单态质量̂M_i=M_i-m_i>0，区别于破缺相的重本征值M_i；所有标准无风味的和风味的非共振单圈衰变不对称性在非简并微扰区精确为零；当m_i=0时配对Yukawa列及衰变宽度消失；风味求和的重相不变组合也恒为零，尽管单个CP奇不变量可能非零。因此从精确家族分离出发，所提出的低能CP破坏与这些衰变不对称性之间的关联不成立。

Conclusion: 精确家族分离不产生所提出的低能CP破坏与衰变不对称性之间的关联；该结果不涉及辐射错位、共振或相干动力学、热效应及高圈源；修正了重质量重建公式，并澄清了对无中微子双β衰变、Yukawa标度、参数计数和轻-重质量排序的启示。

Abstract: Working at tree level and at a common renormalization scale, we examine the family-separated seesaw ansatz of Z.-z.~Xing, arXiv:2605.27049v2. For a fixed light-heavy pairing, imposing $m_iU_{αi}U_{βi}+M_iR_{αi}R_{βi}=0$ together with $UU^\dagger+RR^\dagger=\mathbf{1}$ yields the general exact solution $U=VD^{-1/2}$ and $R=iVE\,\operatorname{diag}\!\left(\sqrt{r_i/(1+r_i)}\right)$, where $r_i=m_i/M_i$, $D=\operatorname{diag}(1+r_i)$, $V$ is unitary, and $E=\operatorname{diag}(η_i)$ with $η_i=\pm1$. Consequently, $U^\dagger U$ and $R^\dagger R$ are diagonal, and distinct columns are exactly orthogonal. An exact unitary completion gives, in a convenient sterile basis, $M_R=D_N-D_ν$ and $Y_ν=(i/v)VE(D_νD_N)^{1/2}$, so that $Y_ν^\dagger Y_ν=D_νD_N/v^2$ is diagonal. In the canonical domain $M_i>m_i$, the singlet masses relevant to the unbroken-phase decay description are $\widehat{M}_i=M_i-m_i>0$, distinct from the broken-phase heavy eigenvalues $M_i$. Hence, at the scale of exact alignment, all standard unflavored and flavored nonresonant one-loop decay asymmetries vanish in the nondegenerate perturbative regime. For $m_i=0$, the paired Yukawa column and decay width vanish instead. The flavor-summed rephasing-invariant combination used in the proposed asymmetry also cancels identically, although individual CP-odd invariants may remain nonzero. Thus the proposed correlation between low-energy CP violation and these decay asymmetries does not follow from exact family separation. This result does not address radiative misalignment, resonant or coherent dynamics, or thermal and higher-loop sources. We also correct the proposed heavy-mass reconstruction formulas and clarify the implications for neutrinoless double-beta decay, Yukawa scaling, parameter counting, and light-heavy mass orderings.

</details>


### [107] [The semileptonic decays of $\mathcal{B}_{Q_{1}Q_{2}}(\frac{1}{2}^{+})\rightarrow\mathcal{B}_{Q_{1}}^{*}(\frac{3}{2}^{+})$ in QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16697)
*Guo-Liang Yu,Zhi-Gang Wang,Jie Lu,Bin Wu,Peng Yang,Ze Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在QCD求和规则框架下系统分析了双重重子弱衰变过程，计算了形状因子并预测了多种物理可观测量（分支比、前后不对称性、极化等），为未来实验研究提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在QCD求和规则框架下系统分析双重重子从1/2+态到3/2+态的弱跃迁过程，为未来实验研究双重重子的性质提供理论预测。

Method: 采用QCD求和规则，在QCD侧进行算子乘积展开到维度6的真空凝聚项，在唯象侧利用16个不同的狄拉克结构消除低自旋态和负宇称态的干扰。

Result: 预测了部分宽度、Γ_L/Γ_T比值、分支比、轻子的前后不对称性参数A_FB^l、末态重子极化矢量的P_z^F分量以及轻子的纵向极化P_z^l等可观测量。

Conclusion: 这些关于弱衰变的理论预测将有助于未来实验中研究双重重子的性质。

Abstract: In the framework of QCD sum rules, we systematically analyze the weak transition process $\mathcal{B}_{Q_{1}Q_{2}}(\frac{1}{2}^{+})\rightarrow\mathcal{B}_{Q_{1}}^{*}(\frac{3}{2}^{+})$. When doing the operator product expansion in the QCD side, we consider the contributions of perturbative part and vacuum condensate terms up to dimension 6. In the phenomenological side, we eliminate the interferences of the low spin states and negative parity states by employing 16 different dirac structures. As an application, these form factors are finally used to analyze the semileptonic decays of $\mathcal{B}_{Q_{1}Q_{2}}(\frac{1}{2}^{+})\rightarrow\mathcal{B}_{Q_{1}}^{*}(\frac{3}{2}^{+})lν$, where these decays are driven by the transition processes $c\rightarrow d/s+l^{+}+ν_{l}$ and $b\rightarrow u+l^{-}+\overlineν_{l}$. The predicted physical quantities include not only the partial widths, ratios of $Γ_{L}/Γ_{T}$ and the branching fractions, but also some observables such as the forward-backward asymmetry parameter $A_{FB}^{l}$ of lepton, the $P_z^{F}$ component of the polarization vector for daughter baryon and the longitudinal polarization of the lepton $P_z^{l}$. We hope all of these theoretical predictions about the weak decays will be helpful for studying the properties of doubly heavy baryons in experiments in the future.

</details>


### [108] [Could $\barΛ_c$ and $Λ_c$ form bound hadronic molecule with explicit $P-wave$ ?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16756)
*Xiu-Wu Wang,Zhi-Gang Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过十二个插值流系统研究了具有 J^PC=0^± 和 1^± 的显式 P波 Λ̄_cΛ_c 双重子态。结果表明，正负电荷共轭态之间差异不大，含 P波的协变导数中胶子贡献可忽略。P波 Λ̄_cΛ_c 的质量数值远高于 Λ̄_c 和 Λ_c 两个重子构成阈值，因此 Λ̄_c 和 Λ_c 不太可能形成显式 P波的束缚分子。还计算了相关态的极点残差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有显式 P波的 Λ̄_cΛ_c 双重子态，探究其质量特性及是否可能形成束缚分子态，填补该能区双重子态研究的空白。

Method: 采用十二种不同的插值流，通过 QCD 求和规则方法系统研究 J^PC=0^± 和 1^± 的 P波 Λ̄_cΛ_c 双重子态，比较正负电荷共轭态差异，分析协变导数中胶子贡献的影响。

Result: 正负电荷共轭态之间质量差异不大；含 P波的协变导数中胶子贡献可忽略；P波 Λ̄_cΛ_c 的质量远高于 Λ̄_c 和 Λ_c 的双重子阈值，表明它们不太可能形成束缚分子态；计算得到了相关态的极点残差。

Conclusion: Λ̄_c 和 Λ_c 在显式 P波条件下难以形成束缚分子态，为双重子态的实验寻找提供了理论依据和参考。

Abstract: In this article, the explicit $P-wave$ of the $\barΛ_cΛ_c$ dibaryon states with the $J^{PC}=0^{-\pm}$ and $1^{-\pm}$ are studied via twelve interpolated currents. Results show that the positive and negative charge conjugations for the studied states do not have too much difference, the contribution of the gluon from covariant derivative of the current with $P-wave$ could be neglected. The numerical results of the masses of the $P-wave$ $\barΛ_cΛ_c$ are high above the threshold of the two $\barΛ_c$ and $Λ_c$ baryon constituents, $\barΛ_c$ and $Λ_c$ are unlikely to form the bound molecules with explicit $P-wave$ . Pole residues of the related states are also calculated.

</details>


### [109] [The muon collider: expected physics, technological solutions, and the prospect of a 21 km ring at the UNK site](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16904)
*L. V. Dudko*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了多TeV缪子对撞机作为后LHC时代能量前沿最具前景的方案之一，详细总结了其物理潜力（包括希格斯耦合、自耦合、电弱过程、顶夸克、超出标准模型新物理及暗物质热窗口闭合）以及关键技术进展（缪子产生、电离冷却、快速加速、高温超导磁体、机器-探测器接口和中微子通量约束），并探讨了利用俄罗斯普罗特维诺现有21公里UNK隧道建设10-20 TeV缪子对撞机的可行性及其非对撞应用（缪子催化聚变、缪子成像、缪子原子同位素分析、缪子自旋谱学）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后LHC时代需要探索更高能量的对撞方案，缪子对撞机由于缪子是基本粒子且同步辐射功率比电子低约10^9倍，能够以紧凑的环形加速器实现与100 TeV级质子对撞机相当的能量达到，同时保持轻子对撞机的干净末态，因此成为最具吸引力的选项之一。

Method: 综述了缪子对撞机的关键技术路线：缪子产生与电离冷却（MICE实验已证明横向冷却）、快速加速方案、高温超导（HTS）磁体、机器-探测器接口设计以及中微子通量约束。特别分析了在俄罗斯普罗特维诺21公里UNK隧道中建设缪子对撞机的可行性，利用磁刚度关系，常规偶极磁体可实现~10 TeV质心能量，HTS偶极磁体可超过IMCC 10 TeV参考设计。

Result: 缪子对撞机能够覆盖广泛的物理研究：希格斯耦合与自耦合测量、电弱和矢量玻色子融合过程、顶夸克物理、广泛的BSM新物理搜索以及电弱WIMP暗物质热窗口的近乎完全闭合。利用现有UNK隧道可实现10-20 TeV质心能量的缪子对撞机，超过了IMCC 10 TeV参考设计。

Conclusion: 缪子对撞机是后LHC时代极具前景的能量前沿方案，结合了高能量达到和干净末态的优势。利用俄罗斯普罗特维诺现有UNK隧道建设10-20 TeV缪子对撞机在技术上是可行的。此外，该设施开发的高强度缪子束还具有广泛非对撞应用，包括缪子催化聚变、缪子成像、缪子原子同位素分析和缪子自旋谱学。

Abstract: A multi-TeV muon collider has emerged as one of the most compelling options for the post-LHC energy frontier. Because the muon is an elementary particle that radiates $\sim\!10^9$ times less synchrotron power than an electron of the same energy, a circular muon collider delivers the full beam energy to the hard collision in a remarkably compact ring, combining the energy reach of a $100$~TeV-class proton machine with the clean final states of a lepton collider. This review summarizes the expected physics results -- Higgs couplings and self-couplings, electroweak and vector-boson-fusion processes, the top quark, a broad beyond-the-Standard Model programme, and a near-complete closure of the thermal window for electroweak WIMP dark matter -- and the status of the enabling technologies, emphasizing for each challenge how it is being solved and where the solution is documented: muon production, ionization cooling (demonstrated in the transverse plane by MICE), rapid acceleration, high-field HTS magnets, the machine-detector interface, and the neutrino-flux constraint. Building on this foundation, this review examines the prospect of housing a muon collider in the existing 21~km UNK tunnel near Protvino, where the magnetic-rigidity relation maps realistic arc fields onto a centre-of-mass energy of order $10$-$20$ TeV -- reaching the IMCC 10~TeV reference with conventional dipoles and exceeding it with high-temperature-superconductor dipoles. It closes with a discussion of non-collider applications of the intense muon beams such a facility would develop -- muon-catalyzed fusion, muography, muonic-atom isotopic analysis, and muon spin spectroscopy -- several with a long tradition in the Russian physics institutes.

</details>


### [110] [Geometric Scaling and the Odderon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16907)
*Michal Praszalowicz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于弹性pp和p\bar{p}截面的几何标度，推导了交叉偶和交叉奇散射振幅的简单公式，通过COMPETE参数化精确复现了ρ参数，但未能匹配13 TeV的TOTEM和ATLAS数据；通过引入对应Odderon的p\bar{p}总截面微调，可以容纳13 TeV的ρ^{pp}数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于弹性pp和p\bar{p}截面的几何标度，推导交叉偶和交叉奇散射振幅的简单公式，以高精度复现ρ参数，并解释13 TeV下TOTEM和ATLAS数据与理论预测的偏差。

Method: 利用几何标度性，用两个相互作用半径R(s)和Q(s)分别表示交叉偶和交叉奇散射振幅；采用COMPETE参数化总截面，并通过引入对应Odderon的p\bar{p}总截面微调来拟合13 TeV数据。

Result: COMPETE参数化能高精度复现ρ^{pp}和ρ^{p\bar{p}}参数，但无法匹配13 TeV TOTEM和ATLAS数据；通过引入Odderon对应的p\bar{p}总截面微调后，可容纳13 TeV ρ^{pp}数据。

Conclusion: Odderon的引入对于解释高能pp散射中的ρ参数数据是必要的，几何标度方法结合COMPETE参数化在多数能量下有效，但在13 TeV需要非平凡的交叉奇贡献。

Abstract: Based on geometric scaling of elastic $pp$ and possibly $p\bar{p}$ cross-sections, we derive simple formulae for
  the complex crossing-even and crossing-odd scattering amplitudes in terms of two interaction radii: $R(s)$ and $Q(s)$, respectively.
  Employing the COMPETE parametrization of the total cross-sections, we reproduce $ρ^{pp}$ and $ρ^{p\bar{p}}$ parameters with high
  accuracy, however we miss the 13~TeV TOTEM and ATLAS points. We show that a slight modification of the $p\bar{p}$ total cross-section
  corresponding to the Odderon, allows to accommodate $ρ^{pp}$ 13~TeV data.

</details>


### [111] [Imprints of Higgs-portal fermionic dark matter on neutron-star tidal deformability and the mass-radius slope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17034)
*Monmoy Molla,Mehedi Kalam,Tuhin Malik*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用三种密度依赖的相对论平均场泛函，研究了掺有费米子暗物质（通过希格斯玻色子交换相互作用的最轻中性微子）的中子星结构。暗物质会软化状态方程，系统性降低最大质量、1.4倍太阳质量处的半径和潮汐形变率。通过对比核物质不确定性，发现潮汐形变率在1.4倍太阳质量处是区分暗物质存在的强力鉴别器（可达7.5-7.8σ显著性），而质量-半径斜率仅在重中子星分支（1.8-2.0 M⊙）具有诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质掺混对中子星可观测量（质量-半径关系、最大质量、声速剖面、潮汐形变率）的影响，并评估这些暗物质印记能否在核物质状态方程不确定性之上被区分出来。

Method: 使用DDME、DDB、GDFM三种密度依赖的相对论平均场泛函描述β平衡核物质；暗物质建模为通过希格斯玻色子交换相互作用的最轻中性微子，暗物质费米动量k_F^DM作为0.02-0.06 GeV的控制参数；耦合求解平均场方程和Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程；利用贝叶斯推断的核物质状态方程作为参考带，分析潮汐形变率Λ和质量-半径斜率dR/dM的鉴别能力。

Result: 暗物质软化状态方程，系统性降低M_max、R_1.4和Λ_1.4；2 M⊙脉冲星极限和NICER数据对暗物质含量给出模型依赖的上限，GW170817潮汐约束对最硬泛函要求最低暗物质含量；在1.4 M⊙处，k_F^DM=0.06 GeV时Λ的降低幅度达到核物质1σ宽度的约8倍（模型独立7.5-7.8σ）；暗物质轨迹在k_F^DM≳0.03-0.04 GeV时离开核物质1σ带；dR/dM仅在1.8-2.0 M⊙重分支具有诊断价值。

Conclusion: 潮汐形变率Λ是区分暗物质掺混的强力可观测量，在1.4 M⊙处可提供高显著性鉴别；质量-半径斜率仅在高质量端有用；研究为利用多信使天文观测约束中子星中暗物质含量提供了定量框架。

Abstract: We investigate the structure of neutron stars (NSs) admixed with fermionic dark matter (DM) using three density-dependent relativistic mean-field (DDRMF) functionals (DDME, DDB, and GDFM) for $β$-equilibrated nucleonic matter. Modeling DM as the lightest neutralino interacting via Higgs exchange, we treat the DM Fermi momentum $k_F^{\rm DM}$ as a control parameter in the range $0.02$-$0.06$ GeV. We solve the coupled mean-field and Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations to obtain the mass-radius relation, maximum mass $M_{\rm max}$, radial sound speed profile $c_s^2$, and tidal deformability $Λ$. In all models, DM softens the equation of state, systematically reducing $M_{\rm max}$, the radius $R_{1.4}$ (at $1.4M_{\odot}$), and the tidal deformability $Λ_{1.4}$ (at $1.4M_{\odot}$) as $k_F^{\rm DM}$ increases. Consequently, the $2 M_\odot$ pulsar limit and NICER data place a model-dependent upper limit on the DM content, while the GW170817 tidal bound requires a minimal DM content for the stiffest functional. Using a recent Bayesian inference of the DDRMF equation of state as the nucleonic reference band, we evaluate if this DM imprint can be distinguished from nucleonic uncertainties using only measureable quantities. Analyzing the tidal deformability $Λ$ and mass-radius slope $dR/dM$ at fixed mass, we find that $Λ$ is a sharp discriminator: at $1.4 M_\odot$ and $k_F^{\rm DM}=0.06$ GeV, the DM-induced reduction of $Λ$ reaches $\simeq 8$ times the nucleonic $1σ$ width (model-independently $7.5$-$7.8σ$). The DM track leaves the nucleonic $1σ$ band for $k_F^{\rm DM}\gtrsim0.03$-$0.04$ GeV, whereas $dR/dM$ becomes diagnostic only for the heavier ($1.8$-$2.0 M_\odot$) branch.

</details>


### [112] [Neutrino lines and photon continua from cascade dark matter decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17039)
*Jun Guo,Shi-ying Zhao,Bin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究费米子暗物质通过轻中介子衰变为中微子线和光子连续谱的双体衰变过程，发现光子约束在短寿命中介子区域占主导，而中微子线搜索在中介子传播导致光子信号抑制时具有竞争力或主导灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索费米子暗物质通过轻中介子衰变产生中微子线和光子信号的独特衰变拓扑，并比较两类粒子搜索对暗物质寿命的限制能力。

Method: 考虑两种基准模型（轴子类似粒子衰变为双光子、动力学混合暗矢量衰变为三光子），推导来自银河系和延迟河外贡献的光子与中微子信号，并计算当前及未来MeV波段伽马射线和中微子巡天对暗物质寿命的限制。

Result: 短寿命中介子区域光子约束占主导；中介子传播显著抑制光子信号时，中微子线搜索能与之竞争甚至提供更优灵敏度；该结论在保守的河外-only限制和简化的银河空间展宽处理下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 该衰变拓扑中，中微子线和光子搜索互补，具体灵敏度取决于中介子寿命，为暗物质间接探测提供了联合约束策略。

Abstract: We investigate the two-body decay of fermionic dark matter, $χ(\barχ)\to X+ν(\barν)$, where the light mediator $X$ subsequently decays into photons. We consider two benchmark models: an axion-like particle with $a\toγγ$, and a kinetically mixed dark vector with $A'\to3γ$. This decay topology produces a monochromatic neutrino line from the primary decay together with a broad secondary photon continuum. A key feature of the scenario is that the photon signal depends on the mediator decay length, whereas the neutrino line is produced promptly and is insensitive to the subsequent propagation of $X$. We derive dark matter lifetime limits from current and projected MeV gamma-ray and neutrino searches, including both Galactic and delayed extragalactic photon contributions. We find that photon constraints generally dominate for short-lived mediators, while neutrino-line searches can become competitive or provide the leading sensitivity in regions where mediator propagation substantially suppresses the photon signal. This conclusion remains stable under conservative extragalactic-only limits and a simplified treatment of Galactic spatial smearing.

</details>


### [113] [Holographic spectral functions of exotic spin-1 mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17071)
*Yan-Qing Zhao,Zhou-Run Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个全息框架，用于计算奇异自旋-1介子的热谱函数。通过在软墙模型中引入胶子凝聚背景，研究了杂化π₁和四夸克态Z_c通道的解离行为，发现两种通道的解离温度均低于相变温度，且Z_c共振比π_1在更低温度下解离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全息方法难以统一处理具有不同紫外标度奇异性的自旋-1介子通道，特别是杂化态和四夸克态的热谱行为尚缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 构建了包含Proca质量的膜流全息框架，在零空间动量极限下统一描述不同紫外标度的算子；在软墙模型中引入胶子凝聚背景，数值计算π₁和Z_c通道的热谱函数。

Result: (1) 两种通道的解离温度均低于相变温度；(2) 增大胶子凝聚参数c会延迟解离，其中π₁通道同时受真空质量偏移和热谱形变影响，而Z_c通道主要受热谱形变主导；(3) 相同环境下Z_c共振比π₁共振在更低温度下解离。

Conclusion: 该全息框架可有效描述奇异自旋-1介子的热谱行为，揭示了不同夸克组态（杂化vs四夸克）在解离机制上的差异。

Abstract: We develop a general holographic framework for computing thermal spectral functions of exotic spin--1 mesons. A bulk Proca mass encodes the canonical ultraviolet dimension of the interpolating operator, allowing channels with different ultraviolet scaling to be treated within a unified membrane-flow formalism in the zero-spatial-momentum limit. We test this framework in a soft-wall model with a gluon-condensate background for the hybrid $π_1$ and tetraquark-like $Z_c$ channels. The results show that dissociation temperatures of both channels lie below the phase transition temperatures. Increasing $c$ delays dissociation in both channels, but through different effects: both the vacuum-mass shift and thermal spectral deformation contribute in the $π_1$ channel, whereas the latter dominates in the $Z_c$ channel. Nevertheless, for the same environment, the $Z_c$ resonance dissociates at a lower temperature than the $π_1$ resonance.

</details>


### [114] [Detailed study of the dual symmetry of dense quark matter within the effective action formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17190)
*T. G. Khunjua,K. G. Klimenko,R. N. Zhokhov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在存在重子数、同位旋、手征和手征同位旋化学势的情况下，质量less (3+1)维NJL模型和QCD模型的拉格朗日量及路径积分测度均具有对偶对称性，导致手征对称性破缺(CSB)相和带电π子凝聚(PC)相在相图中对偶共轭排列。这一发现表明CSB与带电PC相之间的对偶对称性是质量less致密QCD的基本性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在多种化学势（μ_B, μ_I, μ_5, μ_{I5}）存在的条件下，致密夸克物质相图的对称性结构，揭示手征对称性破缺相与带电π子凝聚相之间的深层对偶关系。

Method: 在质量less (3+1)维NJL模型和QCD模型框架下，分析拉格朗日量的对偶变换不变性，并进一步证明路径积分测度在该变换下也保持不变，从而确立整个热力学势的对偶对称性。

Result: 发现CSB相与带电PC相在(μ_B, μ_I, μ_5, μ_{I5})相图中呈对偶共轭对称排列，且该对偶对称性是质量less致密QCD的普适性质。

Conclusion: CSB与带电PC相之间的对偶对称性是质量less致密QCD的一个基本性质，应在任何QCD相图近似方法中均成立，对研究致密夸克物质的相结构有重要意义。

Abstract: The symmetry properties of the phase diagram of dense quark matter composed of $u$ and $d$ quarks with three colors have been investigated in the framework of massless (3+1)-dimensional Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) and QCD models. It turns out that in the presence of baryon $μ_B$, isospin $μ_I$, chiral $μ_5$ and chiral isospin $μ_{I5}$ chemical potentials, the Lagrangians of these models are invariant under the so-called dual transformation ${\cal D}$. In this paper, it has been shown that the path integration measure of the corresponding partition functions is also invariant under ${\cal D}$. Consequently, the entire NJL model (or QCD) thermodynamic potentials are dually symmetric. In particular, it means that in the total $(μ_B,μ_I,μ_5,μ_{I5})$-phase portraits of these models the chiral symmetry breaking (CSB) and charged pion condensation (PC) phases are arranged dually conjugate (or symmetrically) to each other. Dual symmetry between CSB and charged PC phases of quark matter is a fundamental property of massless dense QCD; it should be observed in the framework of any approximation to the QCD phase diagram, and have important implications for studies of the phase structure.

</details>


### [115] [Scrutinizing lepton flavor universality and transition form factor correlation from charmed meson semileptonic decay into light strange vector $K^*$ meson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17253)
*Sheng-Bo Wu,Dong Huang,Fang-Ping Peng,Hai-Bing Fu,Long-Zeng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD光锥求和规则计算了D_s^+ → K^{*0}衰变的过渡形状因子，构建了K^{*0}介子的光锥分布振幅，并预测了半轻子衰变的分支比、CKM矩阵元及前后不对称性参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算D_s^+ → K^{*0}衰变的过渡形状因子及半轻子衰变可观测量，为检验标准模型和提取CKM矩阵元|V_{cd}|提供理论依据。

Method: 采用QCD光锥求和规则，构建包含通常流的关联函数，并利用光锥谐振子模型构造K^{*0}介子的twist-2横向和纵向光锥分布振幅。

Result: 得到大反冲区TFFs: A₁(0)=0.579, A₂(0)=0.414, V(0)=0.830; 分支比B(D_s^+→K^{*0}e^+ν_e)=2.05×10⁻³, B(D_s^+→K^{*0}μ^+ν_μ)=1.95×10⁻³; 提取|V_{cd}|_(e-Channel)=0.225, |V_{cd}|_(μ-Channel)=0.227。

Conclusion: 计算结果与现有实验数据一致，验证了所构建光锥分布振幅的有效性，为精确研究D_s^+半轻子衰变提供了可靠的理论预测。

Abstract: In this paper, we calculate the transition form factors (TFFs) $A_{1,2}(q^2)$ and $V(q^2)$ of $D_s^+ \to K^{*0}$ decays by using the QCD light-cone sum rule (QCD LCSR) and constructing a correlation function containing the usual current, in which the twist-2 transverse and longitudinal light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) $φ^λ_{2;K^{*0}}(x,μ_0)$ with $λ=(\bot,\|)$ of the $K^{*0}$ meson constitute the main source of theoretical uncertainty. Based on this, we construct these LCDAs using the light-cone harmonic oscillator model. Subsequently, the TFFs obtained in the large recoil region are $A_{1}(0)=0.579_{-0.028}^{+0.024}$, $A_{2}(0)=0.414_{-0.023}^{+0.021}$, and $V(0)=0.830_{-0.020}^{+0.020}$, and the corresponding ratios are obtained to be $r_V=1.433_{-0.090}^{+0.110}$ and $r_2=0.715_{-0.067}^{+0.075}$. Furthermore, we predict the correlation of TFFs and their corresponding ratios. Then, we extrapolate the TFFs over the whole physical $q^2$-region and calculate the decay widths and branching fractions for the semileptonic decays $D_s^+\to K^{*0}\ell^+ν_{\ell}$. The results are $\mathcal{B}(D_s^+\to K^{*0}e^+ν_{e})=(2.05_{-0.16}^{+0.13})\times 10^{-3}$ and $\mathcal{B}(D_s^+\to K^{*0}μ^+ν_μ)=(1.95_{-0.15}^{+0.13})\times 10^{-3}$. Meanwhile, we calculated the branching fraction ratio $\mathcal{R}_{μ/e}^{K^{*0}}$ to be $0.950_{-0.002}^{+0.004}$. In addition, we extract values of the CKM matrix element, obtaining $|V_{cd}|_{(e\text{-Channel})} = 0.225_{-0.005}^{+0.005}$ and $|V_{cd}|_{(μ\text{-Channel})} = 0.227_{-0.004}^{+0.011}$. Finally, we calculated the forward-backward asymmetry parameters for the $D_s^+\to K^{*0} \ell^+ν_{\ell}$ decay.

</details>


### [116] [Breaking the Democratic Limit in the Generalized Friedberg-Lee Model: Implications for Neutrino Masses and Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17258)
*N. Razzaghi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在广义Friedberg-Lee框架下，研究了Point D（α=β=-1/3）处的奇异参数空间。在该极限下，中微子质量矩阵呈现S₃置换对称性决定的民主纹理。通过引入保持扭曲Friedberg-Lee（TFL）对称性的最小系统微扰，该模型有效解除质量简并，打破了magic和μ-τ对称性，实现了现实的反转质量等级（m₃=0）、非零θ₁₃和内禀CP破坏。模型预测最大大气混合角和显著狄拉克CP相位，数值结果与全局拟合数据高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 民主极限（Point D）理论上深刻但被实验排除，因为它预测简并质量谱和消失的反应堆角（θ₁₃=0）。作者旨在调和这一高对称极限与实验观测之间的矛盾，建立一个无需临时参数的理论框架。

Method: 在广义FL框架的Point D奇异点处，基于S₃置换对称性构建民主中微子质量矩阵，然后引入保持TFL对称性的最小系统微扰，以受控方式解除质量简并并打破magic和μ-τ对称性。

Result: 模型实现了反转质量等级（m₃=0）、非零θ₁₃和内禀CP破坏；预测最大大气混合角和显著狄拉克CP相位；中微子质量和Jarlskog不变量的数值范围与全局拟合数据高度一致。

Conclusion: 该研究在TFL方案内为中微子味物理提供了一个理论上自洽且与实验数据吻合良好的基础框架，避免了临时参数，具有坚实的理论动机。

Abstract: We investigate a generalized Friedberg--Lee (FL) framework for neutrino masses, focusing on the singular parameter space at Point D ($α= β= -1/3$). At this limit, the neutrino mass matrix exhibits a democratic texture governed by the $S_3$ permutation symmetry. Although theoretically profound, the exact democratic limit is phenomenologically excluded as it predicts a degenerate mass spectrum and a vanishing reactor angle ($θ_{13}=0$). To reconcile this high-symmetry limit with experimental observations, we introduce a minimal and systematic perturbation that preserves the Twisted Friedberg--Lee (TFL) symmetry. This mechanism effectively lifts the mass degeneracy and breaks the magic and $μ$--$τ$ symmetries in a controlled manner. Our derivation avoids \textit{ad hoc} parameters, establishing a robust framework that yields a realistic inverted mass hierarchy ($m_3=0$), a non-zero $θ_{13}$, and intrinsic CP violation. We demonstrate that the model predicts maximal atmospheric mixing and a significant Dirac CP phase. The obtained numerical ranges for the neutrino masses and the Jarlskog invariant show excellent agreement with global fit data, providing a theoretically motivated foundation for neutrino flavor physics within the TFL scheme.

</details>


### [117] [Heavy meson chiral perturbation theory constraints on a charm-coupled GeV-scale QCD axion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17277)
*Bo-Qiang Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用重介子手征微扰论(HMChPT)约束具有粲夸克-only Peccei-Quinn耦合的QCD轴子，发现该模型不存在轻夸克模型中的同位旋破坏问题，最强约束来自σ介子介导的B_s-反B_s混合，而轴子-π混合打开的可观测稀有衰变率略低于当前实验灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轻夸克轴子模型在χPT中存在同位旋破坏问题，而具有粲夸克-only PQ耦合的QCD轴子可规避该问题。由于m_c > Λ_QCD，需要采用重介子手征微扰论(HMChPT)而非标准χPT来约束这一GeV标度的轴子模型。

Method: 采用HMChPT框架，同时处理CP偶的径向伙伴σ介子(领导阶O(1)耦合)和轴子a(O(1/m_c)耦合)。轴子通过轴子-π混合(θ_aπ ≃ f_π/f_a ~ 0.04)产生效应。分析了D介子质量、D-D*劈裂、粲偶素、D-D散射、B_s混合、稀有D衰变以及K^+→π^+a、B→K a等过程。

Result: HMChPT框架下未发现类似χPT中同位旋问题的致命约束。最强约束来自σ介子介导的B_s-反B_s混合过程。轴子-π混合打开了可观测的稀有衰变通道，但衰变率略低于当前实验灵敏度。

Conclusion: 粲夸克-only PQ耦合的QCD轴子模型在HMChPT框架下是自洽的，避免了轻夸克模型的问题。现有约束主要来自B_s混合而非手征微扰论本身，未来的稀有衰变实验有望检验该模型。

Abstract: The QCD axion with charm-only Peccei-Quinn coupling evades the isospin-violation problem of light-quark models in $χ$PT. Since $m_c>Λ_{\rm QCD}$, we employ heavy meson chiral perturbation theory (HMChPT) to constrain this GeV-scale axion, including its CP-even radial partner $σ$ and the axion $a$. In HMChPT, $σ$ enters at leading order with $O(1)$ coupling, while $a$ couples only at $O(1/m_c)$ and manifests via axion-pion mixing with $θ_{aπ}\simeq f_π/f_a\sim0.04$. We analyze $D$-meson masses, $D$-$D^*$ splitting, charmonium, $D$-$D$ scattering, $B_s$ mixing, rare $D$ decays, and the channels $K^+\toπ^+a$, $B\to K a$. No fatal HMChPT constraint emerges, unlike the $χ$PT isospin issue. The strongest bound is from $σ$-mediated $B_s$-$\bar B_s$ mixing; axion-pion mixing opens testable rare decays, though rates are slightly below current sensitivity.

</details>


### [118] [Kapitza Inspired Effective Interaction for the Exotic State X(3872): A Coupled-Channel Potential Model Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17402)
*M. Monemzadeh,N. Tazimi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文通过引入Kapitza诱导势能构建有效势模型，成功解释了X(3872)强子质量及其分子态与四夸克态混合成分，质量预测与实验高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X(3872)作为最令人困惑的奇特强子之一，其质量极其接近D^0\bar{D}^{*0}阈值，现有理论难以完美解释其内部结构。本文旨在提供一种物理动机明确的机制来解释阈值调谐现象。

Method: 构建包含库仑势、线性禁闭势和Kapitza诱导势（代表快速胶子涨落的平均效应）的有效势模型，通过数值求解薛定谔方程得到基态质量，并开展耦合道分析确定分子态与紧致四夸克态的组分比例。

Result: 基态质量预测为3871.7±0.9 MeV，与实验值高度一致。耦合道分析表明分子态成分占65%-72%，紧致四夸克核心占28%-35%。同时给出了低激发态的质量预测。

Conclusion: Kapitza诱导势能在中等距离区域提供有效吸引力，为阈值调谐提供了物理动机明确的机制，模型成功解释了X(3872)的质量和内部结构。

Abstract: The X(3872) remains one of the most intriguing exotic hadrons, lying extremely close to the $D^0\bar{D}^{*0}$ threshold. We construct an effective potential consisting of Coulomb, linear confinement and a Kapitza-inspired term representing the averaged effect of fast gluonic fluctuations. Numerical solution of the Schrödinger equation shows that this term provides effective attraction in the intermediate-distance region, yielding a ground-state mass of $3871.7 \pm 0.9$ MeV in excellent agreement with experiment. Coupled-channel analysis indicates a dominant molecular component ($\sim 65\%-72\%$) with a significant compact tetraquark core ($\sim 28\%-35\%$). We also present predictions for low-lying excited states. The model offers a physically motivated mechanism for threshold tuning.

</details>


### [119] [Relative enhancement of low-mass vector-boson exchange in higher waves matrix elements: parity non-conservation in hydrogen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17440)
*V. A. Dzuba,V. V. Flambaum,G. K. Vong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究轻Z'玻色子对原子中宇称不守恒（PNC）的影响，发现Z'玻色子贡献与标准模型Z玻色子贡献之比随核电荷数Z的减小而快速增加（快于1/Z²）。特别地，在氢和氘中，Z'对高角动量波函数间（如p-d跃迁）的弱相互作用矩阵元存在巨大额外增强，因为p₃/₂和d₃/₂,₅/₂波函数在原点处为零，抑制了接触型Z玻色子贡献。测量这些矩阵元有助于从标准模型背景中分离出Z'信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大统一模型预言存在额外的Z'玻色子，其会对原子中的宇称不守恒（PNC）产生贡献。为了从标准模型背景中有效区分和探测Z'玻色子的信号，需要找到对其敏感且具有增强效应的可观测量。

Method: 通过理论分析比较轻Z'玻色子与标准模型Z玻色子对原子PNC的贡献比例随核电荷数Z的标度行为，并计算氢和氘原子中不同角动量波函数之间（特别是p-d跃迁）的弱相互作用矩阵元，利用波函数在原点处的行为差异导致的增强效应。

Result: 发现Z'贡献与Z贡献之比随Z减小而快于1/Z²增加；在高角动量波函数（如p₃/₂ → d₃/₂,₅/₂）的矩阵元中，Z'贡献因接触型Z相互作用被抑制而获得巨大额外相对增强。

Conclusion: 测量p-d通道的弱相互作用矩阵元可以有效分离Z'玻色子贡献与标准模型背景，为探测轻Z'玻色子提供新的实验途径。

Abstract: Models of unification predict additional $Z'$ boson, which contributes to parity non-conservation (PNC) in atoms. If $Z'$ boson is light, ratio of $Z'$ boson contribution to the Standard Model $Z$ boson contribution to atomic PNC increases with decreasing nuclear charge $Z$ faster than $1/Z^2$. This motivated our previous study of PNC in hydrogen and deuterium proportional to the weak interaction matrix elements $<s|W|p> $. An enormous additional relative enhancement appears in the matrix elements between higher waves, such as $<p_{1/2,3/2} | W | d_{3/2,5/2}> $, since $p_{3/2}$ and $d_{3/2,5/2}$ wave functions vanish at $r \to 0$, suppressing matrix elements of the contact $Z$ boson mediated contact electron-nucleus interaction. Measurements of $<p_{1/2,3/2} | W | d_{3/2,5/2}> $ will simplify disentanglement of the $Z'$ contribution from the Standard Model background.

</details>


### [120] [Heavy quark mass dependence of the $Λ_Q$ light-cone distribution amplitude in QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17471)
*Yu-Ji Shi,Ji Xu,Shuai Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了QCD中Λ_Q重子领头扭光锥分布振幅（LCDA）的重夸克质量依赖性。从因子化公式出发，推导了峰值区域质量依赖的一阶偏微分方程并解析求解，给出了不同重夸克质量下LCDA之间的显式连接因子。进一步利用重正子模型引入Borel求和微扰修正，通过数值分析评估了这些修正对质量依赖的影响及不确定性，为将晶格QCD计算从较小模拟质量外推到物理底夸克质量提供了关键工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格QCD计算通常在比物理底夸克更小的质量下进行，需要一种可靠的外推方法将结果推到物理底夸克质量。现有方法缺乏对重夸克质量依赖的系统理论描述，因此需要建立LCDA的重夸克质量依赖关系。

Method: 从QCD LCDA与bHQET LCDA的因子化公式出发，在峰值区域推导一阶偏微分方程；解析求解方程得到不同质量间LCDA的显式连接因子；利用重正子模型引入Borel求和微扰修正，并通过简单LCDA模型进行数值分析以评估不确定性和幂次修正。

Result: 成功推导并解析求解了LCDA重夸克质量依赖的一阶偏微分方程，得到了连接不同质量LCDA的显式因子；纳入了Borel求和微扰修正，并通过数值模型展示了修正对质量依赖的影响，给出了相关的幂次修正数值估计。

Conclusion: 本研究为从较小模拟质量外推晶格QCD重子LCDA到物理底夸克质量提供了必要的理论工具，可系统评估外推过程中的不确定性和幂次修正。

Abstract: We study the heavy quark mass dependence of the leading-twist light-cone distribution amplitude (LCDA) of the $Λ_Q$ baryon in QCD. Starting from the factorization formula that relates the QCD LCDA to the boosted heavy-quark effective theory (bHQET) LCDA, we derive a first-order partial differential equation governing this mass dependence in the peak region. The equation is solved analytically, and the explicit factor connecting LCDAs at different heavy quark masses is presented. We further incorporate Borel-resummed perturbative corrections from a renormalon model into the factorization. The impact of these renormalon corrections on the mass dependence is studied, and a numerical analysis using a simple LCDA model is performed to illustrate the behavior and to assess the uncertainties arising from the corrections, thereby providing a numerical estimate of the associated power corrections to the mass dependence. Our results provide an essential tool for extrapolating lattice QCD calculations of heavy-baryon LCDAs from smaller simulated masses to the physical bottom quark mass.

</details>


### [121] [Analysis of the hidden-charm pentaquark candidates in the $J/ψΣ^*$ mass spectrum via the QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17492)
*Zhi-Gang Wang,Yang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD求和规则系统研究了光味10重态中uusc\bar{c}型五夸克态（双夸克-双夸克-反夸克结构）的能谱，给出了量子数IJ^P=1(1/2)^-、1(3/2)^-、1(5/2)^-下的五夸克态质量谱，并建议在Σ_b^+→P_cs^+ φ→J/ψ Σ^*+ φ衰变链中进行实验探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了探索重味五夸克态（特别是含奇异夸克和粲夸克的uusc\bar{c}系统）中光味10重态表示下的新强子态，扩充对五夸克态结构的理解。

Method: 采用QCD求和规则方法，系统列举了最低的五夸克组态，计算了不同量子数下的质量谱。

Result: 获得了uusc\bar{c}五夸克态在量子数1(1/2)^-、1(3/2)^-、1(5/2)^-下的能谱结果。

Conclusion: 建议在Σ_b^+→P_cs^+ φ→J/ψ Σ^*+ φ衰变链中进行实验搜索，以验证理论预言。

Abstract: In this work, we study the diquark-diquark-antiquark type $uusc\bar{c}$ pentaquark states in the light-flavor $\mathbf{10}$ representation via the QCD sum rules in details. We exhaust the lowest five-quark configurations and obtain the spectroscopy of the $uusc\bar{c}$ pentaquark states with the quantum numbers $IJ^{P}=1{\frac{1}{2}}^-$, $1{\frac{3}{2}}^-$, $1{\frac{5}{2}}^-$, and suggest to investigate them in the decay chain $Σ_b^+\to P_{cs}^+\,φ\to J/ψΣ^{*+}\, φ$ experimentally.

</details>


### [122] [Vector mesons leading-twist longitudinal distribution amplitudes and related semi-leptonic decays within QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17496)
*Wan-Bing Luo,Ru-Meng Pan,Ya-Xiong Wang,Tao Zhong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用2021年提出的新QCD求和规则方案，计算了轻矢量介子ρ、K*、φ的领头扭度纵向分布振幅的ξ矩至高阶，并利用最小二乘法拟合得到分布振幅行为，进而重新计算了D→(ρ,K*)、Ds→φ半轻衰变过程的形状因子和分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为获得轻矢量介子（ρ、K*、φ）领头扭度纵向分布振幅的精确行为，以提高相关半轻衰变过程理论预测的准确性。

Method: 采用2021年提出的新QCD求和规则方案；改进求和规则公式计算ξ矩至高阶；利用最小二乘法拟合ξ矩得到分布振幅行为；重新计算半轻衰变形状因子和分支比。

Result: 给出了ρ、K*、φ的各阶ξ矩和Gegenbauer矩的具体数值结果（如⟨ξ²⟩∥_{2;ρ}=0.225等），以及相应的分布振幅行为。

Conclusion: 通过新方法获得了更精确的轻矢量介子领头扭度纵向分布振幅，并改进了相关D介子半轻衰变的理论预测。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on the light vector meson leading-twist longitudinal distribution amplitudes (DAs) $φ^\parallel_{2;V}(x,μ)$ with $V = ρ, K^\ast, φ$. In order to obtain their accurate behaviors, a new scheme of QCD sum rule research with respect to DA suggested in 2021 by us is adopted. With an improved sum rule formula, the $ξ$-moments $\langleξ^n\rangle_{2;V}^\parallel$ up to tenth order are calculated. In which, $\langleξ^2\rangle^\parallel_{2;ρ}=0.225^{+0.013}_{-0.012}$, $\langleξ^1\rangle^\parallel_{2;K^\ast}=-0.0228^{+0.0042}_{-0.0040}$, $\langleξ^2\rangle^\parallel_{2;K^\ast}=0.217^{+0.007}_{-0.007}$, $\langleξ^2\rangle^\parallel_{2;φ}=0.209^{+0.020}_{-0.020}$, and the corresponding Gegenbauer moments $a^{2;\parallel}_{2;ρ}=0.074^{+0.039}_{-0.036}$, $a^{1;\parallel}_{2;K^\ast}=-0.038^{+0.007}_{-0.007}$, $a^{2;\parallel}_{2;K^\ast}=0.050^{+0.020}_{-0.019}$, $a^{2;\parallel}_{2;φ}=0.027^{+0.058}_{-0.058}$ at the scale $μ= 1~{\rm GeV}$, respectively. By fitting those $\langleξ^n\rangle^\parallel_{2;V}(n = 1,2,\cdots,10)$ with the least squares method, the behaviors of leading-twist longitudinal DAs for $ρ, K^\ast, φ$ are determined. Further, we recalculate the transition form factors and branching ratio of the $D\to(ρ,K^\ast)$, $D_s\toφ$ semi-leptonic decay processes.

</details>


### [123] [A Gauge Model for Quasi-Dirac Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17825)
*Zhao-Xing Fan,Chun Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于 $L_\mu - L_\tau$ 规范对称性的模型，通过引入反常自由的手征费米子作为右手中微子来源，利用高维算符产生微小狄拉克质量，破缺后自然形成准狄拉克中微子图像，并讨论了相关现象学与宇宙学 implications。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为右手中微子提供自然起源，同时解释中微子微小质量及 Majorana 质量为零的起源。

Method: 引入 $L_\mu - L_\tau$ 阿贝尔规范对称性及反常自由的手征费米子，通过高维算符产生狄拉克质量项，规范对称性破缺后实现准狄拉克中微子机制。

Result: 中微子获得微小非零狄拉克质量，准狄拉克中微子图像自然产生，能够复现 realistic 中微子物理。

Conclusion: 该模型在现象学和宇宙学上具有可行性，为中微子质量和混合提供了一种新的理论框架。

Abstract: In a model with $L_μ - L_τ$ Abelian gauge symmetry, anomaly-free chiral fermions, which are Standard Model singlets, are introduced as the origin of right-handed neutrinos. This $\mathsf{U} (1)$ symmetry keeps the right-handed neutrinos Majorana massless. Tiny nonvanishing Dirac masses of neutrinos are due to higher-dimensional operators with natural coupling constants. After gauge symmetry breaking, a quasi-Dirac neutrino scenario naturally appears, and realistic neutrino physics can be produced. Phenomenological and cosmological aspects of the model are discussed.

</details>


### [124] [Quantum numbers of excited $Ξ_c^\prime$ and $Ω_c$ baryons and the $P$-wave $Σ_c$ spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17923)
*Xuan Luo,Shu-Wei Zhang,Hua-Xing Chen,Hui-Min Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD求和规则和光锥求和规则在重夸克有效理论框架下，计算了P波粲重子的质量和强衰变性质。研究发现每个味道扇区虽允许7个态，但仅4个Σ_c、4个Ξ_c′和5个Ω_c态在实验上可分辨。通过分析实验观测到的Ξ_c和Ω_c激发态质量劈裂模式，依次指定了J^P=1/2^-, 3/2^-, 3/2^-, 5/2^-的量子数，并初步将Ω_c(3119)解释为J^P=3/2^-的ρ模激发。同时预测了4个可分辨P波Σ_c态的质置、宽度和主导衰变模式，这些态可能重叠在观测到的Σ_c(2800)和Σ_c(2900)结构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上对激发重重子的精确测量与系统理论研究的结合，使得解析其谱的精细结构成为可能。本文旨在利用QCD求和规则解决P波粲重子的精细结构问题，特别是对实验观测到的多个Ξ_c和Ω_c激发态进行量子数赋值，并预测尚未完全分辨的Σ_c激发态谱。

Method: 采用QCD求和规则和光锥求和规则，在重夸克有效理论（HQET）框架下，计算P波粲重子的质量和强衰变性质。

Result: 每个味道扇区允许7个P波态，但仅4个Σ_c、4个Ξ_c′和5个Ω_c态在实验上可分辨。Ξ_c(2882)、Ξ_c(2923)、Ξ_c(2939)、Ξ_c(2965)的量子数依次为1/2^-, 3/2^-, 3/2^-, 5/2^-；Ω_c(3000)、Ω_c(3050)、Ω_c(3066)、Ω_c(3090)具有相同的量子数赋值；Ω_c(3119)被初步解释为J^P=3/2^-的ρ模激发。同时预测了4个可分辨P波Σ_c态的质量、宽度和主导衰变模式，它们可能重叠在Σ_c(2800)和Σ_c(2900)结构内。

Conclusion: 窄的Ξ_c和Ω_c态的精确谱学为解析P波Σ_c谱提供了有效途径。本文的理论结果与实验观测一致，成功为多个粲重子激发态分配了量子数，并给出了对尚未充分分辨的Σ_c激发态的预言。

Abstract: Recent precision measurements of excited heavy baryons, combined with systematic theoretical studies, make it possible to resolve the fine structure of their spectra. We calculate the masses and strong-decay properties of the $P$-wave charmed baryons using QCD sum rules and light-cone sum rules within heavy quark effective theory (HQET). Although seven states are allowed in each flavor sector, we find that only four $Σ_c$, four $Ξ_c^\prime$, and five $Ω_c$ states are expected to be experimentally resolvable. The similar mass-splitting patterns of $Ξ_c(2882)$, $Ξ_c(2923)$, $Ξ_c(2939)$, and $Ξ_c(2965)$ and of $Ω_c(3000)$, $Ω_c(3050)$, $Ω_c(3066)$, and $Ω_c(3090)$, together with our theoretical results, lead to the successive quantum-number assignments $J^P=1/2^-,3/2^-,3/2^-$, and $5/2^-$. We tentatively interpret $Ω_c(3119)$ as a predominantly $ρ$-mode excitation with $J^P=3/2^-$. We also predict the masses, widths, and dominant decay modes of four resolvable $P$-wave $Σ_c$ states, which may overlap within the observed $Σ_c(2800)$ and $Σ_c(2900)$ structures. Precision spectroscopy of the narrow $Ξ_c$ and $Ω_c$ states thus provides a route to resolving the $P$-wave $Σ_c$ spectrum.

</details>


### [125] [Benchmarking Machine Learning Architectures for ttH Multilepton Signal Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18022)
*Lukáš Viceník,André Sopczak,Oleksandr Shekhovtsov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种机器学习模型（从XGBoost到LorentzNet）在$t\bar t H$多轻子信号-本底分类任务上的表现，采用标准化训练流程和分层特征集，发现Particle Transformer和LorentzNet性能最优，并验证了ROC AUC与基于信号强度不确定性的模型排序在合理加权训练下的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在特征定义、训练流程和评估指标上存在差异，难以分离模型架构对性能的独立影响，因此需要标准化比较框架。

Method: 采用标准化训练与超参数优化流程，构建分层特征集，对XGBoost到LorentzNet等多种模型进行综合比较；使用信号强度不确定性和ROC AUC指标评估，并分析不同特征集、训练集大小和训练策略的影响。

Result: Particle Transformer和LorentzNet在所考虑的设定下性能最优；在合理加权训练下，ROC AUC与基于不确定性的模型排序具有良好相关性。

Conclusion: 该研究为高能物理中信号发现与信号强度估计的机器学习模型选择提供了系统基准，结论有望推广到LHC和HL-LHC的更广泛搜索与测量场景。

Abstract: Statistical testing for signal discovery and signal-strength estimation in high-energy physics increasingly relies on machine-learning models trained on simulated data. We present a synthetic dataset for $t\bar t H$ multilepton signal--background classification and perform a systematic evaluation of machine-learning models ranging from the widely used XGBoost for tabular data to LorentzNet, which processes events with built-in Lorentz symmetry. Existing studies often differ in feature definitions, training procedures, and evaluation metrics, making it difficult to isolate the impact of model architecture on performance. To address this, we apply standardized training and hyperparameter-optimization procedures, construct a controlled hierarchy of feature sets, and perform a comprehensive comparison of the models. In addition to the analysis-driven metric of signal-strength uncertainty, we report the commonly used ROC AUC metric and show that under realistically weighted training, it correlates well with the uncertainty-based ranking of models. We further investigate model performance as a function of input feature set, training-set size, and the choice between channel-specific and unified multi-channel training. Our results establish the superiority of Particle Transformer and LorentzNet within the considered setup. We also identify potential avenues for further improvement, including more expressive architectures, joint training across additional analysis channels, and larger simulated datasets. Although this study focuses on a specific Higgs-boson analysis, we expect the main conclusions to generalize to a broader class of searches and measurements at the LHC and HL-LHC.

</details>


### [126] [The $Υ(nS) \to B_{(c)}$ transition form factors and their applications to semileptonic and nonleptonic weak decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18023)
*You-Ya Yang,Zhi-Qing Zhang,Zhi-Jie Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在协变光前夸克模型下系统研究了Υ(nS) (n=1,2,3,4) 的遍举半轻子衰变和非轻子衰变，计算了分支比、前后不对称性和纵向极化分数，发现Υ(3S)→B_cℓν_ℓ的分支比最大可达10^{-9}，Υ(3S)→B_cρ和Υ(3S)→B_cD_s^{(*)}的分支比可达10^{-10}，但实验探测极具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协变光前夸克模型框架下系统研究Υ(nS) (n=1,2,3,4) 向B(c)介子的弱衰变过程，填补相关理论空白，为未来实验提供理论参考。

Method: 利用协变光前夸克模型计算Υ(nS)→B(c)的形状因子，进而计算半轻子衰变Υ(nS)→B(c)ℓν_ℓ和非轻子衰变Υ(nS)→B(c)M的分支比，其中ℓ=e,μ,τ，M为π(ρ)、K^{(*)}、D^{(*)}、D_s^{(*)}。同时计算半轻子衰变的前后不对称性A_FB和纵向极化分数f_L。

Result: 半轻子衰变中，Υ(3S)→B_cℓν_ℓ分支比最大，可达10^{-9}量级；非轻子衰变中，Υ(3S)→B_cρ和Υ(3S)→B_cD_s^{(*)}分支比最大，达10^{-10}量级。

Conclusion: 尽管给出了这些弱衰变模式的分支比等可观测量，但考虑到末态粒子的识别和探测效率，未来实验寻找这些衰变模式仍面临较大挑战。

Abstract: The semileptonic and nonleptonic decays of the $Υ(nS)$ with $n=1,2,3,4$ are investigated within the covariant light-front quark model (CLFQM). Using the form factors of the transitions $Υ(nS) \to B_{(c)}$ obtained from the CLFQM, we calculate the branching ratios of the decays $Υ(nS)\to B_{(c)}\ellν_\ell$ and $Υ(nS)\to B_{(c)}M$ with $\ell=e,μ,τ$ and $M$ referring to $π(ρ),K^{(*)},D^{(*)},D^{(*)}_s$. One can find that the branching ratios of the decays $Υ(3S)\to B_c\ellν_\ell$ are the largest among those of considered semileptonic decays and can amount to $10^{-9}$; As to the nonleptonic decays, $Υ(3S)\to B_cρ$ and $Υ(3S)\to B_cD^{(*)}_s$ have the largest branching ratios, which reach up to $10^{-10}$. Given the identification and detection efficiency of final states, searching for these weak decay modes should be fairly challenging in future experiments. The forward-backward asymmetry $A_{FB}$ and the longitudinal polarization fraction $f_L$ are also calculated for those semileptonic decays.

</details>


### [127] [Wide binaries in ultra-faint dwarf galaxies as a probe of extended dark objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18185)
*María Olalla Olea-Romacho*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用宽距双星系统的动力学扰动来约束暗物质亚结构，将点质量模型推广到球形延展暗物质天体，并应用于超暗矮星系牧夫座I的宽距双星候选体。研究推导了95%置信度下暗物质占比随扰动量质量、90%质量半径和密度轮廓（NFW、r^-3/2、r^-9/4）的约束，发现足够致密的天体趋近于点质量极限，而有限尺寸效应在扰动量接近双星尺度时抑制双星瓦解。该约束在质量-半径平面的广泛区域仍对暗物质占比敏感，并能触及常规微引力透镜搜索无法探测的大尺度天体。作为应用，将r^-3/2结果映射到超致密迷你晕，得到了对原初曲率功率谱的约束。超暗矮星系宽距双星因此提供了一个纯粹引力探针对延展暗物质天体的丰度和小尺度原初曲率功率谱进行约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽距双星系统对暗物质亚结构的动力学扰动非常敏感，但现有约束主要针对点质量暗物质天体。本文旨在将理论推广到球形延展暗物质天体，并利用超暗矮星系中的宽距双星来约束延展暗物质天体的丰度以及小尺度原初曲率功率谱。

Method: 将点质量双星瓦解约束推广到球形延展暗物质天体，考虑NFW、r^-3/2和r^-9/4三种密度轮廓，推导扰动质量、90%质量半径与暗物质占比之间的约束关系，并应用于牧夫座I的宽距双星候选体数据，获得95%置信度限制。

Result: 足够致密的延展暗物质天体趋近于点质量极限，而当扰动量尺度与双星尺度相当时，有限尺寸效应会抑制双星瓦解。约束在质量-半径平面广泛区域对暗物质占比敏感，且能探测到比常规微引力透镜搜索更大的天体。将r^-3/2结果映射到超致密迷你晕，获得了对原初曲率功率谱的约束。

Conclusion: 超暗矮星系宽距双星提供了一个纯粹的引力探测手段，可用于约束延展暗物质天体的丰度以及小尺度原初曲率功率谱。

Abstract: Wide stellar binaries are sensitive dynamical probes of dark matter substructure. We generalize existing point-mass disruption constraints to spherical extended dark objects and apply the resulting framework to the population of wide-binary candidates identified in the ultra-faint dwarf galaxy Bootes I. We derive 95% confidence limits on the dark matter fraction as a function of the perturber mass, the radius enclosing 90% of the mass, and the density profile, considering Navarro-Frenk-White, r^-3/2, and r^-9/4 models. Sufficiently compact objects converge to the point-mass limit, whereas finite size suppresses binary disruption when the perturber becomes comparable to the relevant encounter and binary scales. The constraints remain sensitive to dark matter fractions well below unity over a broad region of the mass-radius plane and extend to objects substantially larger than those accessible to conventional microlensing searches. As an application, we map the r^-3/2 results onto ultracompact minihalos and derive an illustrative constraint on the primordial curvature power spectrum. Ultra-faint-dwarf wide binaries therefore offer a purely gravitational probe of the abundance of extended dark matter objects and of the small-scale primordial curvature power spectrum.

</details>


### [128] [Correlated comagnetometry for precision measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18221)
*Yossi Rosenzweig,Yevgeny Kats,Eli Sarid,Menachem Givon,Yonathan Japha,Ron Folman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于同腔内两种碱金属原子相关测量的新方法，通过种间相位差实现高频磁背景自消除，以暗物质信号为例实现了三十倍背景抑制和信噪比数量级提升，并具备模型区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共磁力计仅在低频下通过自抵消消除磁噪声，高频磁背景限制了灵敏度。本文旨在扩展磁背景抑制到高频范围，同时实现免校准和高信噪比的精密测量。

Method: 在同一气室中对两种碱金属物种进行相关测量，利用种间光-物质相互作用响应函数的相位差，实现对磁背景的免校准消除，并对共模强度噪声不敏感。

Result: 以暗物质信号为测试案例，实现了三十倍的背景抑制，信噪比提升一个数量级或更多；同时提供了模型区分能力（对不同亚原子粒子的耦合差异）。

Conclusion: 该方法为精密测量（特别是新奇场探测）开辟了新路径，兼具更高灵敏度和模型区分能力。

Abstract: Magnetometers are among the most widely used probes in science and technology. Comagnetometers increase sensitivity by self-cancellation of magnetic noise, but only at low frequencies. We suggest a correlated measurement of two alkali species in one cell to cancel the magnetic background also at high frequencies. The inter-species phase difference of the light-matter interaction response function is found to be calibration free and insensitive to common-mode intensity noise. Utilizing a dark-matter signal as a testcase, the method achieves a thirtyfold background suppression, raising the signal-to-noise ratio by an order of magnitude or more, depending on the coupling to the different subatomic particles. We show that the method also provides model differentiation. The higher sensitivity and model differentiation open a path to novel probes for precision measurements in general and exotic fields in particular.

</details>


### [129] [HyperIso: A general BSM calculator for flavour observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18222)
*T. Reymermier,N. Fardeau,F. Mahmoudi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍HyperIso，一个基于SuperIso物理遗产但完全重写的模块化C++软件包，用于评估SM和BSM场景中的味物理可观测量。它通过MARTY框架自动计算BSM Wilson系数，提供C++/Python/命令行/GUI接口，支持B、K、D介子衰变及muon g-2等关键观测量，输入支持JSON/YAML和Les Houches格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有味物理工具（如SuperIso）在支持用户自定义BSM模型方面存在局限，需要一个模块化、可扩展且具有多种接口的新工具来灵活评估标准模型和超出标准模型场景下的味物理可观测量。

Method: 基于SuperIso物理遗产，采用现代C++核心模块化重构；集成MARTY框架自动计算BSM Wilson系数（leading order）；提供C++、Python、命令行和图形用户界面；输入支持JSON/YAML配置文件及Les Houches Accord文件。

Result: 实现了对辐射、轻子和半轻子B衰变、K和D介子衰变的分支比和角观测量，以及muon反常磁矩(g-2)的评估；支持SM、一般双希格斯二重态模型(THDM)和超对称模型等内置场景，并通过MARTY支持用户自定义BSM模型。

Conclusion: HyperIso为味物理研究、现象学扫描和BSM场景验证提供了一个灵活且可扩展的新工具，将可观测量计算、不确定性传播和统计解释统一在共同后端。

Abstract: We present HyperIso, a new standalone program for the evaluation of flavour physics observables across a wide variety of Standard Model (SM) and Beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM) scenarios. The code builds on the physics heritage of SuperIso, but is implemented as a new modular software package with a modern C++ core and multiple user interfaces. In addition to native implementations for established scenarios such as the SM, the general Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (THDM), and supersymmetric models, HyperIso interfaces with the MARTY framework to compute automatically BSM Wilson coefficients at leading order. This design makes it possible to study user-defined BSM models while keeping the observable calculation, uncertainty propagation and statistical interpretation in a common backend. The software provides C++, Python, command-line and graphical interfaces. It supports the evaluation of key observables such as the branching ratios and, where available, angular observables, for radiative, leptonic, and semileptonic B decays, as well as for the relevant kaon and D-meson decays, together with the muon anomalous magnetic moment (g-2). The inputs can be provided through JSON/YAML configuration files and Les Houches Accord files, either supplied by the user or generated by external spectrum calculators. HyperIso therefore provides a flexible and extensible tool for flavour studies, phenomenological scans and the validation of BSM scenarios.

</details>


### [130] [Theoretical uncertainties in reconstructing model parameters with gravitational waves from supercooled phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18233)
*Maciej Kierkla,Marek Lewicki,Philipp Schicho,Daniel Schmitt,Bogumila Swiezewska*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了经典尺度不变标准模型扩展中，两种重求和方案（高温有效场论与戴西重求和技术）对引力波信号重建的影响，发现戴西重求和的系统误差远大于探测器的统计误差，导致其被强烈排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来干涉仪可能探测到宇宙一阶相变产生的引力波信号，但从中重建粒子物理模型需要精确的理论控制。经典尺度不变扩展模型通常预测强过冷相变和强引力波信号，而核化率对量子和热修正的处理非常敏感，因此需要比较不同重求和方案的有效性。

Method: 以经典共形 U(1)_X 模型为代表，扫描其参数空间，比较两种重求和方案：(1) 高温有效场论（两圈匹配，含次领头阶反弹作用修正，核化率因子由一圈泛函行列式给出）；(2) 常用戴西重求和技术（有效势，核化率因子由量纲分析估算）。通过 Fisher 矩阵分析 LISA 探测器对注入引力波信号的重建效果。

Result: 戴西重求和技术因系统误差远大于重建不确定度而被强烈排除。

Conclusion: 在经典尺度不变扩展模型中，必须采用更精确的高温有效场论方法，否则理论误差将主导重建，导致模型参数估计不可靠。

Abstract: Future interferometers may detect a gravitational-wave (GW) signal from a cosmological first-order phase transition. Reconstructing the underlying particle-physics model from such a signal requires theoretical control over the map from microphysics to the spectrum. For classically scale-invariant extensions of the Standard Model, which generically predict strongly supercooled transitions and strong GW signals, this map depends sensitively on the treatment of quantum and thermal corrections to the nucleation rate. Taking the classically conformal ${\rm U}(1)_X$ model as representative of this class, we scan its parameter space and compare two resummation schemes. The first is a high-temperature effective field theory, matched at two-loop level and including next-to-leading-order corrections to the bounce action, with the nucleation-rate prefactor given by the full one-loop functional determinants. The second is a commonly employed daisy-resummed effective potential, with the prefactor estimated on dimensional grounds. Reconstructing the fundamental model parameters through a Fisher-matrix analysis of injected GW signals at LISA, we find that the daisy-resummation scheme is strongly disfavored, as its theoretical error dominates over the reconstruction uncertainty.

</details>

### [131] [A Centrality Measure Using Magnitude Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16377)
*Nadja Häusermann,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文基于幅度同调理论，提出了一种新的图节点中心性度量方法，该方法通过移除顶点时幅度同调的变化来衡量节点重要性，并验证了其满足多种合理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将度量空间的幅度这一表达性不变量（蕴含多种几何-拓扑不变量）推广到图分析中，利用幅度同调的最新进展，开发一种新的局部节点中心性度量方法，以提供与传统中心性度量不同的视角。

Method: 借鉴相对同调的概念，通过移除图中某个顶点后幅度同调的变化来衡量该节点的中心性或重要性。

Result: 提出的中心性度量满足多项合理性质，并且与多种已有中心性度量相比，提供了新的视角。

Conclusion: 基于幅度同调的中心性度量是一种有效的图节点重要性评估方法，能够捕捉与传统方法不同的结构信息。

Abstract: The magnitude of a metric space constitutes an expressive invariant that subsumes numerous different geometrical-topological invariants. Building on recent advances in magnitude homology, i.e., a bigraded homology theory that recovers the magnitude, we develop a novel local measure of the centrality or importance of nodes in a graph. Our measure is inspired by the concept of relative homology as it considers the change in magnitude homology when removing a vertex. We show that our proposed measure satisfies several properties a centrality measure is reasonably expected to respect and demonstrate that we introduce a new perspective on centrality by comparing to several established centrality measures.

</details>


### [132] [On the wreath transfer in the homology of symmetric groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17114)
*Dezhou Li*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文给出了奇素数 p 下 wreath transfer 的显式公式，并通过双陪集公式和生成函数方法进行了代数证明，进而为 Goodwillie 微积分中的混合 Adem 关系提供了初等证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 wreath transfer 建立显式计算公式，并由此为 Goodwillie 微积分中的混合 Adem 关系提供初等证明。

Method: 直接代数计算，利用 Cartan 经典双陪集公式结合生成函数方法。

Result: 得到了 wreath transfer 在奇素数 p 下的显式公式。

Conclusion: 作为直接推论，给出了 Goodwillie 微积分中混合 Adem 关系有效性的初等证明。

Abstract: For an odd prime $p$, we give an explicit formula for the wreath transfer $\mathrm{tr} \colon H_*(Σ_{p^2};\mathbb{F}_p)\longrightarrow H_*(Σ_p\wrΣ_p;\mathbb{F}_p)$ associated with the standard inclusion $Σ_p\wrΣ_p\leΣ_{p^2}$. The proof is a direct algebraic computation, using Cartan's classical double-coset formula together with a generating-function argument. As an immediate consequence, we obtain an elementary proof of the validity of the mixed Adem relations in Goodwillie calculus.

</details>


### [133] [Classification of Finite Subquandles in Spherical Quandles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17137)
*Kentaro Yonemura,Naoki Nakamura*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 球面quandle的所有有限子quandle都可以由二面体quandle和定义在根系上的quandle完全描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究球面quandle的有限子结构，揭示其与二面体quandle和根系quandle之间的内在联系。

Method: 通过代数与几何方法分析球面quandle的有限子quandle的结构特征，并与已知的二面体quandle和根系quandle进行对比分类。

Result: 证明了球面quandle的每一个有限子quandle都可由二面体quandle或根系定义的quandle来表示。

Conclusion: 二面体quandle和根系quandle是理解球面quandle有限子结构的基本构件。

Abstract: Every finite subquandle of a spherical quandle is accounted for by dihedral quandles and by quandles defined on root systems.

</details>


### [134] [The Euler Characteristic of Finite Subset Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17830)
*Walid Taamallah*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文计算了拓扑空间X的有限子集空间Sub_n X的欧拉示性数，对于具有有限CW复形伦型的空间X，给出了显式公式：χ(Sub_n X) = Σ_{i=1}^n C(χ(X), i)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限子集空间的拓扑不变量，特别是欧拉示性数，这是代数拓扑中的一个基本问题。

Method: 通过代数拓扑方法，利用欧拉示性数的加性和乘性性质，对具有有限CW复形伦型的空间进行分析。

Result: 得到了显式公式：χ(Sub_n X) = Σ_{i=1}^n C(χ(X), i)，其中C表示二项式系数。

Conclusion: 该公式给出了有限子集空间欧拉示性数的简明计算方式，将原空间的欧拉示性数与子集空间的欧拉示性数通过组合恒等式联系起来。

Abstract: For a topological space $X$, the space of finite subsets $\mathrm{Sub}_n X$ consists of non-empty subsets of $X$ of cardinality at most $n$. We compute the Euler characteristic of $\mathrm{Sub}_n X$ for any space $X$ having the homotopy type of a finite CW complex. We obtain the explicit formula $χ(\mathrm{Sub}_n X) = \sum_{i=1}^n \binom{χ(X)}{i}$.

</details>


### [135] [Periodic phenomena in stable motivic homotopy theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18165)
*Jackson Morris*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本综述研究了稳定同伦论工具在 motivic 同伦论中的应用，重点讨论了 motivic Adams 谱序列、球面 motivic 稳定同伦群的周期性以及合成谱，并提出了未来研究的若干问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理稳定同伦论工具如何影响并推动 motivic 同伦论的发展，为研究者提供该交叉领域的全面概览，并指出尚未解决的关键问题以引导后续研究。

Method: 采用综述方法，系统回顾和综合分析稳定同伦论中的工具（如 Adams 谱序列、周期性方法、合成谱理论）在 motivic 同伦论中的具体应用与演变。

Result: 总结了多种 motivic Adams 谱序列的构造与性质，揭示了球面 motivic 稳定同伦群中的周期性现象，并阐述了合成谱在 motivic 框架下的应用。

Conclusion: 稳定同伦论工具在 motivic 同伦论中产生了深远影响，但仍存在许多开放问题有待进一步探索。

Abstract: In this survey, we study how tools from stable homotopy theory have manifested and impacted motivic homotopy theory. In particular, we discuss various motivic Adams spectral sequences, periodicity in the motivic stable homotopy groups of spheres, and synthetic spectra. We conclude with many problems for future investigation.

</details>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Universal quantum dimensions: $γ$-dependent factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16359)
*M. Y. Avetisyan,R. L. Mkrtchyan*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文发展了计算Vogel意义下通用量子维度的方法，特别是针对γ依赖因子的贡献，首次得到了E通用多重态的通用量子维度，并提出了一种可推导高维表示通用公式的显式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作仅能计算经典代数通用多重态中非零关联成员的γ无关部分，本文旨在将此方法扩展到γ依赖因子，从而获得完整通用量子维度，特别是此前未解决的E通用多重态。

Method: 基于Vogel的通用量子维度框架，利用经典代数与E₈成员已知的量子维度，通过通用分解关系（伴随表示的第四次幂）推导γ依赖因子；同时将sl与so、sp成员之间基于杨图垂直与水平求和的关系部分推广到例外情形，并设计了显式算法。

Result: 首次获得了E通用多重态的通用量子维度；对于其余例外代数，其量子维度自动恢复，验证了方法自洽性；部分实现了杨图求和关系向例外情形的推广；给出了可推导高维表示通用公式的算法。

Conclusion: 通用量子维度为Chern-Simons观测量（如纽结不变量、配分函数）提供了一种统一描述，适用于所有规范代数。

Abstract: We develop an approach for computing the universal, in Vogel's sense, quantum dimensions. In a previous work a method for calculating the $γ$-independent part of these dimensions for universal multiplets with nonzero associated members for classical algebras was proposed. In the present paper, this approach is extended to the $γ$-dependent factors. In particular, we derive these contributions for the $E$ universal multiplet, which appears in the universal decomposition of the fourth power of the adjoint representation, thereby obtaining its universal quantum dimension for the first time.
  The analysis requires knowledge of the quantum dimensions of the classical and $E_8$ members of the multiplet; for the remaining exceptional algebras, the corresponding quantum dimensions are recovered automatically, providing an additional consistency check of the approach. We also partially extend a previously conjectured relation between the $sl$ and $so, sp$ members - formulated in terms of vertical and horizontal sums of Young diagrams - to the exceptional case. Finally, we present an explicit algorithm implementing the proposed method, which enables the derivation of universal formulae for higher-dimensional representations. The universal quantum dimensions provide, in some cases, a unified description of Chern-Simons observables, including knot invariants and partition functions, in a form valid simultaneously for all gauge algebras.

</details>


### [137] [Characteristic classes on a noncommutative background: a new approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16480)
*Dimitry Gurevich,Vladimir Roubtsov,Pavel Saponov*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文引入了U(gl(N))及其q-变形（修正反射方程代数）的偏导数类似物，构建了相应的微分代数、量子de Rham算子和Leibniz法则，并利用Cayley-Hamilton恒等式构造了射影模、Grassmannian联络及Chern类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典微分几何与表示论中的概念（如偏导数、微分形式、de Rham算子）推广到量子群包络代数U(gl(N))及其q-变形（修正反射方程代数）的框架下，并建立相应的微分结构。

Method: 引入了关于U(gl(N))生成元及修正反射方程代数生成元的偏导数类似物；利用量子偏导数构建微分代数与量子de Rham算子；通过量子对偶结构导出Leibniz法则；利用Cayley-Hamilton恒等式构造射影模，并引入Grassmannian联络与Chern类。

Result: 成功定义了量子偏导数、微分代数与de Rham算子；导出了一种与经典Leibniz法则不同的新法则；为U(gl(N))给出了另一种更适合推广的Leibniz法则形式；利用Cayley-Hamilton恒等式构造了射影模及相应的Grassmannian联络与Chern类。

Conclusion: 本文在量子群包络代数及其q-变形上系统建立了微分演算框架，揭示了量子Leibniz法则与经典情形的差异，并通过Cayley-Hamilton恒等式实现了射影模与特征类的代数构造，为量子微分几何与表示论提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We introduce analogues of partial derivatives with respect to the generators of the enveloping algebras U(gl(N)) and their q-deformations, the so-called modified reflection equation algebras. Using these quantum partial derivatives, we introduce the corresponding differential algebras, define the quantum de Rham operator, and exhibit the Leibniz rule for its action. Our Leibniz rule differs from its classical analogue and stems from the construction of a quantum double. In the case of the algebra U(gl(N)), we present another form of the Leibniz rule that is more convenient for prolonging the differential calculus to certain extensions of this algebra.
  We then use analogues of the Cayley-Hamilton identity for the generating matrices of U(gl(N)) and of reflection equation algebras to construct projective modules over these algebras. For these projective modules, we introduce Grassmannian connections and define the corresponding Chern classes.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum Sugawara operators in $\mathrm{U}_q(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_{M|N})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16627)
*Naihuan Jing,Ming Liu,Jian Zhang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文为量子仿射超代数 $\mathrm{U}_q(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_{M|N})$ 显式构造了Sugawara算子，推广了量子仿射代数情形下的结果，并计算了其Harish-Chandra像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子仿射代数中Sugawara算子的构造推广到量子仿射超代数 $\mathrm{U}_q(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_{M|N})$ 情形，并研究其Harish-Chandra像。

Method: 显式构造Sugawara算子，推广已有结果；通过计算Harish-Chandra像刻画其结构性质。

Result: 给出了 $\mathrm{U}_q(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_{M|N})$ 上Sugawara算子的显式表达式，并得到了其Harish-Chandra像的计算结果。

Conclusion: 成功将Sugawara算子的构造从量子仿射代数推广至量子仿射超代数情形，并通过Harish-Chandra像揭示了其代数结构。

Abstract: We construct Sugawara operators for the quantum affine superalgebra $\mathrm{U}_q(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_{M|N})$ in an explicit form which generalizes the results given in \cite{JLM} for the quantum affine algebras. We also calculate the Harish-Chandra images of the Sugawara operators.

</details>
