### [139] [Holographic CFT Thermodynamics as a Bridge Between the Weak Gravity and Weak Cosmic Censorship Conjectures for EMPYM--AdS Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16216)
*Saeed Noori Gashti,Behnam Pourhassan,Izzet Sakalli,Saheb Soroushfar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-幂律-杨-米尔斯-反德西特黑洞中的弱宇宙审查猜想与弱引力猜想，发现在标量微扰下宇宙审查成立，弱引力猜想仅决定演化方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究弱宇宙审查猜想和弱引力猜想在带有非阿贝尔幂律杨-米尔斯场、麦克斯韦场和负宇宙学常数的黑洞族中的表现，探讨它们在量子引力理论中如何约束奇点和带电物质。

Method: 在全息CFT热力学框架下，将中心电荷C和CFT体积Vol作为独立变量，推导包含杨-米尔斯电荷和非线性指数γ变分的扩展第一定律及欧拉关系。通过带电有质量标量场微扰，分析视界能量和电荷通量确定超辐射阈值，并展开度量函数f(r)在极值/近极值构型的最小值附近，研究吸收过程对f_min的影响。

Result: 吸收过程保持f_min非正，视界得以存活。杨-米尔斯场将有效荷质比阈值降低到Reissner-Nordström值1以下。局域稳定性分析在热容中发现Davies点。在考察的参数范围内，EMPYM族在标量微扰下尊重宇宙审查，弱引力猜想仅固定朝向或远离极值演化的方向。

Conclusion: EMPYM黑洞在标量微扰下满足弱宇宙审查猜想，弱引力猜想决定演化方向而非破坏宇宙审查。

Abstract: The weak cosmic censorship conjecture (\WCCC) and the weak gravity conjecture (\WGC) sit at the heart of how singularities and charged matter are constrained in any consistent theory of quantum gravity. We study both conjectures for Einstein--Maxwell--power--Yang--Mills--AdS (\EMPYM) black holes, a family in which a non-Abelian power-law Yang--Mills field, a Maxwell field, and a negative cosmological constant act together. Working in holographic conformal field theory (CFT) thermodynamics, we treat the central charge $C$ and the CFT volume $\Vol$ as independent variables and obtain an extended first law that carries variations of the Yang--Mills charge and of the nonlinearity exponent $γ$. A closed Euler relation follows, and we check it together with the full set of equations of state to machine precision. The perturbation is a charged massive scalar minimally coupled to the Maxwell sector. Its horizon energy and charge fluxes fix the superradiance threshold $ω<\tilde{q}_s\tildeφ_h$, and the mass--energy relation $μ_s=ω$ turns this into the bound $\tilde{q}_s/μ_s>r_{\min}/\tilde{Q}$. Expanding the metric function $f(r)$ about its minimum for extremal and near-extremal configurations, we find that absorption keeps $f_{\min}$ non-positive, so the horizon survives. The Yang--Mills sector lowers the effective charge-to-mass threshold below the Reissner--Nordström value of unity, while a local stability analysis locates a Davies point in the heat capacity. Across the parameter range examined the \EMPYM\ family respects cosmic censorship under scalar perturbations, with the \WGC\ fixing only the direction of evolution toward or away from extremality.

</details>


### [140] [Universality of Magic in Local Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16403)
*Valentin Benedetti,Atish Dabholkar,Marcello Dalmonte*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了在离散实现中，没有任何稳定子态能在连续极限下流向真空或任何在短距离上类似真空的态。原因在于稳定子态的纠缠谱是平坦的，而局域QFT中循环分离态的纠缠谱非平坦（源于子区域von Neumann代数的type III$_1$性质）。这意味着模拟物理相关的QFT态必然需要超越稳定子态和Clifford操作的量子资源，并对全息原理有重要启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究离散量子场论中的稳定子态是否能在连续极限下逼近真实的QFT真空态，从而厘清经典可模拟的量子态与真实物理态之间的根本性差距。

Method: 通过对比稳定子态（纠缠谱平坦）与局域QFT中循环分离态（纠缠谱非平坦）的纠缠谱结构差异，利用代数QFT中von Neumann代数的type III$_1$分类性质进行论证。

Result: 稳定子态在连续极限下无法流向QFT真空或任何短距离类似真空的态，模拟真实QFT态需要超越稳定子态和Clifford操作的量子资源。

Conclusion: 稳定子态与真实QFT真空态存在本质性的纠缠结构差异，这限制了经典可模拟量子态在物理相关场景中的应用，并对全息对偶中的态构造提出约束。

Abstract: We show that no stabilizer state in a discrete realization of a local quantum field theory can flow in the continuum to the vacuum or to any state that resembles the vacuum at short distances. The argument rests on the fact that the entanglement spectrum is flat for stabilizer states but non-flat for cyclic and separating states in a local QFT as a consequence of the type III$_1$ nature of the von Neumann algebras associated with arbitrary subregions. Our result implies that simulating physically relevant QFT states necessarily requires quantum resources beyond stabilizer states and Clifford operations. We comment on the implications for holography.

</details>


### [141] [Recursive construction of scalar one-loop integrals in dimensional regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16416)
*Paul Mork*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文推导了标量单圈费曼积分的递推结构，基于Schläfli微分公式，将ε展开系数与更低阶系数通过额外外腿的积分联系起来，证明所有Laurent展开系数均可表示为多重对数函数，并首次给出任意质量六边形积分在O(ε)阶的显式闭式MPL表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以高效计算高精度ε展开系数，需要一种构造性算法将任意阶系数系统性地表示为多重对数函数。

Method: 基于Schläfli双曲单形微分公式，建立递推结构连接不同阶ε系数；以ε=0项（双曲单形体体积）为种子，迭代递推生成高阶项。

Result: 所有Laurent展开系数均可由多重对数函数表示；首次给出任意质量和离壳欧氏运动学下六边形积分O(ε)系数的闭式MPL表达式。

Conclusion: 该递推结构提供了计算任意阶ε展开的构造性算法，严格证明了标量单圈积分在维数正规化下的MPL可表示性。

Abstract: We derive a novel recursive structure for dimensionally regularised scalar one-loop Feynman integrals based on Schläfli's differential formula for hyperbolic simplices. The recursion relates the Laurent coefficients in the dimensional regulator $\varepsilon$ of an $N$-point integral to lower-order coefficients of integrals with additional external legs. The construction is seeded by the $\varepsilon=0$ contributions, which admit a geometric interpretation as volumes of simplices in hyperbolic space and are known in terms of multiple polylogarithms (MPLs). Iterating the recursion therefore provides a constructive algorithm for computing arbitrary orders in the $\varepsilon$-expansion of scalar one-loop integrals with arbitrary masses and kinematics, while remaining entirely within the class of MPLs. In particular, this establishes that all coefficients in the Laurent expansion of dimensionally regularised scalar one-loop integrals can be expressed in terms of MPLs. As a first application beyond existing results, we obtain an explicit closed MPL expression for the $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon)$ coefficient of the scalar hexagon with arbitrary masses and off-shell Euclidean kinematics.

</details>


### [142] [A unified expansion of Einstein's gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16459)
*Arkachur Bhattacharya,Pushkar Soni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，通过参数化(s,n)和收缩参数ε，将伽利略引力、卡罗尔引力等非洛伦兹引力理论统一纳入爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用的系统展开中，并特别推导了弦卡罗尔(SC)引力，证明其能描述一般非极端黑洞的近视界几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中不同的非洛伦兹引力理论（如伽利略引力、卡罗尔引力）被分别处理，缺乏统一的理论框架来涵盖所有这些理论。

Method: 引入参数化标度(s,n)和收缩参数ε，建立统一的平坦几何与代数；在切空间中系统展开爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用（偶数幂ε展开），通过固定(s,n)得到不同非洛伦兹引力理论。

Result: 该统一展开可复现爱因斯坦引力、伽利略引力、卡罗尔引力等多种引力理论；新推导的弦卡罗尔(SC)引力中局域度规有两个零本征值，且一般非极端黑洞的近视界几何是SC引力的解。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为理解非洛伦兹引力提供了系统方法，SC引力有望成为研究一般黑洞近视界物理的新工具。

Abstract: Non-Lorentzian theories of gravity, most common of which are Galilean and Carrollian gravity, arise from General relativity under suitable scalings. General relativity can be obtained by gauging the Poincaré algebra. A convenient formulation of non-Lorentzian gravity follows the contraction of the Poincaré algebra in the tangent space to its non-Lorentzian counterparts, e.g. Galilean and Carrollian algebras. In existing literature, different non-Lorentzian theories of gravity have been addressed separately. In this paper, we introduce a single unified framework of expansion to address all these different theories. We show that different scalings can be unified into a single covariant form parametrized by $(s,n)$, alongside the contraction parameter $ε$. Keeping these parameters unfixed in the limit $ε\to 0$ defines a $\textit{unified flat geometry}$ and its $\textit{unified algebra}$, which reduces to a specific non-Lorentzian geometry for a particular choice of $s$ and $n$. Using this setup in the tangent space, we systematically expand the Einstein-Hilbert action in even powers of $ε$, which we call a $\textit{unified expansion}$ of Einstein's gravity, whose leading-order theory is fixed by $(s,n)$. This reproduces various classes of gravitational theories, including Einstein gravity (the trivial case), Galilean gravity, Carroll gravity, all of which can be extracted from this expansion. Using the expansion, we then formulate String Carroll (SC) gravity, where the local metric has two vanishing eigenvalues. The near-horizon region of generic non-extremal black holes has been recently shown to be a SC geometry. By considering explicit examples, we confirm that these near-horizon geometries constitute solutions of SC gravity, paving the way of understanding physics near the horizon of generic black holes in terms of SC gravity.

</details>


### [143] [Quantum phases at high chemical potential in 2-flavor matrix-QC$_2$D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16774)
*Nirmalendu Acharyya,Prasanjit Aich,Arkajyoti Bandyopadhyay,Sachindeo Vaidya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过变分法对角化矩阵-QCD$_{2,2}$哈密顿量，研究了大重子数、同位旋和手征化学势区域下的基态相结构，发现了一系列由量子相变连接的LOFF-like相，其基态携带有非零自旋并自发破缺旋转对称性，自旋主要由夸克的内部自旋贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在强耦合区域下，大重子数、同位旋和/或手征化学势驱动的两色两味QCD矩阵模型的基态性质与量子相变行为。

Method: 采用变分技术对角化简化后的哈密顿量，通过调节化学势参数研究基态相变。

Result: 发现当μ_B和c（或μ_B和μ_I）主导时，调节剩余参数导致量子相变，形成由重子数B和同位旋I标记的复杂相图，其中包括多个自旋非零、自发破缺旋转对称性的LOFF-like相。

Conclusion: LOFF-like基态由自旋-1的双（反）夸克（类似库珀对）组成，夸克内部自旋贡献了总自旋的显著部分甚至全部，结果与Splittorff-Son-Stephanov的有效场论预言一致。

Abstract: We investigate the matrix model of two-color two-flavor QCD (matrix-QCD$_{2,2}$) in regimes with large baryon ($μ_{_B}$), isospin ($μ_{_I}$), and/or chiral ($c$) chemical potentials. In these regimes, the Hamiltonian simplifies considerably, making it possible to investigate the ground state for intermediate and strong Yang-Mills coupling. By diagonalizing the Hamiltonian using the variational techniques, we show that in regimes where $μ_{_B}$ and $c$ (or $μ_{_B}$ and $μ_{_I}$) dominate, tuning the remaining parameters leads to quantum phase transitions (QPTs). These transitions form a complex web of phases, each of which has a ground state uniquely labelled by baryon number $B$ and isospin $I$. Several of these phases are LOFF-like, characterized by a ground state carrying non-zero spin and hence spontaneously breaking rotational symmetry. These results are consistent with older effective field theory predictions by Splittorff-Son-Stephanov \cite{Splittorff:2000mm}. The fermionic content of these LOFF-like ground states consists of spin-1 di-(anti-) quarks which are analogous to Cooper pairs. We compute the spin-fraction carried by the quarks and find that it constitutes a significant portion -- in some cases nearly the entirety -- of the total spin.

</details>


### [144] [Kibble-Zurek Mechanism and Current-Phase Relation in a Holographic Josephson Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16804)
*Zhi-Hong Li,Huai-Fan Li,Hai-Qing Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在全息超导环的约瑟夫森结中，通过Kibble-Zurek机制研究了电流-相位关系，发现正弦关系周期性出现，并揭示了临界电流与几何参数及温度的依赖关系，为强耦合高温超导体的非平衡动力学提供了实验可验证的预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息超导环中约瑟夫森结的电流-相位关系，特别是非平衡动力学和强耦合效应下的行为，为高温超导体提供可实验检验的理论框架。

Method: 利用Kibble-Zurek机制实现随机非平衡动力学，通过空间调制的电荷密度构造弱连接，在宽范围几何和热力学参数下研究电流-相位关系，并分析临界电流与几何参数（宽度、陡度、深度）及温度的关系。

Result: 发现了正弦电流-相位关系因几何的紧致形状而周期性出现；临界电流随结的几何参数变化，并随最终温度呈特征性指数衰减，反映了强耦合区域中序参量的热退化。

Conclusion: 建立了全息约瑟夫森器件的稳健理论框架，为高温超导体的非平衡动力学提供了实验可测试的预言。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive study of the current-phase relation of the Josephson junction in a holographic superfluid ring, realized from the stochastic and non-equilibrium dynamics according to the Kibble-Zurek mechanism. By employing a spatially modulated charge density to engineer a weak link, the current-phase relation is investigated in a range of geometric and thermodynamic parameters. The seminal sinusoidal relation between the current and the phase emerges periodically due to the compact shape of the geometry. We also identify the relations between the critical current and the geometric parameters of the junction: the width, steepness and depth. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the critical current exhibits a characteristic exponential decaying against the final temperature, reflecting the thermal degradation of the order parameter in a strong-coupling regime. Our results establish a robust framework for holographic Josephson devices, offering experimentally testable predictions for the non-equilibrium dynamics of high-$T_c$ superconductors.

</details>


### [145] [Electric field fluctuations and renormalization group flows in a self-interacting scalar field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16932)
*Melanie Martínez,Enrique Muñoz,Marcelo Loewe*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了背景电场存在经典随机涨落时，复λφ⁴模型中自相互作用带电标量场的物理参数变化。通过复制技巧对电场涨落进行统计平均，发现带电电流之间产生有效相互作用，其耦合常数与电场涨落的自相关函数幅度成正比。获得了修整传播子、相互作用顶点和重整化群方程，并绘制了耦合流图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究经典随机噪声（背景电场的随机涨落）对带电标量场理论物理参数的影响，特别是在弱电场和超强电场两种极端情形下的表现。

Method: 采用复制技巧对背景电场的经典随机涨落进行统计平均；通过协变导数将随机背景电场纳入Schwinger传播子中；计算生成泛函并推导出重整化群方程。

Result: 随机平均过程导致带电电流间产生有效相互作用，耦合常数正比于电场涨落的自相关函数幅度；获得了修整传播子、相互作用顶点和重整化群方程及其耦合流图。

Conclusion: 经典随机噪声会改变标量场理论的有效相互作用结构，且这种效应可通过重整化群流图在耦合参数空间中直观呈现。

Abstract: We consider a self interacting charged scalar field represented by the complex λφ4 model, embedded in a background electric field exhibiting classical stochastic fluctuations. We studied the effects of the classical stochastic noise on the physical parameters of the scalar field theory, for both weak and ultra strong electric field regimes. The stochastic background electric field is included in the Schwinger propagator through the covariant derivative, and the generating functional of the theory is found by means of the replica trick in order to compute the statistical average over electric fluctuations. As a result of the averaging process, an effective interaction between charged currents emerges, with a coupling constant proportional to the magnitude of the auto-correlation function of the electric field fluctuations. We obtained the dressed propagators, the interaction vertices, and the renormalization group equations of the theory, along with the corresponding streamplots in the manifold of interaction couplings.

</details>


### [146] [Non-Abelian Thirring model at large $N$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17246)
*Songyuan Li,Konstantinos Siampos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究半单群G在级别k下的非阿贝尔玻色化Thirring模型，在变形参数λ下计算了流算符和复合流算符的两点关联函数至λ的三阶，提取了β函数和异常维度，结果表明不存在k/c_G量级的额外临界点，与Destri & de Vega的费米子非阿贝尔Thirring模型结果一致，但与Dashen & Frishman声称的存在1/c_G量级额外固定点的结论相矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清非阿贝尔Thirring模型中是否存在额外临界点的争议，特别是验证Dashen & Frishman关于存在1/c_G量级固定点的断言。

Method: 采用非阿贝尔玻色化方法处理Thirring模型，在大二次Casimir算符c_G假设下，计算流算符和复合流算符的两点关联函数至λ的三阶微扰，从中提取β函数和异常维度。

Result: β函数和异常维度的计算表明不存在k/c_G量级的额外临界点。

Conclusion: 结果支持Destri & de Vega的费米子非阿贝尔Thirring模型结论，反驳Dashen & Frishman关于额外固定点的存在性主张。

Abstract: We consider the non-Abelian bosonized Thirring model for a semi-simple group $G$ at level $k$, with deformation parameter $λ$. We compute the two-point correlation functions of current and composite current operators to cubic order in $λ$, assuming large values of the quadratic Casimir $c_G$ of the group $G$ in the adjoint representation. From these, we extract the $β$-function and the anomalous dimensions of both the current and composite current operators, showing the absence of an additional critical point of order $k/c_G$. Our findings align with those of Destri & de Vega for the Fermionic non-Abelian Thirring model, but contradict the claim in Dashen & Frishman regarding the existence of an additional fixed point of order $1/c_G$.

</details>


### [147] [The Information Content of Krylov Observables: A Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17346)
*Ritam Basu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文使用机器学习分析三种Krylov空间可观测量（扩散复杂度、离散Wigner负性、归一化负性）的信息含量，发现归一化负性可作为混沌的特征探针，能以R²≈0.999重建热场温度，以98%准确率识别对称类，而二阶矩信息盈余是混沌的标志性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Krylov空间中的三种可观测量（扩散复杂度、离散Wigner负性、归一化负性）各自携带的信息量及其物理意义，特别是寻找混沌的量子特征信号。

Method: 使用小规模残差网络（16-32神经元）和提升树，对约57,000个标记演化数据（涵盖GUE、GOE、Poisson系综、可积SL(2,R)/CFT扇区及混沌插值模型）进行训练，分析三种可观测量对不同物理量的预测能力。

Result: 三种可观测量均能以R²≈0.999确定热场温度；无法重构精细谱型因子（R²≈0.18）；能以98%准确率识别对称类；在可积扇区信息等价；归一化负性在混沌插值中展现出持续增大的信息盈余差距（+0.33→+0.77），而原始负性的差距衰减至零。

Conclusion: 二阶矩信息盈余是混沌的特征信号，由归一化负性携带，作者给出了其分析机制和定量界。

Abstract: We employ machine learning to quantify the information carried by three Krylov-space observables: the spread complexity $\mathcal{C}(t)$, the discrete Wigner negativity $N(t)$, and the normalized negativity $χ(t)=N(t)/|S(t)|$, with $S(t)$ the survival amplitude, recently proposed as a second-moment infall probe (arXiv:2607.04065). Small residual networks (16-32 neurons) and boosted trees are trained on half of $\sim 57{,}000$ labeled evolutions spanning the GUE, GOE and Poisson ensembles, the integrable $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$/CFT sector, and the chaos interpolation $H(\varepsilon)=H_{SL(2,\mathbb{R})}+\varepsilon R_0 W_{GUE}$. Either moment determines the thermofield temperature at $R^2\simeq 0.999$. Neither reconstructs the fine spectral form factor ($R^2\simeq 0.18$ in every ensemble); smoothing the target does not repair this, and windows wide enough to help erase the dip-ramp physics itself: the SFF strictly refines both moments. The coarse $e^S$ plateau is nevertheless recovered at $R^2=0.861$, mostly from the first 20% of $\mathcal{C}(t)$. A single curve identifies the symmetry class at up to 98% accuracy. In the integrable sector the observables are informationally equivalent, as exact negative-binomial slaving demands, while the negativity best resolves the $(h,α)$ degeneracy ($N\to h$: 0.999). Along the interpolation the asymmetry gap of $χ$ over $\mathcal{C}$ switches on with chaos, growing from +0.33 to +0.77 as the level statistics cross to GUE, while the raw-$N$ gap decays to zero. The second-moment informational surplus is therefore a signature of chaos, carried specifically by the normalized negativity, and we derive an analytical mechanism and a quantitative bound for it.

</details>


### [148] [Geometric Approach to Quantum Theory. L-functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17566)
*Albert Schwarz*

Main category: hep-th

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于L-泛函形式论的理论框架，将其推广应用于QED、线性化引力和淬火无序系统，并提出构建红外有限微扰理论的具体猜想。

Result: 尚未给出最终结果，主要内容为形式论回顾与应用展望，核心成果是提出了一个关于如何构造红外有限QED微扰理论的猜想。

Conclusion: L-泛函形式论可能为QED红外问题提供新的解决方案，但该猜想仍需进一步的数学严格证明和物理检验。

Abstract: This publication consists of slides from my talk at the Simons Center for Geometry and Physics in 2024. It contains a brief review of the L-functional formalism, along with a discussion of its possible applications to QED, linearized gravity, and quenched disorder. The most interesting part is the discussion of the infrared problem in QED and a conjecture on how to construct an infrared-finite perturbation theory for QED.

</details>


### [149] [The next-to-next-to-leading order BFKL eigenvalue at odd conformal spin in planar N=4 super Yang-Mills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17608)
*Alex Prygarin,Claudelle Capasia Madjuogang Sandeu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出了平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中三圈（NNLO）BFKL特征值在奇合规自旋n下的闭式解，形式为嵌套调和和与有理函数的有限组合，并通过直接Mellin提取从Caron-Huot-Herranen三圈被积函数中精确提取原子表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将BFKL特征值从两圈推进到三圈精度，并探索其闭式解析结构，特别是奇自旋n下的完整形式。

Method: 采用直接Mellin变换从已知的三圈被积函数中提取原子表；使用有理算术精确计算每个奇数n≥3的系数；引入基于调和和与阶梯距离的系数闭合方案；采用有限数据全纯递推处理残差网格；通过Quantum Spectral Curve数值验证。

Result: 47个系数槽中40个闭合为两个阶梯距离的调和和；残差5个核层中1个服从有限数据全纯递推；系数加权阶梯在深度一上坍缩为digamma和trigamma函数；在ν=0处重现独立QSC值至n=91；特征值严格无乘积形式，奇自旋下无1+(-1)^n反常项。

Conclusion: 建立了三圈BFKL特征值在奇自旋下的完整闭式表达式，但未声称n一致形式；16个核闭合中11个已证明，5个数值验证。

Abstract: We present the three-loop (next-to-next-to-leading order) BFKL eigenvalue of planar N=4 super Yang-Mills at odd conformal spin n in closed form, as a finite combination of nested harmonic sums and rational functions of z=(|n|-1)/2 + i nu. For each odd n>=3 the atom table is extracted exactly, in rational arithmetic, from the Caron-Huot-Herranen three-loop integrand by direct Mellin extraction; a shipped command regenerates it on demand, with n=1 a supplied boundary block. The coefficients are rational multiples of 1, pi^2 and zeta_3; in a fixed elementary basis, forty of the forty-seven coefficient slots close as harmonic sums of the two ladder distances (all-spin form conjectural, the split a property of the basis), while the residual five-kernel layer includes one grid obeying a finite-data holonomic recurrence, an all-n rule for the rest still open. Beyond the explicit harmonic sums the coefficient-weighted ladder collapses onto depth-one digamma and trigamma functions. At nu=0 the intercepts reproduce the independent Quantum Spectral Curve values spin by spin through n=91. At each odd spin the eigenvalue is strictly product-free, the additive case of the Kotikov-Lipatov hermitian-separable form; the next-to-leading anomalous term carrying 1+(-1)^n is absent at odd n. No n-uniform form is claimed. Derivations (eleven of sixteen kernel closures proven, five verified numerically) and complete expressions are given in a companion paper.

</details>


### [150] [The next-to-next-to-leading order BFKL eigenvalue at odd conformal spin in planar N=4 super Yang-Mills: closed form, coefficient structure, and arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17613)
*Alex Prygarin,Claudelle Capasia Madjuogang Sandeu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出了平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论在奇数共形自旋n下的三圈（NNLO）BFKL本征值的闭合解析解，该解由连续嵌套调和和与有理函数在整数移位阶梯上构成。通过从Caron-Huot-Herranen三圈被积函数中精确提取原子表，验证了n≤17及多个m值的自旋一致性，并在nu=0处与量子谱曲线独立值吻合至n=91。研究发现系数为1、π²和ζ₃的有理倍数，其中47个系数槽中的40个可闭合为调和和形式，剩余5个核层中存在一个满足整循环递推的格点，并报告了一个算术障碍：异常格点系数在每个n=p+2（n-2为素数）处引入新的分母素数，证明该有限定律且推测其持续存在，导致其生成函数不可能为代数或全局有界函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微扰量子场论中，BFKL方程描述高能散射中的小x物理行为。该文致力于计算N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中三圈水平的BFKL本征值，这是检验AdS/CFT对应和探索可积性的关键。此前已有双圈结果，三圈计算极为复杂，需要发展新的计算方法来获得闭合解析形式。

Method: 采用Caron-Huot-Herranen三圈被积函数，通过有理算术精确提取每个奇数自旋n的原子表（atom table）；使用移位阶梯上的连续嵌套调和和（continued nested harmonic sums）与有理函数表示；引入主生成函数（已验证至n=17及多个m值）但不用于逐自旋表的生成；利用量子谱曲线（QSC）进行交叉验证；分析系数在1、π²、ζ₃基组下的结构，将47个槽中的40个闭合为调和和形式，剩余5个核层中的格点满足整循环递推关系。

Result: 首次获得三圈BFKL本征值的闭合解析表达式，验证了与QSC一致性至n=91（奇数自旋）；发现47个系数槽中40个可表示为调和和，剩余5个异常格点系数在每个n=p+2（n-2为素数）处产生新的分母素数，证明该有限定律且推测其无限延续，由此推出异常格点的生成函数不可能为代数或全局有界函数。所有偶数自旋推测可由同一主生成函数描述。每个奇数自旋的本征值满足Kotikov-Lipatov厄米可分形式（无乘积项）。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了三圈BFKL本征值的精确计算，揭示了其中深刻的数论结构（调和和、素数分母障碍），表明异常格点的算术复杂性可能源于更深层的数学结构，其生成函数超越代数函数范畴。偶数自旋结果尚待严格推导但已有明确推测。该结果对理解N=4 SYM的可积性和AdS/CFT对偶具有重要意义。

Abstract: We present the three-loop (next-to-next-to-leading order) BFKL eigenvalue of planar N=4 super Yang-Mills at odd conformal spin n in closed form, as a finite combination of continued nested harmonic sums and rational functions of z=(|n|-1)/2 + i nu on an integer-shifted ladder. For each odd spin the atom table is extracted exactly, in rational arithmetic, from the Caron-Huot-Herranen three-loop integrand; a shipped command regenerates it for any odd n>=3, with n=1 a supplied boundary block. A resummed master generating function, conjectured to reproduce those integrands at every spin (verified through n=17 and at m=9,11,13,15,21,31), is not used for the per-spin tables. At nu=0 the intercepts reproduce the independent Quantum Spectral Curve values spin by spin through n=91. The coefficients are rational multiples of 1, pi^2 and zeta_3; in a fixed elementary basis, forty of the forty-seven slots close as harmonic sums of the two ladder distances (all-spin form conjectural). The residual five-kernel layer includes one grid obeying a holonomic recurrence; once the harmonic sums are separated the coefficient-weighted ladder collapses onto two depth-one polygamma transcendentals. We report an arithmetic obstruction: the exceptional-grid coefficients recruit a new denominator prime at each n=p+2 (n-2 prime) through the computed range, a finite law we prove and conjecture to continue; on that continuation their generating data cannot be algebraic or globally bounded. Even spins are conjecturally contained in the same master. At each odd spin the eigenvalue is the product-free case of the Kotikov-Lipatov hermitian-separable form. A companion Letter announces the result.

</details>


### [151] [Hypercomplex formulation of dissipative scalar electrodynamics and phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17626)
*B. R. López-Raymundo,R. Cartas-Fuentevilla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了超复标量电动力学，其中耗散作为U(1)×SO(1,1)扩展局域规范对称性固有的动态特性出现。通过构造有效自由能泛函，推导了广义Ginzburg-Landau方程并分析了热力学稳定性。对真空态的四阶分析表明，与不变量相关的系数约束禁止混合态共存，正式确立了双临界点的存在。因此，系统在穿越临界温度时被迫发生不连续的一级相变。此外，双曲对称性在有效四次耦合取负值时引发结构不稳定性，使自由能无下界并产生不稳定渐近方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立超复标量电动力学形式体系，将耗散作为扩展规范对称性的内禀动力学特性加以描述，而非作为外部附加效应引入。

Method: 基于U(1)×SO(1,1)扩展局域规范对称性构建超复标量电动力学，构造有效自由能泛函并推导广义Ginzburg-Landau方程，对真空态进行至四阶的解析分析。

Result: (1) 系数约束禁止混合态共存，正式确立双临界点；(2) 系统穿越临界温度时发生不连续的一级相变；(3) 有效四次耦合取负值时双曲对称性导致结构不稳定性，自由能无下界。

Conclusion: 超复标量电动力学中耗散源于扩展规范对称性，系统表现出强制一级相变和由双曲对称性引发的结构不稳定性等独特热力学行为。

Abstract: A hypercomplex formulation of scalar electrodynamics is developed, in which dissipation emerges as an intrinsic dynamic property dictated by the extended local gauge symmetry U(1)XSO(1,1). By constructing an effective free-energy functional, we derive the generalized Ginzburg-Landau equations and analyze the thermodynamic stability of the system. Our analysis of the vacuum state up to the fourth order reveals that the constraint on the coefficients associated with the invariants prohibits the coexistence of mixed states, formally establishing the existence of a bicritical point. Consequently, upon crossing the critical temperature, the system is forced to undergo a discontinuous first-order phase transition. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the hyperbolic symmetry induces a structural instability when the effective quartic couplings take negative values, rendering the free energy unbounded from below and creating asymptotic directions of instability.

</details>


### [152] [Analytic HTEE in Moving Plasmas and Its Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17721)
*Mohammad Ali-Akbari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在AdS₃/CFT₂对偶框架下，研究了洛伦兹boosted BTZ黑洞背景中1+1维等离子体的全息类时纠缠熵（HTEE），发现了临界boost速度导致的实部虚部转变和极值曲面分支终结现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索boosted等离子体中HTEE的行为，特别是boost速度如何影响全息类时纠缠熵的实部与虚部结构，以及是否存在临界速度导致物理图像的转变。

Method: 在AdS₃/CFT₂对偶下，采用洛伦兹boosted BTZ黑洞背景，解析求解耦合的极值曲面方程，得到HTEE的闭式表达式。

Result: 1）低于临界boost速度时，极值曲面转折点为纯虚数，熵的虚部为普适贡献，实部依赖boost速度；2）存在临界boost速度，在此处复极值曲面分支终止；3）超过临界速度后，物理允许的极值曲面变为纯实数，HTEE过渡到类似标准全息纠缠熵的类空间实鞍点；4）零速度极限恢复已知静态BTZ结果。

Conclusion: 发现boost速度可诱导HTEE从复值到实值的相变，揭示了类时纠缠熵与类空纠缠熵在特定条件下的连续过渡机制。

Abstract: We investigate the holographic timelike entanglement entropy of a boosted $(1+1)$-dimensional plasma within the AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ correspondence, considering a Lorentz-boosted BTZ black hole background. We solve the coupled extremal-surface equations analytically and obtain a closed-form expression for the HTEE. We show that below a critical boost velocity, the turning points of the extremal surface are purely imaginary, leading to the universal imaginary contribution to the entropy, while the real part acquires a nontrivial dependence on the boost velocity. Moreover, we identify a critical boost velocity at which the complex extremal-surface branch terminates. Beyond this critical velocity, the physically admissible extremal surfaces become purely real, signalling a transition from the holographic timelike entanglement entropy to a spacelike-like real saddle that is geometrically similar to the standard holographic entanglement entropy. The known static BTZ result is recovered in the zero-velocity limit, as expected.

</details>


### [153] [Leading Yukawa terms in the three-loop anomalous dimension and four-loop $β$-function of ${\cal N}=1$ supersymmetric theories for various renormalization prescriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17848)
*Ratmir Jumanov,Konstantin Stepanyantz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了任意可重整化N=1超对称理论中物质超场反常维度的三环贡献（正比于Yukawa耦合的六次方），并基于高阶协变导数正规化结合最小减除的对数（HD+MSL）方案下NSVZ关系的有效性，计算了相应的四环规范β-函数，分析了其方案依赖性，最终构建了使重整化群函数形式最简的“最小”重整化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在N=1超对称理论中，NSVZ关系将规范β-函数与反常维度联系起来，但该关系对重整化方案有依赖性。作者希望利用HD+MSL方案下NSVZ关系成立的性质，计算更高阶的修正，并探究不同重整化方案下的影响，最终寻找形式最简的方案。

Method: 使用高阶协变导数方法进行正规化，结合最小减除对数方案，通过微扰计算得到三环反常维度（Yukawa耦合六次方阶），再利用NSVZ关系推导四环规范β-函数，并系统分析不同重整化方案对结果的影响。

Result: 获得了三环反常维度中正比于Yukawa耦合六次方的解析结果；利用NSVZ关系推导了对应的四环规范β-函数；揭示了这些重整化群函数对重整化方案的依赖性；构造了使函数形式最简的“最小”重整化方案。

Conclusion: 在HD+MSL方案下，NSVZ关系确保了β-函数与反常维度之间的精确关系，使得高阶计算具有一致性。通过恰当选择重整化方案，可以简化超对称理论中重整化群函数的形式，同时保持超对称性导出的精确方程仍然成立。

Abstract: For an arbitrary renormalizable ${\cal N}=1$ supersymmetric theory with a single gauge coupling regularized by higher covariant derivatives we calculate the three-loop contribution to the anomalous dimension of the matter superfields proportional to the sixth powers of Yukawa couplings for a wide class of renormalization prescriptions. Next, taking into account that in the case of using the higher covariant derivatives supplemented by minimal subtractions of logarithms the NSVZ relation is valid in all orders, we calculate the corresponding four-loop contribution to the gauge $β$-function and investigate its scheme dependence. In particular, we construct the so-called ``minimal'' renormalization prescription for which the renormalization group functions have the simplest possible form provided that the exact equations for the gauge and Yukawa $β$-functions following from supersymmetry remain valid.

</details>


### [154] [Dynamical Timelike Entanglement Entropy in an Evaporating Schwarzschild--AdS Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17874)
*Digen Das,Prabwal Phukon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将timelike entanglement entropy (tEE) 从静态背景扩展至蒸发中的Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞，通过绝热推广的Kruskal构造获得了依赖于整个蒸发历史的动态tEE，揭示了蒸发过程导致的不均匀Page时间间隔、相位延迟和振幅调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有tEE研究局限于静态背景，而真实黑洞因Hawking辐射而蒸发，时空是动态演化的。本文旨在建立蒸发黑洞中tEE的第一性原理框架，以探测引力系统中时间量子关联的动力学行为。

Method: 采用有效Stefan-Boltzmann描述模拟Hawking辐射与外部吸收浴的耦合，得到时变视界半径$r_h(t)$和表面引力$\kappa(t)$；利用近视界Rindler结构进行绝热推广的Kruskal构造，引入累积热相位$\Phi(t)=\int_0^t\kappa(t')\,dt'$替代静态相位$\kappa t$；通过绝热参数$\mathcal A(t)$验证构造的有效性。

Result: 绝热参数$\mathcal A(t)$在整个感兴趣区域保持很小($\lesssim0.012$)，在$r_h=l/\sqrt3$临界半径处精确为零。动态tEE连续追踪蒸发过程，Page时间间隔变得不均匀，伴随相位延迟和振幅调制。动态熵依赖于$\kappa(t)$的完整历史而非瞬时值，保留了整个蒸发过程的记忆。

Conclusion: 建立了蒸发黑洞中动态tEE的第一性原理框架，证明了tEE具有蒸发历史记忆效应，为研究蒸发黑洞中的时间量子关联提供了新工具。

Abstract: Timelike entanglement entropy (tEE) has recently emerged as a novel probe of temporal quantum correlations in gravitational systems. Existing studies are largely restricted to static backgrounds. In this work we extend the construction of tEE to an evaporating Schwarzschild--AdS black hole. The evaporation is modeled through an effective Stefan--Boltzmann description of Hawking radiation coupled to an external absorptive bath, yielding a time-dependent horizon radius ($r_h(t)$) and surface gravity $κ(t)$. We derive, from the near-horizon Rindler structure of the evolving horizon, an adiabatic generalization of the static Kruskal construction, obtaining the accumulated thermal phase $Φ(t)=\int_0^tκ(t')\,dt'$ as the natural replacement for the static phase $κt$. The validity of this construction is governed by an explicit adiabatic parameter $\mathcal A(t)$, which we verify numerically remains small ($\lesssim0.012$) throughout the regime of interest. It vanishes exactly where the horizon crosses the critical radius $r_h=l/\sqrt3$ identified independently from the static thermodynamics. Using $Φ(t)$, we construct a dynamical timelike entanglement entropy that continuously tracks the evaporation process and derive the corresponding dynamical Page-like times. Unlike the uniformly spaced Page-like times of the static geometry, evaporation induces non-uniform temporal spacing, together with a progressive phase delay and amplitude modulation of the oscillatory tEE. Because the dynamical entropy depends on the full accumulated history of $κ(t)$ rather than its instantaneous value alone, it retains a memory of the entire evaporation process. These results establish a first-principles dynamical framework for investigating temporal quantum correlations in evaporating black holes.

</details>


### [155] [Comments on holographic spread complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18024)
*Zhehan Li,Jia Tian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了将展宽复杂度增长率与体探针径向动量联系起来的全息提议，在"复杂度=任何事物"框架下给出了更一般的推导，并通过平直时空和德西特时空的对比分析，揭示了半经典极限本身不足以建立动量-复杂度关系，关键在于量子态必须保持相干态结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在识别原始全息提议的潜在假设，并阐明经典探针动力学如何从量子动力学中涌现出来。

Method: 直接对AdS探针进行量子化并利用外推词典，在"复杂度=任何事物"框架下给出更一般的推导；同时在平直时空和德西特时空中检验该提议。

Result: 在平直时空中，物理哈密顿量不保持相干态结构，展宽复杂度测量的是波包的色散展宽而非经典动量；在德西特时空静态贴片能量表示中的半经典波包同样表现出色散行为，而适配于主级数表示的相干态可呈现指数增长的展宽复杂度，其增长率与经典径向动量具有相同的时间依赖性。

Conclusion: 半经典极限本身不足以建立动量-复杂度关系；量子态必须保持相干态结构才能使展宽复杂度增长率对应经典径向动量。

Abstract: We revisit the holographic proposal relating the growth rate of spread complexity to the radial momentum of a bulk probe, aiming to identify its underlying assumptions and clarify how classical probe dynamics emerges from quantum dynamics. By quantizing AdS probes directly and using the extrapolation dictionary, we provide a more general derivation of the proposal. In particular, we interpret it as a concrete realization of a classical complexity observable and its quantization in the ``complexity=anything'' framework. Since probe dynamics is not intrinsically tied to the AdS/CFT correspondence, we also examine the proposal in flat and de Sitter spacetimes. In flat spacetime, the physical Hamiltonian does not preserve the coherent-state structure, and spread complexity measures the dispersive broadening of the wave packet rather than classical momentum. In de Sitter space, generic semiclassical wave packets in the static-patch energy representation show the same dispersive behavior. By contrast, coherent states adapted to the principal-series representation can exhibit exponential growth of spread complexity, with a growth rate that has the same time dependence as the classical radial momentum. These examples indicate that a semiclassical limit alone is not sufficient for a momentum--complexity relation.

</details>


### [156] [Non-injective field redefinitions and quantum inequivalence in scalar theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18166)
*Bin Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究通过多项式场重定义（常数单位雅可比）拉回自由有质量多重态得到的标量理论。主要例子使用Alpöge提出的三变量非单射映射，定义了具有平坦单位体积场空间度量和三个孤立真空的三标量sigma模型。每个真空局部由三个质量为m的自由模式描述，精确运动方程在每个叶上退化为自由Klein-Gordon方程。但全局理论并非单一自由理论：场空间度量不完备，目标空间中实原像数目变化，对易位置算子具有非恒定联合谱多重性。这排除了场重定义的全局酉实现和形式正则动量的正则Weyl指数化。四标量映射分析显示相同机制，并有一个额外场控制纤维多项式。两个例子区分了局部叶上的微扰等价和全场空间的全局量子等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通过非单射多项式场重定义（常数单位雅可比）构建的标量理论是否在全局意义上等价于自由理论，检验场重定义在量子理论中的全局酉实现可能性。

Method: 以Alpöge三变量非单射映射为主要例子，进行线性归一化后构造三标量sigma模型；分析其场空间度量、真空结构、运动方程；对比局部自由理论与全局非自由性质；并用四标量映射（含五次纤维）扩展分析。

Result: 发现全局理论并非单一自由理论：场空间度量不完备、实原像数目变化、位置算子联合谱多重性非常数，因此无法全局酉实现场重定义及正则Weyl指数化。

Conclusion: 区分了局部叶上的微扰等价与全场空间的全局量子等价，表明此类非单射场重定义在全局量子层面上不等价于自由理论。

Abstract: We study scalar theories obtained by pulling a free massive multiplet back through a polynomial field redefinition with constant unit Jacobian. Our main example uses the three-variable noninjective map recently announced by Alpöge. After a linear normalization, it defines a three-scalar sigma model with a flat, unit-volume field-space metric and three isolated vacua. Each vacuum is locally described by three free modes of mass m, and the exact equations of motion reduce locally on each sheet to free Klein--Gordon equations. The global theory is nevertheless not a single free theory: the field-space metric is incomplete, the number of real preimages changes across target space, and the commuting position operators have nonconstant joint spectral multiplicity. This rules out a global unitary implementation of the field redefinition and a regular Weyl exponentiation of the formal canonical momenta. We then analyze a four-scalar map with a generic quintic fiber. It exhibits the same mechanism with an additional field that controls the fiber polynomial. The two examples separate perturbative equivalence on a chosen local sheet from global quantum equivalence of the full field space.

</details>


### [157] [Gravitational Vacuum Polarization: Decoupling and the Conformal Anomaly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18180)
*Emil Mottola*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出了量子标量场应力张量关联函数⟨T^{ab}T^{cd}⟩的紧凑形式，重点研究了m→∞极限下的退耦和m→0时共形反常的红外起源。验证了协变守恒的Ward恒等式，分析了自旋-2和自旋-0两个张量通道的形状因子，发现ξ=1/6时自旋-0形状因子在n=4维度完全有限，无需UV正规化，其谱函数在m=0时退化为零能处的狄拉克δ函数，揭示了低能有效引力理论中共形反常导致的无质量标量Goldstone集体激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推导量子标量场应力张量关联函数的紧凑形式，阐明其在质量极限下的退耦行为以及共形反常的红外起源，验证Ward恒等式的完整性。

Method: 采用n维正规化方法计算形状因子，通过最小减除处理极点项，分析自旋-2（无迹）和自旋-0（有迹）两个张量通道的关联函数，验证协变守恒的Ward恒等式，并引入Wilson重正化群流描述有效场论极限。

Result: 验证了Ward恒等式的成立；发现ξ≠1/6时最小减除的形状因子不满足退耦条件但可通过简单修正解决；ξ=1/6时自旋-0形状因子在n=4完全有限，无需UV正规化；其谱函数满足UV有限和规则；m=0时谱函数退化为零能狄拉克δ函数。

Conclusion: 共形反常在低能有效引力理论中产生无质量标量Goldstone集体激发；ξ=1/6情况下自旋-0通道具有特殊的非微扰性质，其质量依赖描述了从紫外到红外的Wilson重正化群流。

Abstract: A compact form of the stress tensor $\langle T^{ab}T^{cd}\rangle$ correlation function of a quantum scalar field of arbitrary mass $m$ and curvature coupling $ξ$ is presented, with particular emphasis on decoupling in the $m\to\infty$ limit and infrared origin of the conformal anomaly as $m \to 0$. The Ward Identity (WI) of covariant conservation is verified for the full second order metric variation of the one-loop effective action, of which $\langle T^{ab}T^{cd}\rangle$ is part, including local contact terms. This WI is satisfied by two tensors, one spin-2 (traceless) and the second spin-0 (traceful), each multiplied by a Lorentz invariant form factor computed in $n$-dimensional regularization. Minimal subtraction of pole terms at $n =4$ produces form factors that fail to satisfy decoupling for general $ξ\neq 1/6$, but can be simply amended to do so. Particular interest attaches to the $ξ=1/6$ case, in which the spin-0 form factor at $n =4$ is completely finite, requires no UV regularization or subtractions, and satisfies decoupling directly. Its imaginary part defines a spectral function that obeys a UV finite sum rule for any $m$, while its real part unambiguously determines the $\square R$ in the massless limit. Its $m^2$ dependence describes a Wilsonian renormalization group flow to an effective field theory limit at large distances. At $m= 0$ the spin-0 spectral function becomes a Dirac $δ$-function at zero energy, demonstrating the existence of a massless scalar Goldstone collective excitation due to the conformal anomaly in the low energy effective theory of gravity.

</details>


### [158] [All Tree-Level Massive Cosmological Correlators via Spectral Gluing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18223)
*Jonathan Gräfe,Denis Werth*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了宇宙学树图级关联函数中隐藏的简洁超几何结构，通过谱表示和谱胶合算法，将复杂的超几何依赖简化为有理函数，发现了广义超几何函数间的新型恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量宇宙学关联函数在树图级就已展现出丰富的超几何结构，这种复杂性源于粒子在de Sitter时空中的扭曲传播。作者希望揭示这种表观复杂性背后隐藏的简单数学结构。

Method: 利用谱表示方法计算任意树图级标量场关联函数（含一般质量），证明其基本构建块属于Lauricella广义超几何函数族，通过谱积分胶合。开发了谱胶合算法，通过图组合学计算这些积分，得到解析级数表示。使用图湮灭算子发现广义超几何函数间的新恒等式。

Result: 谱胶合算法自然生成质量关联函数作为本征函数展开的解；剥离动力学传播子后，剩余的超几何运动学依赖坍缩为有理函数；发现了一类新的广义超几何函数间的恒等式。

Conclusion: 宇宙学关联函数的刚性超几何解析结构隐藏着由图组合学决定的简洁性，暗示着质量关联函数性质自然涌现的内在几何原理。

Abstract: Massive cosmological correlators exhibit a rich hypergeometric structure already at tree level, reflecting the distorted propagation of particles in de Sitter spacetime. In this paper, we reveal that this apparent complexity conceals a remarkably simple underlying mathematical structure. Using the spectral representation, we compute arbitrary tree-level correlators of scalar fields with generic masses and show that they are constructed from fundamental building blocks belonging to the family of Lauricella generalised hypergeometric functions, glued together by spectral integrals. We develop a spectral gluing algorithm that evaluates these integrals through elementary graph combinatorics, yielding explicit series representations that resum the dependence on internal energies away from soft limits. This algorithm naturally generates solutions to the differential equations satisfied by massive correlators as expansions in the corresponding eigenfunctions. Acting with a set of graph annihilators, we uncover a new class of magical identities among generalised hypergeometric functions, revealing an unexpected simplification: once the dynamical propagators are stripped away, the remaining hypergeometric kinematic dependence collapses to rational functions. Our results expose a hidden simplicity in the rigid hypergeometric analytic structure dictated by graph combinatorics, and hint at an intrinsic geometric principle from which properties of massive correlators naturally emerge.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Frame Representation of the First-Order Part of the Laplace-Beltrami Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16425)
*A. G. Nuramatov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了定向黎曼流形上Laplace-Beltrami算子一阶部分的几何内容。相对于局部标准正交标架，该一阶部分定义了一个特殊的向量场，其系数通过Levi-Civita联络形式表达；对应的一次形式定义的协变导数通过联络分解消除了Laplace-Beltrami算子的一阶部分，并代之以一个自然标量势。该构造依赖于标架，其在局部旋转下的变换补偿了平方和部分的相应变化，从而保持完整的Laplace-Beltrami算子不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Laplace-Beltrami算子一阶部分背后的几何本质，理解其与标架选取、联络形式及标量势之间的内在联系。

Method: 通过引入局部标准正交标架，将Laplace-Beltrami算子的一阶部分与Levi-Civita联络形式相联系，定义对应的向量场和一次形式；利用协变导数的联络分解消除一阶部分并引入自然标量势；分析标架旋转下各部分的变换行为。

Result: Laplace-Beltrami算子的一阶部分可被解释为与Levi-Civita联络相关的一个向量场，其对应的一次形式通过协变导数分解产生一个自然标量势；该构造在局部正交标架旋转下，一阶部分与平方和部分协同变换以保持算子整体不变。

Conclusion: 揭示了Laplace-Beltrami算子一阶部分的几何角色：它并非冗余项，而是与标架选取和联络结构几何关联的重要组成部分，其变换行为确保了算子的几何不变性。

Abstract: We investigate the geometric content of the first-order part of the Laplace--Beltrami operator on an oriented Riemannian manifold. Relative to an arbitrary local orthonormal frame, the first-order part of the Laplace--Beltrami operator defines a distinguished vector field, whose coefficients are expressed through the Levi--Civita connection forms.
  The corresponding one-form determines a covariant derivative whose connection factorization removes the first-order part of the Laplace--Beltrami operator and replaces it by a natural scalar potential.
  The construction is frame dependent. We show that its transformation under local rotations of the orthonormal frame compensates the corresponding variation of the sum-of-squares part, leaving the full Laplace--Beltrami operator invariant.
  These results reveal the geometric role of the first-order part of the Laplace--Beltrami operator.

</details>


### [160] [Approximate thermodynamics of the two-dimensional Ising model in an external magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16735)
*Mathieu Gaudreault*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了二维伊辛模型在外磁场中的热力学近似表达式。通过四元数表示和小角度近似，将转移矩阵简化为中心对称矩阵，从而得到自由能的近似表达式，并推导了磁化强度、磁化率、内能和比热。与BKL算法数值模拟对比，在近似有效区域内结果吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外磁场破坏了二维伊辛模型的对称性，使得自由能的精确计算变得极其复杂。现有方法难以处理这一非对称情形，因此需要发展有效的近似方法。

Method: 采用四元数表示将模型写入参考系，应用小角度近似简化转移矩阵为中心对称矩阵，利用已知的最大特征值依赖于外磁场强度的性质，推导自由能及多种热力学量的近似表达式。

Result: 得到了磁化强度、磁化率、内能和比热的近似表达式，并与BKL算法数值模拟结果进行了对比验证。

Conclusion: 提出的近似表达式在近似有效区域内与数值模拟结果一致，为外磁场下二维伊辛模型的热力学性质提供了有效的理论近似工具。

Abstract: We introduce approximate expressions for the thermodynamics of the two-dimensional Ising model in an external magnetic field. The external field breaks the system's symmetry, which complicates the exact calculation of the free energy. To address this, we write the model in its quaternion representation to apply a small angle approximation with respect to a reference frame. The approximation simplifies the transfer matrix to a centrosymmetric matrix for which the largest eigenvalue is known and depends on the external field strength. We therefore write an approximate expression for the system's free energy and derive the magnetization, susceptibility, internal energy, and specific heat. These results are compared with numerical simulations performed with the BKL algorithm. We find that the approximate expressions and numerical simulations agree in regions where the approximation is valid.

</details>


### [161] [Subextensive Random Boundary Perturbations in the Short-Range Edwards--Anderson Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16770)
*Hexiang Wang,Keheng Zhu,Mauris Chueng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究证明，在立方体Edwards-Anderson Ising模型中，任何边界随机扰动只要能量包络相对于体积可忽略（期望和几乎必然意义下），就不会改变淬火比自由能的极限。对于二维及以上具有独立有限方差体无序的系统，Efron-Stein估计还给出了沿完整盒子序列的几乎必然自平均性。该结果适用于特定自由能，不涉及有限体积Gibbs测度的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究边界随机扰动对最近邻Edwards-Anderson Ising模型淬火比自由能极限的影响，探究在何种条件下边界效应不会改变体热力学极限。

Method: 通过能量包络分析，证明边界扰动在期望和几乎必然意义下相对于体积可忽略时，自由能极限保持不变；利用Efron-Stein估计证明几乎必然自平均性；对i.i.d.标量表面场、固定随机外自旋和周期卷绕键三种情形验证假设。

Result: 任何能量包络相对于体积可忽略的边界扰动不改变淬火比自由能的极限；二维及以上独立有限方差体无序系统具有沿全序列的几乎必然自平均性；三种典型边界条件（i.i.d.标量表面场、固定随机外自旋、周期卷绕键）下淬火均值达到O(L^{-1})量级的比较。

Conclusion: 边界扰动在满足能量可忽略条件下不影响体自由能极限，且体无序系统具有自平均性。结果仅限于比自由能，不保证有限体积Gibbs测度的收敛性。

Abstract: We consider the nearest-neighbor Edwards--Anderson Ising model on cubic boxes with random perturbations supported at the boundary. We prove that any perturbation admitting an energy envelope that is negligible compared with the volume, both in expectation and almost surely, leaves the limiting quenched specific free energy unchanged. For independent finite-variance bulk disorder in dimension at least two, an Efron--Stein estimate also yields almost-sure self-averaging along the full sequence of boxes. The hypotheses are verified for i.i.d. scalar surface fields, fixed random exterior spins, and periodic wrap-around bonds, with an $ O(L^{-1}) $ comparison of quenched means. The result concerns the specific free energy and does not assert convergence of finite-volume Gibbs measures.

</details>


### [162] [The Lee-Yang property of the Blume-Capel model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16958)
*Yuri Kozitsky,Yurij Holovatch*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现铁磁相互作用可以在Blume-Capel模型中诱导Lee-Yang性质，即使单自旋配分函数在βΔ > ln2时失去该性质。这是首次发现相互作用可以恢复Lee-Yang性质的案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blume-Capel模型的单自旋配分函数在βΔ > ln2时失去Lee-Yang性质，但作者希望探究铁磁相互作用是否能够重新诱导该性质。

Method: 基于Lee-Yang定理和Lieb-Sokal定理的理论分析，研究铁磁相互作用对Blume-Capel模型Lee-Yang性质的影响。

Result: 证明铁磁相互作用可以诱导Blume-Capel模型具有Lee-Yang性质，即使在单自旋配分函数不满足该性质的参数区域（βΔ > ln2）。

Conclusion: 首次发现相互作用可以恢复Lee-Yang性质，为理解相变和临界现象提供了新视角。

Abstract: The Lee-Yang theory is based on the theorem concerning a property of the ferromagnetic Ising model partition function proved by T. D. Lee and C. N. Yang in 1952. It provides powerful tools for understanding the very nature of phase transitions and critical phenomena in various spin and similar models. Sometimes, this theory is applied to models for which the theorem's validity has not been proved. By the main result of E. H. Lieb and A. D. Sokal, Commun. Math. Phys. 80, 153 (1981), for a given ferromagnetic model, this validity is guaranteed by the same property of the single-spin partition function. Due to the anisotropy term $\sum_i ΔS_i^2$, the single-spin partition function of the Blume-Capel model fails to have the Lee-Yang property for $βΔ> \ln 2$. In this article, we show that the ferromagnetic interaction in such a model can induce the Lee-Yang property, even for these values of $βΔ$. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first result of this kind.

</details>


### [163] [Stochastic stability of master-slave synchronization for dissipative PDEs with Burgers-type nonlinearity and application to data assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17002)
*Joaquín Miguez,Inés P. Mariño*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类含Burgers型对流非线性项和多项式线性微分算子的非线性耗散演化方程的主-从同步随机稳定性。通过傅里叶截断将方程离散化，证明了确定性同步流形的局部指数稳定性，并在引入观测噪声后建立了随机同步误差与确定性参考误差之差的有限时间均方界O(σ²)及尾概率估计。在全局单边耗散假设下得到时间一致界。最后将随机从动力学解释为连续时间基于同步的数据同化方案，并与集合卡尔曼-布西滤波器进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究含对流非线性项的耗散偏微分方程在观测噪声下的主-从同步稳定性，为基于同步的数据同化方法提供理论保证，并揭示其与经典卡尔曼滤波类数据同化方法的结构性差异。

Method: 通过傅里叶截断将无穷维演化方程离散化为有限维复状态向量系统；利用Lyapunov方法建立确定性同步流形的局部指数稳定性；将观测噪声建模为Itô扩散，运用随机分析推导同步误差偏差的均方界和尾概率估计；在物理空间与傅里叶空间之间通过Parseval恒等式转换。

Result: 1. 建立了耦合强度条件下确定性同步流形的局部指数稳定性；2. 给出了随机同步误差与确定性参考误差之差的O(σ²)有限时间均方界及相应尾概率估计；3. 在全局单边耗散假设下获得了时间一致界。

Conclusion: 本文为含对流非线性的耗散演化方程在噪声环境下的同步稳定性提供了严格的数学分析框架，证明了基于同步的数据同化方案具有稳定的增益结构（稳定性导向），与集合卡尔曼-布西滤波器的自适应协方差增益形成本质区别。

Abstract: We investigate the stochastic stability of master--slave synchronization for a class of nonlinear dissipative evolution equations with a Burgers-type convective nonlinearity and a polynomial linear differential operator. The family includes the Burgers, Kuramoto--Sivashinsky, Kawahara, Benney--Lin, and Nikolaevskiy equations. Under periodic boundary conditions, each equation is represented through a finite-dimensional Fourier truncation, yielding a complex state vector coupled to a slave system driven by observations of the master. We first establish local exponential stability of the deterministic synchronization manifold under a simple condition on the coupling strength. We then introduce observational noise in the coupling signals, which transforms the slave system into an Itô diffusion and prevents exact synchronization. Our analysis focuses on the deviation $Δ(t)$ between the stochastic synchronization error and the exponentially stable deterministic reference error. We prove an $\mathcal{O}(σ^2)$ finite-time mean-square bound on $\|Δ(t)\|^2$, together with a corresponding tail-probability estimate. Under a global one-sided dissipativity assumption, the localization is removed and an $\mathcal{O}(σ^2)$ time-uniform bound is obtained. These estimates are derived in Fourier space and translated to physical space via Parseval's relation. Finally, we interpret the stochastic slave dynamics as a continuous-time synchronization-based data-assimilation scheme and compare its structure with the ensemble Kalman--Bucy filter, emphasizing the difference between prescribed stability-oriented and adaptive covariance-based gains.

</details>


### [164] [The de Sitter Scalar Discrete Series: Gupta-Bleuler Structure and Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17124)
*Maryam Bajalan,Jean-Pierre Gazeau,Hamed Pejhan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了 de Sitter 群 SO₀(1,4) 的标量离散级数幺正不可约表示 Π_{p,0} 可以在 dS 超曲面上实现为具有不定度规的 Krein 空间中的 Gupta-Bleuler 三重态结构，并通过体-边变换在共形边界上建立与之相互交织的边界实现，同时保持反射正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 de Sitter 时空中，量子场论面临无法定义全局正定真空态的困难，传统的 Wightman 公理体系不直接适用。作者希望为 dS 群离散级数幺正表示建立一种协变的 Krein 空间实现，并通过体-边界对应关系，为 dS 量子场论提供严格的数学基础。

Method: (1) 在 dS 超曲面上构造协变的 Krein 空间，赋予 dS 不变的 Klein-Gordon 不定内积；(2) 利用 Gupta-Bleuler 三重态结构描述群作用的不可分解性，正模、负模和零模分别对应不同子空间，物理商空间承载 UIR；(3) 通过 Fourier 型体-边界变换，将体理论映射到共形边界 ℐ⁺ 和 ℐ⁻ 上，建立边界实现并保持反射正性；(4) 利用对径对称性关联两个边界的实现。

Result: (1) 离散级数表示 Π_{p,0} 在体理论中可实现为 Krein 空间上的 Gupta-Bleuler 三重态，其中正模与负模来自 Krein 空间本身，零模出现在中间阶段，物理商空间承载 UIR；(2) 边界实现保留了体理论中的物理与规范结构，且仍保持不可分解性；(3) 体-边界变换提供了 Π_{p,0} 在体和边界实现之间的一一对应交织映射，并保持反射正性。

Conclusion: 本文为 dS 群离散级数表示建立了完整的 Krein 空间实现框架，澄清了体理论与共形边界之间的对应关系，为 de Sitter 时空中的量子场论（特别是非正定度规场景）提供了严格的群论基础。

Abstract: We show that scalar discrete-series unitary irreducible representations (UIRs) $Π_{p,0}$ ($p=1,2,\cdots$) of the de Sitter (dS) group $\mathrm{SO}_0(1,4)$ admit a dS-covariant Krein realization on the dS hyperboloid, endowed with a dS-invariant non-degenerate Klein-Gordon (KG) sesquilinear form, in which the group action is indecomposable and organizes naturally into a Gupta-Bleuler triplet. The positive- and negative-norm sectors are already present in the underlying Krein space, whereas a null sector emerges only at an intermediate stage, where the induced KG form becomes degenerate and its radical leads canonically to the physical quotient carrying the UIR $Π_{p,0}$. We further show that suitable limits of the bulk theory at the ``future'' and ``past'' conformal boundaries ${\mathcal{I}}^\pm$ give rise to dS-invariant boundary realizations endowed with induced kernel inner products. While the bulk negative-norm sector admits no independent boundary counterpart, the boundary realization retains the physical and gauge structures inherited from the bulk. The resulting boundary module nevertheless remains indecomposable, with its physical quotient carrying the discrete-series representation $Π_{p,0}$. The antipodal symmetry provides a natural relation between the realizations on ${\mathcal{I}}^+$ and ${\mathcal{I}}^-$, ensuring the consistency of the boundary construction and its geometric interpretation. At the heart of the analysis lies a Fourier-type bulk-boundary transform that provides a dS-covariant identification of the bulk and boundary physical sectors, establishing a one-to-one intertwining correspondence between the bulk and boundary realizations of $Π_{p,0}$ while preserving reflection positivity.

</details>


### [165] [Moving Collinear Cracks in a Prestressed Dry Sandy Medium Fracture Response under Traveling Punch Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17155)
*Diksha,Soniya Chaudhary,Pawan Kumar Sharma*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了初始应力干砂介质中两个共线Griffith裂纹在集中裂纹面载荷和移动冲头压力作用下的动态断裂行为，通过解析方法推导了裂纹密度函数、应力强度因子和裂纹张开位移的闭式表达式，揭示了裂纹速度、几何参数、初始应力、砂性及移动载荷对裂纹尖端增强和张开的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对交通运输基础设施、岩土系统、地下开挖等工程结构中干砂介质在移动载荷作用下的断裂评估需求，建立考虑初始应力和砂性效应的动态断裂分析框架。

Method: 采用移动坐标系将瞬态问题转化为稳态问题，利用Fourier积分变换推导特征方程和牵引位移关系，将问题简化为耦合Cauchy型奇异积分方程，对足够厚条带进行渐近核简化后通过有限Hilbert变换解析求解。

Result: 获得了裂纹密度函数、I型应力强度因子（内外裂纹尖端）和裂纹张开位移的闭式表达式，并通过退化为多种极限情况验证了分析的一致性。

Conclusion: 裂纹速度、几何参数、初始应力、砂性以及移动冲头加载共同显著影响裂纹尖端的应力强度因子和裂纹张开位移，所提解析框架为干砂介质中移动载荷下的断裂评估和工程设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The dynamic fracture behaviour of two moving collinear Griffith cracks in an initially stressed dry sandy medium subjected to concentrated crack-face loading and moving punch pressure is investigated. A moving coordinate system transforms the transient problem into a steady state formulation, while the effects of initial stress and sandiness are incorporated into the governing equations. Fourier integral transforms are employed to obtain the characteristic equation and the transformed traction displacement relations. The crack face, symmetry, and outer surface conditions reduce the problem to coupled Cauchy type singular integral equations. For a sufficiently thick strip, an asymptotic kernel reduction is developed, and the resulting equations are solved analytically using the finite Hilbert transform. Closed form expressions are derived for the crack density functions, Mode I stress intensity factors at the inner and outer crack tips, and the crack opening displacement. Several limiting cases are recovered from the general formulation, providing analytical consistency checks. The results demonstrate the combined influence of crack speed, crack geometry, initial stress, sandiness, and moving punch loading on crack tip intensification and crack opening. The proposed analytical framework provides useful insights for fracture assessment and the design of transportation infrastructure, geotechnical systems, underground excavations, and other engineering structures involving dry sandy media subjected to moving loads.

</details>


### [166] [Fouling maps and polynomial first integrals from symmetric tensor fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17407)
*Rafael Azuaje,Juan Carlos Marrero,Edith Padrón*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在力学系统余切丛的哈密顿力学框架下，引入"fouling映射"作为保持位形坐标的规范变换的非可逆推广，发展了构造多项式fouling映射的张量方法，并证明这类映射可诱导哈密顿流下的不变(1,1)-张量场，其幂的迹构成多项式运动常数。对于半黎曼位形空间上的力学哈密顿函数，完整刻画了由对称张量场诱导的多项式丛映射并给出fouling性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将保持位形坐标的规范变换（fouling变换）推广到非可逆情形，建立fouling映射的概念，并发展系统化方法构造多项式运动常数。

Method: 采用张量方法构造多项式fouling映射，研究其在哈密顿流下诱导的不变(1,1)-张量场，通过张量幂的迹获得多项式守恒量；在力学哈密顿函数（半黎曼位形空间上的动能加势能）情形下，分析对称张量场诱导的丛映射并推导fouling性的充分必要条件。

Result: 建立了多项式fouling映射的构造框架，证明了其诱导的不变张量场性质，给出了半黎曼位形空间上力学系统的fouling映射完整刻画，并通过欧氏平面和二维球面上的实例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为构造哈密顿力学系统的多项式守恒量提供了新的张量方法框架，推广了经典规范变换理论至非可逆情形。

Abstract: Under the framework of time-independent Hamiltonian mechanics on the cotangent bundles $T^*Q$ of the configuration spaces $Q$ of mechanical systems, we introduce the concept of fouling map as a non-invertible generalization of the so-called fouling transformations --canonoid transformations preserving configuration coordinates--. We develop a tensorial method for constructing polynomial fouling maps. We show that each such map induces a $(1,1)$-tensor field invariant under the Hamiltonian flow, whose traces of its powers are polynomial constants of motion. For mechanical Hamiltonian functions --the kinetic energy plus the potential energy on a semi-Riemannian configuration space $(Q,g)$--, we completely characterize polynomial bundle maps arising from symmetric $(k+1,0)$-tensor fields and derive the conditions ensuring their fouling nature. Several explicit examples on the Euclidean plane and on the 2-sphere illustrate the method.

</details>


### [167] [Baxter Q-operators from a Schwinger-Boson construction of the master T-operator and the mKP hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17642)
*Zengo Tsuboi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为有理非齐次 gl(M) 自旋链的主 T-算符提供了一种替代定义：通过 Schwinger 玻色子在辅助 Fock 空间上实现 gl(M)，构造 L-算符和单项矩阵，并取迹得到主 T-算符。该定义统一了两种 Baxter Q-算符构造方法——一种来自主 T-算符在 Miwa 变量处的留数，另一种来自退化 Yangian L-算符的振子形式。通过缩并极限和迹操作，两种构造等价，并给出了 Q-算符的显式留数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为有理非齐次 gl(M) 自旋链的主 T-算符提供一种基于 Schwinger 玻色子辅助 Fock 空间的替代定义，并统一两种已有的 Baxter Q-算符构造方法。

Method: 利用 Schwinger 玻色子在辅助 Fock 空间上实现 gl(M)，构造 L-算符和单项矩阵，通过对 Fock 空间取迹定义主 T-算符；通过 Howe 对偶性重现 Schur 函数展开；分析 Miwa 变量趋于边界扭曲本征值倒数时迹的极点行为，缩并 L-算符得到退化 Yangian L-算符，进而给出 Q-算符的留数公式。

Result: 基于 Schwinger 玻色子的主 T-算符定义与传统的 Schur 函数展开定义等价；两种 Baxter Q-算符构造（基于主 T-算符留数和基于退化 Yangian L-算符的振子形式）被统一；得到了 Q-算符的显式留数公式，以及一种 Holstein-Primakoff 型大占据数缩并等价方案。

Conclusion: 本文给出的主 T-算符替代定义不仅重现了已知的 Schur 函数展开和 Cherednik-Bazhanov-Reshetikhin 行列式公式，还统一了两种 Baxter Q-算符的构造方法，为自旋链可积性理论提供了更简洁的框架。

Abstract: The master T-operator is a generating function for transfer matrices and a tau-function of the modified KP (mKP) hierarchy. It is conventionally introduced through a Schur-function expansion whose coefficients are fused transfer matrices satisfying the Cherednik--Bazhanov--Reshetikhin determinant formula. For rational inhomogeneous gl(M) spin chains, we give an alternative definition: we realize gl(M) on an auxiliary Fock space generated by finitely many families of Schwinger bosons, form a monodromy matrix from the resulting L-operator, and define the master T-operator as its trace over the Fock space. Howe duality then reproduces the Schur-function expansion. This realization relates two constructions of Baxter Q-operators. In the construction of [arXiv:1112.3310] (see also [arXiv:1010.4022]), the Q-operators are obtained from residues of the master T-operator with respect to selected Miwa variables. In the construction of [arXiv:1010.3699], they are defined using degenerate Yangian L-operators in oscillator form. When selected Miwa variables approach the inverses of boundary-twist eigenvalues, the defining trace develops poles. After a suitable rescaling of this L-operator, only the terms contributing to the highest-order pole survive in the normalized limit; before the trace is taken, they form the degenerate Yangian L-operator of the second construction. The trace then gives an explicit residue formula for the corresponding Q-operator. Independently, a Holstein--Primakoff-type large-occupation-number contraction of this L-operator on subspaces with fixed total occupation numbers yields the same degenerate Yangian L-operator.

</details>


### [168] [Universal Correlators on Exponentially Ramified Spectral Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17711)
*Mohamad Alameddine,Alexander Hock*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧致谱曲线上具有指数型（及更一般本质奇点）作为分支点的广义拓扑递推，通过整体公式的轮廓变形将本质奇点贡献替换为亚纯点留数，建立了适用于指数型分支谱曲线的递归框架，并证明其可视为Bouchard-Eynard高阶拓扑递推中分支点阶数趋于无穷的收敛极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广义拓扑递推主要针对亚纯分支点，而实际物理和数学问题中常出现指数型乃至本质奇点（如超越函数、Mirzakhani曲线的x-y对偶），缺乏系统的递归处理框架。

Method: 利用广义拓扑递推的整体表述，对递归留数公式进行轮廓变形，将本质奇点处的贡献转化为亚纯点处的留数求和，从而在广义拓扑递推框架内自然地处理指数型分支点。

Result: 建立了适用于本质奇点分支曲线的递归框架；证明了该框架可视为Bouchard-Eynard高阶拓扑递推中分支点阶数趋于无穷的收敛极限（dy保持正则非零）；通过超越函数和Mirzakhani曲线x-y对偶等实例验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将广义拓扑递推的适用范围从亚纯分支点拓展到指数型/本质奇点分支点，为更广泛的谱曲线（如超越函数）提供了统一的递归工具，并揭示了与高阶拓扑递推的深层联系。

Abstract: We investigate generalized topological recursion on compact spectral curves admitting exponential, and more generally essential, singularities as ramification points. Exploiting the global formulation of generalized topological recursion, we establish a contour deformation of the recursive residue formula that replaces contributions from these essential singularities by residues at meromorphic points. This provides a natural recursive framework for exponentially ramified spectral curves while remaining entirely within the generalized topological recursion formalism. Our formalism can also be viewed as a limiting case of the Bouchard-Eynard higher-order topological recursion, obtained when the order of a ramification point tends to infinity in a convergent manner, as occurs, for example, for exponential singularities while $dy$ remains regular and non-vanishing. We further illustrate the resulting formalism through several examples, including transcendental functions and the $x$-$y$ dual of the Mirzakhani curve.

</details>


### [169] [On the use of equilibrium models to describe dynamic adsorption regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17941)
*M. Calvo-Schwarzwalder,A. Valverde,A. Cuesta López,A. Cabrera-Codony,U. Thorat,T. G. Myers*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了耦合伪一级动力学与Sips等温线的柱吸附模型，通过行波近似推导解析解，发现PFO-Sips模型预测突变性穿透曲线而非平滑增长。多组实验验证表明PFO模型始终逊于纯Sips模型，且存在根本性结构缺陷，尽管近百年来被广泛使用，实则不适用于描述柱吸附动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示伪一级（PFO）动力学模型在柱吸附动力学描述中的根本性缺陷，挑战其近百年来在文献中的广泛使用。

Method: 将PFO动力学与Sips等温线耦合构建柱吸附模型；采用行波近似（traveling wave approximation）推导特定操作条件下的解析解；利用多组实验数据集验证模型性能并与纯Sips模型对比。

Result: PFO-Sips模型预测出突变性、非连续的穿透曲线，与纯Sips模型的平滑连续增长显著不同；在所有实验验证中，PFO模型表现均劣于Sips模型；验证过程暴露了PFO框架内部的不一致性。

Conclusion: 伪一级（PFO）模型存在根本性结构缺陷，本质上不适用于描述柱吸附动力学，尽管其已在文献中被广泛使用了近一个世纪。

Abstract: We present a column adsorption model that couples a Pseudo-First-Order (PFO) kinetic formulation with the Sips isotherm framework. Using a traveling wave approximation, we derive analytical solutions for specific operating conditions. Qualitatively, these solutions deviate significantly from their pure Sips counterparts: instead of a smooth, continuous increase in concentration at the column outlet, the PFO-Sips model predicts an abrupt, sudden breakthrough. We validate these analytical solutions against diverse experimental datasets from the literature. The results reveal that the PFO-based model consistently underperforms compared to the original Sips formulation. Furthermore, this validation exposes fundamental inconsistencies within the PFO framework. We demonstrate that despite its widespread use in the literature for almost a century, the PFO model is inherently flawed and structurally unfit for describing column adsorption dynamics.

</details>


### [170] [$(1,k)$ CFT and RH problem with the $c=-2$ case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18120)
*Mikhail Bershtein,Andrei Grigorev,Anton Shchechkin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用(1,k) Virasoro模型的共形块构造（修正）Riemann-Hilbert问题的解。对于k>1情况，解不唯一且共形块空间维数增加。重点研究k=2（中心荷c=-2，辛费米子）情形，显式构造3个穿刺点下的解并证明唯一性，同时得到c=-2 tau函数的新双线性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Iorgov-Lisovyy-Teschner的方法推广到k>1情形，解决因穿刺点处更奇异行为导致的Riemann-Hilbert问题解的非唯一性问题，并在CFT侧解释共形块空间维数增加的现象。

Method: 采用(1,k) Virasoro模型的共形块来构造（修正）Riemann-Hilbert问题的解；针对k=2情况（中心荷c=-2，对应辛费米子理论），显式构造3个穿刺点下的解，并通过适当的初始数据条件证明解的唯一性。

Result: 在k=2、3个穿刺点情形下成功构造了修正Riemann-Hilbert问题的显式解，并在合适的初始数据条件下证明了其唯一性；同时得到了c=-2 tau函数的新双线性关系。

Conclusion: 共形块方法可推广至k>1的Virasoro模型，尽管解的唯一性需额外的初始条件来保证；c=-2情形下的tau函数存在新的双线性关系，这为辛费米子模型及更一般的(1,k)模型的研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: Following approach of Iorgov--Lisovyy--Teschner, we construct solutions of the (modified) Riemann--Hilbert problem using conformal blocks of $(1,k)$ Virasoro models. For $k>1$ case, the solution of this Riemann--Hilbert problem is not unique due to more singular behavior at punctures. On the CFT side the dimension of the space of conformal blocks also increases. We specifically study the $k=2$ case, which corresponds to the central charge $c=-2$ and symplectic fermions. We explicitly construct a corresponding solution of the modified Riemann--Hilbert problem in the case of 3 punctures and prove its uniqueness under suitable initial data conditions. We also obtain new bilinear relations for $c=-2$ tau functions.

</details>


### [171] [Entropy production in Knudsen thermodynamics of compartmented systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18202)
*Timothy Chumley,Renato Feres,Zhenyi Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究了一类随机动力系统中的熵产生与非平衡输运，该模型描述Knudsen气体在分隔容器中的运动。通过信息论方法将熵产生率表达为前向与时间反演路径测度之间的相对熵，在互易假设下简化为经典热力学公式（随机Clausius关系），并发展了一套模块化分析框架，通过广义Maxwell-Smoluchowski散射算子在多室系统中得到显式解析解，揭示热棘轮效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立随机动力系统中熵产生的严格信息论基础，并将其与经典热力学公式联系起来，同时发展一套模块化分析方法以处理复杂多室系统中的非平衡输运问题。

Method: 将熵产生率定义为前向与时间反演路径测度的相对熵；在互易假设下引入温度，推导随机Clausius关系；发展模块化分析框架，通过室散射算子和逗留统计刻画每个开放室，利用更新-报酬定理将各室贡献组合为全局熵产生率。

Result: 在互易假设下信息论熵产生率简化为经典热力学公式（平均能量传递除以局部温度）。对完全热适应的循环三室系统（两个不同温度热壁加一个势垒），得到了熵产生率和净概率循环的闭式表达式，揭示了类似于热流逸的热棘轮效应。

Conclusion: 所提出的模块化框架可有效分析复杂多室系统非平衡输运，广义Maxwell-Smoluchowski散射算子家族提供了可显式解析处理的模型，热棘轮效应对应于热流逸现象。

Abstract: We investigate entropy production and nonequilibrium transport in a class of random dynamical systems modeling a Knudsen gas confined to a compartmented container. The system consists of a single particle undergoing random billiard motion, with collisions leading to random reflection or transmission through semi-reflecting, compartment-separating walls.
  The stationary entropy production rate is first expressed as the relative entropy between forward and time-reversed path measures. Under a reciprocity assumption, used to introduce temperature into the general random billiard system, it is shown how this information-theoretic definition reduces to the classical thermodynamic formula: the mean energy transferred to the walls divided by their local temperatures, a stochastic Clausius relation. We then develop a modular analysis of compartmented systems. Each open compartment is characterized by a compartment scattering operator and its sojourn statistics. The sequence of compartment entrance states defines a Markov chain whose stationary distribution is used in a renewal-reward theorem to assemble the compartment contributions into the global entropy production rate.
  The framework is illustrated with a series of examples of increasing complexity governed by a generalized Maxwell-Smoluchowski scattering operator and amenable to detailed and explicit analysis. Such operators are defined by a few parameters: temperature, a partial thermal accommodation, the height of potential barriers, and a porosity coefficient. The central example is a cyclic three-compartment system consisting of two thermal walls at different temperatures and a potential barrier. For full thermal accommodation we obtain closed-form expressions for the entropy production rate and net probability circulation around the cycle, revealing a thermal ratchet effect analogous to thermal transpiration.

</details>
