# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [An integral inequality for compact Bach-flat $\mathcal{A}_{2}$-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16552)
*Fábio Reis dos Santos,Elisa Joaquim Santos*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 论文建立了闭Bach-平坦$\mathcal{A}_2$-流形（具有非负数量曲率和常数非负第二Schouten曲率的黎曼流形）的Catino型积分不等式，并在等号情形下证明了刚性结果：流形要么是Einstein的，要么等距覆盖于带乘积度量的$\mathbb{S}^{1}\times\mathbb{S}^{n-1}(κ)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究闭Bach-平坦$\mathcal{A}_2$-流形的几何性质，通过积分不等式建立曲率条件与流形分类之间的联系，推广Catino的相关结果。

Method: 采用几何分析中的积分不等式方法，结合Bach平坦条件和第二Schouten曲率为常数的约束，推导Catino型积分不等式；通过分析等号成立条件进行分类讨论，得到刚性分类。

Result: 建立了针对闭Bach-平坦$\mathcal{A}_2$-流形的Catino型积分不等式；在等号情形下，流形要么是Einstein流形，要么等距覆盖于$\mathbb{S}^{1}\times\mathbb{S}^{n-1}(κ)$（带乘积度量）。

Conclusion: 该积分不等式及其刚性分类结果刻画了Bach-平坦$\mathcal{A}_2$-流形在给定曲率条件下的几何结构，丰富了对这类特殊黎曼流形的认识。

Abstract: We establish a Catino-type integral inequality for closed Bach-flat $\mathcal{A}_2$-manifolds, namely Riemannian manifolds with nonnegative scalar curvature and constant nonnegative second Schouten curvature. In the equality case, we derive rigidity results showing that the manifold is either Einstein or isometrically covered by $\mathbb{S}^{1}\times\mathbb{S}^{n-1}(κ)$ endowed with the product metric.

</details>


### [173] [The Fried Conjecture for Morse-Smale Flows: A Survey on Ray-Singer and Milnor Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16719)
*Giovanni Molinari*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文综述了紧致黎曼流形上Morse-Smale流的广义Fried猜想，通过构建扭曲de Rham复形与Hodge分解定义Ray-Singer挠率，引入Ruelle Zeta函数与Thom-Smale复形刻画动力学数据，最终证明Ray-Singer度量与Milnor度量相等，将解析挠率识别为Thom-Smale组合挠率与Ruelle Zeta函数在零点值的乘积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立动力系统（Morse-Smale流）与解析几何（Ray-Singer挠率）之间的深层联系，并证明广义Fried猜想——即解析挠率与动力系统Zeta函数及组合挠率之间的精确相等关系。

Method: 1) 构造扭曲de Rham复形并推导Hodge分解；2) 定义Ray-Singer挠率（解析侧）；3) 刻画Morse-Smale向量场并引入Ruelle Zeta函数编码闭轨道谱数据；4) 构造Thom-Smale复形纳入不动点贡献；5) 定义Milnor度量；6) 证明Ray-Singer度量与Milnor度量相等。

Result: 证明了Ray-Singer度量与Milnor度量相等，即解析挠率等于Thom-Smale组合挠率乘以Ruelle Zeta函数在零点处的值。

Conclusion: 广义Fried猜想成立：动力系统的谱信息（Ruelle Zeta）与不动点信息（Thom-Smale复形）共同决定了解析挠率，实现了分析与动力系统的统一。

Abstract: This paper reviews the generalised Fried conjecture for Morse-Smale flows on compact Riemannian manifolds. We first establish the proper framework by constructing the twisted de Rham complex and deriving the Hodge decomposition, which underpins the definition of the Ray-Singer torsion. On the dynamical side, we characterise Morse-Smale vector fields, introducing the Ruelle Zeta function to encode the spectral data of closed orbits and constructing the Thom-Smale complex to include the contribution of fixed points. These invariants are synthesised into the definition of the Milnor metric on the determinant line of the twisted cohomology. Finally, we present the theorem proving the conjecture: the Ray-Singer metric coincides with the Milnor metric, identifying the analytic torsion with the product of the Thom-Smale combinatorial torsion and the value at zero of the Ruelle Zeta function.

</details>


### [174] [Torsion and curvature of ACYT and AHKT 8-manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16752)
*Stefan Ivanov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究8维近复Hermite流形上具有全斜对称Nijenhuis张量（相对于唯一近Hermite联络）时的性质，证明了如果三个Ricci张量消失则挠率3-形式闭合；进而推导出复8流形上Strominger-Bismut联络的三个Ricci张量消失则挠率闭合（即空间为pluriclosed）。还证明了紧致ACYT 8流形在挠率联络下具有平行Nijenhuis张量时，挠率闭合当且仅当挠率外微分的迹为零。紧致ACYT 8流形在挠率联络下具有平行Nijenhuis张量时，恰好当挠率3-形式同时平行于挠率联络和Levi-Civita联络时成为Ricci平坦SU(4)瞬子。此外定义了近超Kähler 8流形上保持Sp(2)结构且具有全斜对称挠率的线性联络（AHKT流形），给出了成为AHKT的精确条件，并证明AHKT 8流形恰好在三个Lee形式相等时成为HKT流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究8维近复Hermite流形上具有全斜对称Nijenhuis张量（相对于唯一近Hermite联络）时的几何性质，特别是在挠率联络下平行Nijenhuis张量对挠率闭合条件和Ricci平坦SU(4)瞬子结构的刻画。同时推广到近超Kähler 8流形上具有全斜对称挠率的Sp(2)结构联络（AHKT流形）并探讨其与HKT流形的关系。

Method: 采用微分几何和张量分析的方法，利用近Hermite流形上唯一带有挠率3-形式的近Hermite联络的Ricci张量条件、Nijenhuis张量的平行性以及挠率3-形式的闭合性之间的关系进行推导。对于AHKT流形，通过分析近超Kähler流形上保持Sp(2)结构且具有全斜对称挠率的线性联络的条件来刻画。

Result: 1) 紧致近Hermite 8流形上若全斜对称Nijenhuis张量平行于唯一近Hermite联络且三个Ricci张量为零，则挠率3-形式闭合。2) 紧致复8流形上Strominger-Bismut联络的三个Ricci张量为零时挠率闭合（pluriclosed）。3) 紧致ACYT 8流形上挠率联络下Nijenhuis张量平行时，挠率闭合当且仅当挠率外微分的迹为零。4) 紧致ACYT 8流形上挠率联络下Nijenhuis张量平行时，其成为Ricci平坦SU(4)瞬子当且仅当挠率3-形式同时平行于挠率联络和Levi-Civita联络。5) AHKT 8流形恰好在三个Lee形式相等时成为HKT流形。

Conclusion: 本文建立了8维近复Hermite流形上挠率联络的Ricci曲率条件与挠率闭合性之间的关联，给出了ACYT 8流形成为Ricci平坦SU(4)瞬子的等价刻画，并揭示了AHKT流形与HKT流形之间的精确关系。

Abstract: We observe that on a compact almost Hermitian 8 manifold with totally skew-symmetric Nijenhuis tensor parallel with respect to the unique almost hermitian connection with torsion three form if the three Ricci tensors of this connection vanish then the torsion 3-form is closed. Consequently, on a compact complex 8-manifold if the three Ricci tensors of the Strominger-Bismut connection vanish then its torsion is closed, i.e. the space is pluriclosed. We also deduce that a compact ACYT 8-manifold with paralle Nijenhuis tensor with respect to the torsion connection has closed torsion if and only if the trace of the exterior derivative of the torsion vanishes. It is shown that a compact ACYT 8-manifold wich Nijenhuis tensor is parallel with respect to the torsion connection is a Ricci flat $SU(4)$ instanton exactly when the torsion 3-form is parallel with respect to the torsion and to the Levi-Civita connections simultaneously.
  We consider an almost hyperhermitian 8-manifold with an $Sp(2)$ structure admitting linear connection preserving the $Sp(2)$ structure and having totally skew symmetric torsion and call it AHKT manifold. The exact conditions an almost hyperhermitian 8-manifold to be an AHKT are presented and it is shown that an AHKT 8-manifold is HKT exactly when the three Lee forms coincide

</details>


### [175] [Sub-Finslerian Interpolation Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16817)
*Haowei Lin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明前向理想子Finsler流形支持最优传输的插值不等式，将Barilari和Rizzi的结果从子Riemannian推广到子Finslerian情形。通过引入子Finslerian Jacobi场和建立最优传输理论，结合Jacobian估计刻画广义扭曲系数，推导出Brunn-Minkowski和Borell-Brascamp-Lieb等几何不等式，并证明Randers子Finslerian Heisenberg群满足测度收缩性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将子Riemannian流形上最优传输的插值不等式推广到更一般的子Finslerian流形，建立子Finslerian几何中的传输不等式和几何不等式理论。

Method: 引入子Finslerian Jacobi场，建立子Finslerian流形上的最优传输理论；结合子Finslerian Jacobian估计来刻画广义扭曲系数。

Result: 推导出Brunn-Minkowski不等式和Borell-Brascamp-Lieb不等式等基本几何不等式；证明Randers子Finslerian Heisenberg群满足测度收缩性质。

Conclusion: 成功将子Riemannian几何中的最优传输理论及相关几何不等式推广到子Finslerian框架，并通过具体实例验证了理论的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that forward ideal sub-Finslerian manifolds support interpolation inequalities for optimal transport, extending the results of Barilari and Rizzi, arXiv:1705.05380, from the sub-Riemannian to the sub-Finslerian setting. A key role is played by the introduction of sub-Finslerian Jacobi fields and the establishment of optimal transport theory on sub-Finslerian manifolds. By combining this transport framework with sub-Finslerian Jacobian estimates, we characterize the generalized distortion coefficients. As an application, we deduce several fundamental geometric inequalities, including the Brunn-Minkowski and Borell-Brascamp-Lieb inequalities. Finally, for the case of the Randers sub-Finslerian Heisenberg group, whose metric is defined by a sub-Riemannian metric perturbed by a drift term, we explicitly show that it satisfies the measure contraction property.

</details>


### [176] [Homogeneous and flow-invariant geometry on the unit tangent bundle of hyperbolic space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16818)
*Daniel Koama,Léonard Todjihoundé*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在黎曼流形的单位切丛上构造了Sasaki度量，将实双曲空间的单位切丛实现为齐性空间，并利用Hopf坐标和Busemann函数构造了测地流不变的Riemann度量$g_{Hopf}$，同时识别了与Busemann函数相关的自然叶状结构的全测地叶（水平柱面），并给出了具有挠率的显式度量联络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建双曲空间单位切丛上的几何结构，特别是构造测地流不变的度量，并研究其叶状结构和联络性质。

Method: 利用Sasaki度量构造、齐性空间描述、Hopf坐标与Busemann函数相结合的方法。

Result: 构造了$U\mathbb{H}^n$上的$G$-不变度量及测地流不变的Riemann度量$g_{Hopf}$，并刻画了水平柱面为全测地子流形。

Conclusion: 给出了双曲空间单位切丛上具有挠率的显式度量联络，揭示了Busemann函数诱导的自然叶状结构。

Abstract: We construct the Sasaki metric on the unit tangent bundle $U_g M$ of a Riemannian manifold $(M , g)$ and describe the unit tangent bundle $U\mathbb{H}^n$ of real hyperbolic space as a homogeneous space, both under $SO_0 (1, n)$ and under the larger group $SO_0 (1, n) \times SO_0 (1, 1)$, yielding explicit of $G$-invariant metrics. Using Hopf coordinates and Busemann functions, we then construct a Riemannian metric $g_{Hopf}$ on $U\mathbb{H}^n$ that is invariant under the geodesic flow, and we identify the horospherical cylinders as totally geodesic leaves of a natural foliation associated to a Busemann function, with respect to an explicit metric connection with torsion.

</details>


### [177] [$m$-Positive Stability of Holomorphic Vector Bundles and Moduli Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17203)
*Dan Popovici*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出了高秩向量丛的m-正性概念（当m=1时退化为经典Griffiths正性），并基于此推广了经典Mumford-Takemoto稳定性理论，通过为每个真凝聚子层关联一个光滑函数，将稳定性条件转化为相对逐点m-正性性质。建立了与Hermite-Einstein几何的联系，证明了Hermite-Einstein丛是一致半稳定的，研究了模空间，并与经典稳定性概念进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广经典Mumford-Takemoto稳定性理论，使其更依赖于全纯结构和Hermite纤维度量而非光滑结构的数值不变量，同时引入m-正性概念作为高阶向量丛的正性条件。

Method: 提出了高秩向量丛的m-正性概念（m=1时退化为Griffiths正性）；通过为每个真凝聚子层关联一个光滑函数来定义新的稳定性条件，将稳定性转化为相对逐点m-正性性质；建立与Hermite-Einstein几何的联系。

Result: Hermite-Einstein丛是一致半稳定的；研究了新的模空间；与经典Mumford-Takemoto稳定性概念进行了比较。

Conclusion: 新稳定性条件将重点放在全纯结构和Hermite纤维度量上，而非光滑结构的数值不变量，为向量丛稳定性理论提供了新的几何视角，并与Hermite-Einstein几何建立了联系。

Abstract: We first propose a notion of $m$-positivity for higher-rank vector bundles, a variant of which reduces to the classical Griffiths positivity when $m=1$. Based on this, we go on to propose a generalisation of the classical Mumford-Takemoto theory of stability by means of a smooth function that we associate with every proper coherent subsheaf ${\cal F}$ of a given holomorphic vector bundle $E$. This places the emphasis on the holomorphic structure and the Hermitian fibre metric of $E$, rather than on numerical invariants of the smooth structure of $E$, making our stability conditions into relative pointwise $m$-positivity properties of $E$ with respect to its proper coherent subsheaves ${\cal F}$. We establish links with Hermite-Einstein geometry, prove that Hermite-Einstein bundles are uniformly semi-stable, study the resulting moduli spaces, and compare the new notions with the classical Mumford-Takemoto (semi-)stability notions.

</details>


### [178] [On non-positive Weyl connection on Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17210)
*Tomasz Dudek,Aleksy Tralle*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在完全可解三维李群中，只有SOL群存在非正不变Weyl联络，验证了Wojtkowski猜想，并推测该方法可推广到一般完全可解李群情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Wojtkowski提出的关于非正不变Weyl联络在完全可解李群上存在性的猜想。

Method: 通过对三维完全可解李群进行分类分析，逐一检验各类群是否存在非正不变Weyl联络，并特别关注SOL群的特殊性质。

Result: 在完全可解三维李群中，仅有SOL群允许非正不变Weyl联络，其余群均不满足条件。

Conclusion: 确认了Wojtkowski猜想在三维情形下的正确性，并推测该分类方法可推广至任意维完全可解李群。

Abstract: The paper contributes to the problem of finding non-positive invariant Weyl connections on Lie groups. The main result is that in the case of completely solvable 3-dimensional Lie groups only the group SOL admits such connections, confirming the conjecture of Wojtkowski. We conjecture that our method can be extended to the general case of completely solvable Lie groups.

</details>


### [179] [Rigidity of positive mass theorem with fast metric decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17236)
*Jianchun Chu,Man-Chun Lee,Jingbo Wan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明在维数≥4的欧氏空间中，若度量具有非负标量曲率且衰减速度快于Schwarzschild度量，则该度量必然是平坦的。这补充了Mazurowski-Yao和You-Zhang的工作，从而在所有维度上建立了关于快速度量衰减下正质量定理刚性（即Gromov猜想）的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Gromov关于正质量定理在快速度量衰减下刚性的猜想在所有维度上的完整证明。

Method: 分析欧氏空间中具有非负标量曲率和快速衰减（速度快于Schwarzschild度量）的度量，在维度四及以上情形下证明其必须是平坦的。

Result: 在维数≥4时，非负标量曲率且衰减快于Schwarzschild度量的欧氏空间度量必然是平坦的（即无质量）。

Conclusion: 补充了已有工作，在所有维度上完整建立了Gromov关于正质量定理在快速衰减下的刚性猜想。

Abstract: In this work, we consider metrics on Euclidean space with nonnegative scalar curvature and rapid decay at infinity. We show that, in dimensions four and higher, any such metric is necessarily flat if its decay rate exceeds that of the Schwarzschild metric. This complements recent works by Mazurowski-Yao and You-Zhang, thereby establishing Gromov's conjecture on the rigidity of the positive mass theorem under fast metric decay in all dimensions.

</details>


### [180] [Singly periodic minimal Wente torus with ends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17379)
*Carlos Andrés Toro Cardona*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了由菱形格参数化的单周期极小曲面，这些曲面被球曲率线叶状化，并由此发现了新的自由边界和毛细极小环带，其中一些是嵌入的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造由菱形格参数化、被球曲率线叶状化的单周期极小曲面，并探索其在自由边界和毛细极小环带中的应用。

Method: 利用菱形格参数化构造单周期极小曲面，并研究其球曲率线叶状结构，进而分析自由边界和毛细极小环带的存在性。

Result: 发现了新的自由边界和毛细极小环带实例，连接不同中心但相同半径的两个球体，其中一些是嵌入的。

Conclusion: 成功构造了具有特定几何结构的单周期极小曲面，并找到了新的自由边界和毛细极小环带例子。

Abstract: We construct singly periodic minimal surfaces in $\mathbb{R}^3$ parametrized by rhombic lattices and foliated by spherical curvature lines. As an application we find new examples of free boundary and capillary minimal annuli into two spheres of different center and same radius, some of them embedded.

</details>


### [181] [The deformed Vortex equations and equivariant stability conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17459)
*Tristan C. Collins,Yukai Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究X×ℙ¹上SU(2)-等变全纯向量丛的高秩变形Hermitian-Yang-Mills方程，对于涡旋型向量丛，证明了方程解的存在性与Z-稳定性等价，并表明Z-稳定性由Bridgeland稳定性蕴含（猜想等价）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立高秩dHYM方程解的存在性与代数几何稳定性条件之间的等价关系，并探索其与Bridgeland稳定性的联系。

Method: 将问题限制在X×ℙ¹上的SU(2)-等变全纯向量丛，聚焦涡旋型向量丛，通过Z-稳定性刻画解的存在性。

Result: 证明了对涡旋型向量丛，高秩dHYM方程可解当且仅当其满足Z-稳定性；且Z-稳定性由Bridgeland稳定性蕴含。

Conclusion: 高秩dHYM方程的解析问题与Bridgeland稳定性理论之间存在深刻联系，Z-稳定性可能是连接两者的关键桥梁。

Abstract: We study the higher rank deformed Hermitian-Yang-Mills (dHYM) equations for $SU(2)$-equivariant holomorphic vector bundles over $X\times \mathbb{P}^1$ for $X$ a compact Riemann surface. For a class of vector bundles of vortex type, we establish the equivalence between existence of solutions to higher rank dHYM equations and an appropriate notion of algebro-geometric stability, called $Z$-stability. We show that $Z$-stability is implied by, and conjecturally equivalent to, Bridgeland stability in $D^{b}{\rm Coh}^{SU(2)}(X\times \mathbb{P}^1)$.

</details>


### [182] [On the structure of $2$-step nilpotent Lorentzian naturally reductive Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18017)
*Brian Luporini,Silvio Reggiani,Francisco Vittone*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在换位子理想非退化条件下，具有自然约化左不变洛伦兹度量的2步幂零李群的结构，将其从标准非退化中心假设推广到更一般情形，并完整描述了退化中心情况下的李代数构造、表示分解及各向同性代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然约化2步幂零李群上洛伦兹度量的研究从标准非退化中心假设推广到更弱的换位子理想非退化条件，从而统一处理非退化和退化中心两种情形。

Method: 以表示群 N ⋊ H^aut 为框架，放宽非退化中心条件至换位子理想非退化，建立洛伦兹情形的扩展构造；对退化中心情形，证明其李代数为半直积的中心扩张，且黎曼因子是自然约化的；通过表示的不变分解（含洛伦兹因子）和各向同性代数及等距自同构群单位连通分支的显式描述完成结构刻画。

Result: 建立了换位子理想非退化条件下2步洛伦兹幂零李群自然约化结构的完整描述，涵盖非退化和退化中心情形，给出了李代数结构、表示分解及各向同性代数的显式刻画。

Conclusion: 在换位子理想非退化这一更弱条件下，完成了自然约化2步洛伦兹幂零李群的结构描述，为后续研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study $2$-step nilpotent Lie groups with naturally reductive left-invariant Lorentzian metrics with respect to the presentation group $N \rtimes H^{\operatorname{aut}}$. Replacing the standard non-degenerate center assumption with the weaker condition that the commutator ideal be non-degenerate, we develop a framework that extends the construction to the Lorentzian context and covers both the non-degenerate and degenerate center cases. In the degenerate case, we show that the associated Lie algebra is a central extension of a semidirect product whose Riemannian factor is naturally reductive. Furthermore, we obtain invariant decompositions of the defining representation, including a distinguished Lorentzian factor, and provide an explicit description of the isotropy algebra and the identity component of the isometric automorphism group. These results complete the structural description of naturally reductive $2$-step Lorentzian nilpotent Lie groups under the assumption of non-degeneracy in the commutator ideal.

</details>


### [183] [Positivity and non-positivity results for the sixth-order $Q$-curvature of conformal metrics in $\mathbb{R}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18205)
*Jérôme Vétois,Samuel Zeitler*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 上共形欧几里得度量的低阶 $Q$-曲率正性问题。当 $m\ge4,k=3$ 时，对 $n\in[2m,4m-6]$ 证明了 $Q_g^{(6)}>0$；但当 $n\ge N_m$ 时，构造了反例说明 $Q_g^{(2m)}>0$ 但 $Q_g^{(6)}$ 可负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $Q_g^{(2m)}\ge0$ 且不恒为零的条件下，探究低阶 $Q$-曲率 $Q_g^{(2k)}\ (k=1,\dots,m-1)$ 是否必然正定。此前对 $m=2,k=1$ 及 $m\ge2,k\in\{1,\min(m-1,2)\}$ 已有肯定结果，本文旨在推进到 $k=3$ 情形。

Method: 采用解析与几何相结合的方法，利用共形欧几里得度量的性质及 $Q$-曲率的递推结构。对正结果使用屏障条件与非线性分析技术；对反例通过显式构造共形度量实现 $Q_g^{(2m)}>0$ 但 $Q_g^{(6)}<0$，并通过球极投影延拓到球面。

Result: 正结果：当 $m\ge4,k=3$ 且 $n\in\{2m,2m+1,\dots,4m-6\}$ 时，$Q_g^{(6)}>0$；负结果：当 $k=3$ 且 $n\ge N_m$（某 $N_m\in\mathbb R$）时，存在共形欧几里得度量使得 $Q_g^{(2m)}$ 处处为正但 $Q_g^{(6)}$ 在某点取负值。

Conclusion: 低阶 $Q$-曲率的正性问题在 $k=3$ 时具有维度依赖性：在中等维度范围成立正性，但在高维情形不成立。这与 $k=1,2$ 时在所有维度均成立的正性结论形成鲜明对比。

Abstract: Given $n,m\in\mathbb{N}$ such that $n\ge2m\ge4$, letting $g$ be a conformally Euclidean metric on $\mathbb{R}^n$, we consider the question of positivity of the lower-order $Q$-curvatures $Q_g^{(2k)}$ for $k\in\left\{1,\dotsc,m-1\right\}$ when $Q_g^{(2m)}$ is assumed to be nonnegative and not identically zero. We assume moreover that the scalar curvature of the metric $g$ is nonnegative near infinity if $n=2m$ or that $Q_g^{(2m)}$ satisfies a slow decay barrier condition near infinity if $n>2m$. Positive results for this question have been obtained by Gursky and Malchiodi for $m=2$ and $k=1$ in the context of closed manifolds with nonnegative scalar curvature and by Li and Xu and Li, Wei, and Xu for $m\ge2$ and $k\in\left\{1,\min(m-1,2)\right\}$ in the context of conformally Euclidean metrics on $\mathbb{R}^n$. These results hold for all $n\ge2m$. Considering the case where $m\ge4$ and $k=3$, we obtain a positive result for this question when $n\in\left\{2m,2m+1,\dotsc,4m-6\right\}$, namely for these dimensions, we obtain that if $Q_g^{(2m)}\ge0$ and $Q_g^{(2m)}\not\equiv0$ in $\mathbb{R}^n$, then $Q_g^{(6)}>0$. On the other hand, in surprising contrast with the results of Gursky and Malchiodi, Li and Xu, and Li, Wei, and Xu, we find that the answer to this question is negative when $k=3$ and $n\ge N_m$ for some $N_m\in\mathbb{R}$. In this case, we are able to construct examples of conformally Euclidean metrics such that $Q_g^{(2m)}$ is positive everywhere, but $Q_g^{(6)}$ is negative at some point. By stereographic projection, our examples extend to metrics conformal to the standard metric on $\mathbb{S}^n$.

</details>


### [184] [Sharp Weitzenböck and PIC2 Estimates from Sectional-Scalar Curvature Pinching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18216)
*Jian Ge*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在n维欧氏向量空间（n≥4）上证明了代数曲率张量在非负截面曲率条件下的最优点态估计，并以此推导出在严格截面-标量曲率夹挤条件下H²(M;ℝ)的消失性、调和2-形式平行性以及贝蒂数的拓扑结论，同时给出了PIC2的最优点态截面-标量夹挤准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立截面曲率非负条件下曲率张量的最优点态估计，并利用该估计研究流形上调和2-形式的存在性与拓扑限制，特别是贝蒂数的 vanishing 与上同调群的结构。

Method: 利用代数曲率张量分解Rm_g = K_min I + E，结合非负截面曲率的假设，通过点态估计得到q₂(E)的下界；再借助Ricci流的归一化演化以及PIC2条件的分析，导出刚性分类结果。

Result: (1) 得到了q₂(E) ≥ -2(ℓ-1)/(3ℓ)・Scal(E)Id的最优点态估计；(2) 严格夹挤条件下H²(M;ℝ)=0，弱端点时所有调和2-形式平行；(3) 奇数维b₂(M)=0，偶数维b₂(M)≤1；(4) 偶数维权b₂(M)>0时(M,g)等距于ℂP^ℓ（Fubini-Study度量）；(5) 五维闭流形满足严格夹挤条件时是有理同调球面；(6) 给出了PIC2的最优点态截面-标量夹挤准则K_min ≥ n(n-1)/(n²-n+12)S₀ ⇒ PIC2。

Conclusion: 通过最优点态估计揭示了截面曲率非负条件下曲率张量的精细结构，建立了曲率夹挤条件与流形拓扑（贝蒂数、上同调群）之间的深刻联系，并利用Ricci流实现了从严格夹挤到常正截面曲率的演化。

Abstract: Let $V$ be an $n$-dimensional Euclidean vector space, ,where $n\ge 4$, and $\ell = \lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor$. We prove the sharp pointwise estimate
  \[
  q_2(E) \ge -\frac{2(\ell -1)}{3\ell} \mathrm{Scal}(E) \mathrm{Id}_{Λ^2V^*}
  \]
  for every algebraic curvature tensor $E$ on $V$ with nonnegative sectional curvature. Applying this estimate to the decomposition $\operatorname{Rm}_{g}=K_{\min}I+E$, we obtain the vanishing of $H^2(M; \mathbb{R})$ under a dimension-dependent strict sectional-scalar curvature pinching condition. At the weak endpoint, all harmonic two-forms are parallel. Apart from the flat case, this yields $b_2(M)=0$ in odd dimensions and $b_2(M)\le 1$ in even dimensions. At even-dimensional endpoint, $b_2(M)>0$ forces $(M, g)$ to be isometric, up to scaling, to $\mathbb{CP}^{\ell}$ with its Fubini-Study metric. As a consequence every closed five-dimensional manifold satisfying the strict pinching condition implies is a rational homology sphere. An anisotropic rescaling of the same homogeneous four-frame estimate also gives the sharp pointwise sectional-scalar pinching criterion
  \[
  K_{\min} \ge \frac{n(n-1)}{n^2-n+12}S_0 \quad \Longrightarrow \mathrm{PIC2}.
  \]
  The strict pinching places the curvature tensor in the interior of PIC2 and normalized Ricci flow brings it to a positive constant sectional curvature.

</details>

### [185] [On Accretion and Neutrino Investigations of Thermodynamically Reconstructed Black Holes from Three-Parameter Generalized Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16337)
*F. Barzi,W. El Hadri,H. El Moumni,K. Masmar,S. Mazzou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究基于三参数广义熵重构的静态黑洞，探讨其近吸积与中微子敏感热信号。广义熵通过地平线响应因子Ξ_h固定，有效外部几何在保持视界面积和Schwarzschild渐近性的同时，受参数λ_G=1/Ξ_h-1和整数p≥2控制。正λ_G使光子球和ISCO内移、阴影尺度减小、辐射效率提高，负λ_G则相反。中微子扇区通过无量纲温度矩分析，高指数矩更显著体现熵致盘形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立广义熵与黑洞可观测热信号（光子球、阴影、吸积盘辐射、中微子温度矩等）之间的直接联系，从而通过天文观测检验或约束广义熵框架。

Method: 采用热力学重构方法：由广义熵固定地平线响应因子Ξ_h，要求有效几何的表面引力温度与广义热力学温度一致，从而重构外部度规。分析光子球、临界阴影尺度、圆轨道、ISCO、Novikov–Thorne盘通量、盘温度、辐射效率、无穷远能量光度、红移谱代理及中微子温度矩（n=4,6,9）。

Result: 正λ_G使光子球和ISCO向内移动，阴影尺度减小，辐射效率提高，能量释放集中于内盘；负λ_G产生相反趋势。中微子温度矩的层级显示，更高温度指数（n=9）下熵致盘形变更为明显。

Conclusion: 紧致轨道和薄盘发射的可观测变化可通过地平线响应直接链接到广义熵，为通过天文观测检验广义熵框架提供了具体可测信号。

Abstract: We study accretion and neutrino-sensitive thermal signatures of a static black hole reconstructed from a three-parameter generalized entropy. The construction is thermodynamic by design: the entropy deformation is not mapped to a radial-coordinate redefinition or to a prescribed Reissner--Nordström-like correction. Instead, the generalized entropy fixes the horizon response factor $Ξ_h=dS_G/dS|_{S_h}$. The effective exterior geometry is then reconstructed by requiring its surface-gravity temperature to reproduce the generalized thermodynamic temperature. As a result, the metric preserves the horizon area and the Schwarzschild asymptotics, and reduces smoothly to Schwarzschild when $Ξ_h\to1$.
  The deformation is controlled by $λ_G=1/Ξ_h-1$, while the integer $p\geq2$ determines the radial localization of the near-horizon correction. We compute the photon sphere, critical shadow scale, circular geodesics, ISCO, Novikov--Thorne flux, disk temperature, radiative efficiency, energy-at-infinity luminosity, a redshifted spectral proxy, and neutrino-sensitive temperature moments. In particular, positive $λ_G$ moves the photon sphere and ISCO inward, decreases the shadow scale, raises the radiative efficiency, and concentrates the energy release toward the inner disk. By contrast, negative $λ_G$ produces the opposite trend.
  Finally, the neutrino sector is modeled conservatively using dimensionless temperature moments instead of a full neutrino-dominated accretion flow or annihilation-deposition calculation. The hierarchy among the $ n=4$, $n=6$, and $n=9$ moments reveals that entropy-induced disk deformation becomes increasingly apparent with higher temperature exponents. Thus, observable changes in compact orbits and thin-disk emission can be directly linked to generalized entropy via the horizon response.

</details>


### [186] [No late-time role for adiabatic torsion: a no-go result for Hubble-cutoff holographic dark energy in Einstein--Cartan cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16370)
*Fernando Izaurieta,Samuel Lepe,Cristian Quinzacara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，爱因斯坦-卡坦引力中的均匀挠率模（Φ∝a⁻³）虽曾被提议可作为全息暗能量的红外截断，但实际无法挽救哈勃半径作为截断的方案：它仅能在挠率反弹附近产生短暂的加速窗口，而将该窗口置于可观测红移会违背宇宙膨胀历史。通过对DESI DR2 BAO、高红移星系、CMB和BBN数据的约束，挠率参数Ω_Φ被严格限制在10⁻⁴至10⁻²⁴量级，其对暗能量状态方程的影响远低于DESI观测偏好。在Granda-Oliveros截断下，挠率反而加剧了大撕裂奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期有研究主张，爱因斯坦-卡坦引力中的均匀挠率模可作为全息暗能量的红外截断，产生晚期宇宙加速和幻影穿越现象，可能解释DESI观测结果。本文旨在检验这一主张是否成立。

Method: 通过解析分析验证挠率模在哈勃截断下的动力学行为，利用DESI DR2 BAO似然拟合、高红移星系（z=14.32）存在性、CMB和BBN数据对挠率参数Ω_Φ=(Φ₀/H₀)²进行多层次约束，并考察Granda-Oliveros截断下的奇点行为。

Result: 哈勃截断下全息暗能量密度从减速参数中消失，唯一加速区间是挠率反弹附近的瞬态窗口；要求该窗口在可观测红移会导致哈勃率在近期为零，违背膨胀历史。DESI给出Ω_Φ<8.7×10⁻⁴（95%CL），高红移星系给出8.4×10⁻⁵，CMB给出3.1×10⁻¹⁰，BBN给出5×10⁻²⁴。暗能量状态方程偏差|1+ω₀|≤2Ω_Φ/Ω_Λ，比DESI偏好低2到22个数量级。Granda-Oliveros截断下挠率加剧大撕裂奇点。

Conclusion: 爱因斯坦-卡坦均匀挠率模无法作为全息暗能量的可行红外截断，其对观测宇宙学的影响微乎其微，远不足以解释DESI所暗示的动力学暗能量信号。

Abstract: In Friedmann cosmology with Einstein--Cartan torsion, the homogeneous torsion mode compatible with a separately conserved matter sector scales as $Φ\propto a^{-3}$ and enters the Friedmann constraint as a stiff component of negative energy density, $-3Φ^{2}\propto a^{-6}$. It has recently been claimed that this mode rescues the Hubble radius as an infrared cutoff for holographic dark energy, producing late-time acceleration and a phantom-divide crossing of possible relevance to DESI. We show that it cannot. With the Hubble cutoff the holographic density drops out of the deceleration parameter, and the only accelerating regime is the transient window $\bar{a}\leq a<4^{1/3}\bar{a}$ around the torsion bounce at $H(\bar{a})=0$; placing that window at observable redshifts would force the Hubble rate to vanish in our recent past, against the measured expansion history. Requiring a viable history yields nested upper bounds on $Ω_Φ\equiv(Φ_{0}/H_{0})^{2}$: fitting the official DESI DR2 BAO likelihood gives $Ω_Φ<8.7\times10^{-4}$ ($95\%$ CL), the existence of a spectroscopically confirmed galaxy at $z=14.32$ gives $8.4\times10^{-5}$, the CMB gives $3.1\times10^{-10}$, and Big Bang nucleosynthesis gives $5\times10^{-24}$. Today's imprint on the dark energy equation of state, $|1+ω_{0}|\leq2Ω_Φ/Ω_Λ$, falls short of the DESI preference by two orders of magnitude at best and twenty-two at worst, and on the phantom side. In the Granda--Oliveros cutoff, torsion deepens rather than prevents the big-rip singularity. An appendix derives the Friedmann pair from the Einstein--Cartan field equations and shows that the $a^{-3}$ scaling is the kinematics of a diluting spin fluid; escaping the no-go requires breaking exactly that.

</details>


### [187] [Probing f(R) AdS Black Hole via Hawking Evaporation, Shadows and Thermal Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16432)
*Muhammad Israr Aslam,Saira Waheed,Rabia Saleem,Nazek Alessa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(R) AdS黑洞框架下研究了霍金蒸发过程、阴影和热涨落。通过Stefan-Boltzmann定律数值分析发现黑洞寿命无限，最终形成残余。还分析了阴影半径随参数ψ₀减小、随λ增大的特性，以及吸积物质对光子环光学外观的影响。此外，系统讨论了热力学量及参数对稳定性和相变的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在f(R)修正引力理论背景下，系统研究AdS黑洞的霍金蒸发过程、视觉阴影特性及热力学性质，特别是探讨修正参数（λ和ψ₀）对这些物理过程的调控作用。

Method: 采用Stefan-Boltzmann定律数值分析霍金蒸发率；通过几何光学方法研究阴影半径和吸积物质的光学外观；利用热涨落修正的热力学公式分析相变和稳定性。

Result: (1) 黑洞寿命无限，最终形成残余；(2) 蒸发速率依赖于AdS半径和耦合参数；(3) 阴影半径随ψ₀增大而减小，随λ增大而增大；(4) 光子环的光学外观变化主要受参数影响；(5) 热力学量及相变行为受λ和ψ₀调控。

Conclusion: f(R) AdS黑洞的蒸发最终停止于残余态，且其阴影和热力学性质可通过修正参数进行有效调控，为理解修正引力下的黑洞物理提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The process of Hawking evaporation, shadows and thermal fluctuations are investigated within the fabric of f(R) AdS Black Hole (BH). Specifically, the Hawking evaporation process is analyzed numerically using the Stefan-Boltzmann law. The results indicate that the BH lifetime is always infinite, which means the BH becomes a remnant in the late time. Additionally, the evaporation rate depends on the AdS radius and coupling parameters. We further examined the visual properties of BH shadows observed for various values of the two parameters. The results reveal that the BH shadow radius decreases with $ψ_{0}$, while it increases with $λ$. Consequently, we further investigate the infalling accretion matter in the vicinity of BHs. The results depict that while variations in relevant parameters do influence the central region, the important factor is the change in optical appearance of the bright photon ring, which is exhibited at the position of the photon sphere. Next, we discuss many thermodynamical quantities, such as temperature, entropy, Helmholtz free energy, internal energy, corrected pressure, enthalpy, Gibbs free energy and specific heat and interpret how the variations in $λ$ and $ψ_{0}$ impact on the stability and phase transitions of the AdS BHs.

</details>


### [188] [Gravitational Waves as Thermodynamic Shear Excitations in Scalar-Tensor Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16437)
*David S. Pereira*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Jordan标量-张量引力中，横向无迹扰动激活了标量场的剪切做功通道，该应力功率具有规范不变性，并赋予自旋-2模式热力学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标量-张量引力中横向无迹扰动如何激活标量场的局部剪切做功机制，并揭示其热力学意义。

Method: 在Jordan框架下，对均匀无剪切背景施加横向无迹扰动，计算标量场有效热力学介质的剪切做功项 $\mathcal W_φ=π_{ab}^{(φ)}σ^{ab}$，并提取纯TT贡献的主导项 $\mathcal P_{\rm TT}^{[1,1]}$，分析其规范不变性和能量平衡关系。

Result: 获得了纯TT贡献的主导表达式 $\mathcal P_{\rm TT}^{[1,1]} =\overline{\mathcal K\mathcal T} \dotχ_{ij}^{\rm TT}\dotχ_{\rm TT}^{ij}/4$，证明该应力功率在二阶是规范不变的，并发现标量-张量耦合以相反符号出现在GR归一化的振幅平衡中。

Conclusion: 标量-张量引力提供了一个可控范例，其中传播的自旋-2模式获得了本质上引力的热力学解释，尽管其作为独立张量能量源的解读依赖于归一化约定。

Abstract: In Jordan-frame scalar--tensor gravity, a timelike scalar gradient defines an effective thermodynamic medium. We show that a transverse--traceless perturbation of a homogeneous shear-free background activates the local shear-work channel $\mathcal W_φ=π_{ab}^{(φ)}σ^{ab}$, with leading pure-TT contribution $\mathcal P_{\rm TT}^{[1,1]} =\overline{\mathcal K\mathcal T} \dotχ_{ij}^{\rm TT}\dotχ_{\rm TT}^{ij}/4$. This stress power follows from local traction mechanics, enters the scalar-sector energy balance, and is gauge invariant at second order. The same scalar--tensor coupling controls non-GR tensor damping and appears with the opposite sign in a GR-normalized amplitude balance, although its interpretation as a separate tensor-energy source is normalization dependent. Scalar--tensor gravity thus provides a controlled example in which a propagating spin-2 mode acquires an intrinsically gravitational thermodynamic interpretation.

</details>


### [189] [Biases in Tests of General Relativity from Microlensed Gravitational-Wave Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16458)
*Anirban Kopty,Apratim Ganguly,N. V. Krishnendu,Anuj Mishra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了引力波微透镜效应对标准引力理论检验的影响。通过模拟GW150914-like的透镜信号，发现波光学区域的衍射效应会导致误判为对广义相对论的偏离（最高~4.5σ），而几何光学和长波长区域的信号则与GR一致。研究表明微透镜是未来高精度GR检验中不可忽略的天体物理系统误差来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波观测的可靠性依赖于精确的波形模板，未建模的物理效应可能引入系统误差，导致对广义相对论的假偏离。本文旨在探究引力波微透镜如何影响现有的GR检验方法，并量化其导致的误判风险。

Method: 使用GW150914-like模拟信号，经孤立点质量微透镜（10-10^5 M⊙）透镜化，采用未透镜波形模板进行贝叶斯参数估计。在参数化检验、修正色散关系检验、IMRCT和meta-IMRCT框架中量化偏差，并基于GR分位数引入统一的GR偏离显著性统计量。

Result: 微透镜诱导的波形畸变可在一维GR检验中产生~4σ的假偏离，二维一致性检验中达到~4.5σ。假偏离主要出现在波光学区域，长波长和几何光学区域信号与GR一致。假偏离的显著性与微透镜可探测性（贝叶斯因子）弱相关。

Conclusion: 波光学引力透镜是当前及未来高精度GR检验的重要天体物理系统误差来源，下一代引力波分析需对传播效应进行建模。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) observations of compact binary mergers enable precision tests of general relativity (GR) in the strong-field regime, but their reliability depends on accurate waveform modeling. Unmodeled physical effects can induce systematic biases that mimic deviations from GR. Here we study the impact of GW microlensing on standard LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA tests of GR using GW150914-like simulated signals lensed by isolated point-mass objects with masses in the range $10-10^{5}M_\odot$. We perform Bayesian parameter estimation with unlensed waveform templates and quantify biases in parameterized tests, modified dispersion relation tests, the inspiral-merger-ringdown consistency test (IMRCT) and the meta-IMRCT framework. To compare these tests with a common discriminator, we introduce a unified GR-deviation significance statistic based on the GR quantile, applicable to both one- and multi-dimensional deviation parameters. We find that microlensing-induced waveform distortions can produce significant false deviations from GR, reaching $\sim4σ$ in one-dimensional tests and $\sim4.5σ$ in two-dimensional consistency tests, despite the injected signals being fully GR-compatible. These false deviations arise mainly in the wave-optics regime, where diffraction induces frequency-dependent amplitude and phase modulations, while signals in the long-wavelength and geometric-optics regimes remain largely consistent with GR. We also find that the significance of these apparent deviations does not correlate strongly with microlensing detectability as measured by Bayes factors, showing that GR tests probe waveform projections not captured by global lensing diagnostics. Our results establish wave-optics gravitational lensing as an important astrophysical systematic for present and future precision tests of GR and highlight the need to model propagation effects in next-generation GW analyses.

</details>


### [190] [Separating the linearized Einstein equations in Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16581)
*Jianwei Mei*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文展示了Kerr时空中线性化爱因斯坦方程的直接分离。通过结合无迹条件、Killing-Yano对称性、宇称要求和de Donder规范条件，成功将Kerr黑洞的线性化爱因斯坦方程简化到可处理水平，实现了自旋-2扰动的方程直接分离和唯一解耦模函数的推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Kerr时空中自旋-2扰动（引力波）解耦和分离的难题，因为Kerr黑洞的旋转性使得线性化爱因斯坦方程在传统方法下难以分离变量。

Method: 结合无迹条件、Killing-Yano对称性、宇称要求和de Donder规范条件，将线性化爱因斯坦方程逐步简化；扰动度规的迹满足自旋-0扰动方程故被排除；最终实现方程的直接分离并推导出解耦模函数。

Result: 成功实现了Kerr时空中线性化爱因斯坦方程的直接分离，并获得了自旋-2扰动唯一的一组解耦模函数。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了Kerr黑洞自旋-2扰动的解耦问题，为后续引力波理论研究提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: A direct separation of the linearized Einstein equations in Kerr is presented. The trace of the perturbed metric is found to obey the spin-0 perturbation equation and so is irrelevant for the spin-2 perturbations. By combining the traceless condition, the Killing-Yano symmetry, a parity requirement, and the de Donder gauge condition, it has been found possible to reduce the linearized Einstein equations in Kerr to a manageable level, thereby enabling the direct separation of the equations and the derivation of a unique set of decoupled mode functions for the spin-2 perturbation of the Kerr black hole.

</details>


### [191] [Regular Black Hole Formation and Gamma-Ray Burst from Matter Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16590)
*Vitalii Vertogradov,Zhanna Kuznetsova,Yu Shi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了重子物质坍缩成Dymnikova-Hayward-Bardeen规则黑洞过程中，为避免奇点形成而产生的新物质形态及其伴随的电磁辐射，提出该过程可作为伽马射线暴的可能机制，并指出与观测兼容要求规则化效应很小，使规则黑洞与史瓦西黑洞差异微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大质量恒星引力坍缩形成规则黑洞时，为避免中心奇点而动态产生的新物质形态是否会产生可观测的电磁辐射信号，并解释伽马射线暴的起源。

Method: 研究重子物质坍缩成Dymnikova-Hayward-Bardeen规则黑洞的过程，估算由避免奇点的物质扇区形成所产生的辐射密度和相应的热光度。

Result: 此类过程可作为伽马射线暴的可能机制；与伽马射线暴兼容要求规则化效应很小，对应的规则黑洞与史瓦西黑洞差异微弱。

Conclusion: 规则黑洞形成过程中的能量释放可能解释伽马射线暴，但规则化效应必须很小，使得规则黑洞几乎与史瓦西黑洞不可区分。

Abstract: During the gravitational collapse of a massive star into a regular black hole, a new form of matter must be produced in order to prevent the formation of a central singularity. Since such matter is not present in the initial stellar configuration, it must emerge dynamically during the collapse. This formation process is expected to be accompanied by a strong release of energy in the form of electromagnetic radiation, which may be observable. Here we investigate the gravitational collapse of baryonic matter into Dymnikova-Hayward-Bardeen regular black holes. We estimate the radiation density and the corresponding bolometric luminosity generated by the formation of the matter sector responsible for singularity avoidance. We show that such processes provide a possible mechanism for gamma-ray bursts. Moreover, compatibility with gamma-ray burst requires small regularization effects. As a result, the corresponding regular black holes differ weakly from the Schwarzschild black hole.

</details>


### [192] [Asymptotic analysis of varying light speed cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16814)
*Dimitrios Trachilis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了光速和引力常数随时间变化的各向同性宇宙学模型中的潜在奇点，通过相空间动力学系统分析和渐近分裂方法，发现闭合模型会经历膨胀后重新塌缩至未来奇点，开放宇宙则持续膨胀，并阐述了这些奇点的具体特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在光速和引力常数随时间变化的条件下，各向同性宇宙学模型中可能存在的奇点问题，以理解变光速和变引力常数理论下的宇宙演化行为。

Method: 将基本场方程简化为二维系统，采用相空间中的动力学系统技术进行分析，并应用渐近分裂方法研究奇点行为。

Result: 发现初始膨胀的闭合模型会经历重新塌缩并走向未来奇点，而开放宇宙则会持续膨胀至未来；同时明确了这些奇点的具体特征。

Conclusion: 变光速和变引力常数宇宙学模型中的奇点行为依赖于宇宙的空间曲率，闭合模型存在再塌缩奇点，开放模型则无限膨胀，且这些奇点的性质可通过动力学系统方法得到刻画。

Abstract: We investigate potential singularities in isotropic cosmological models featuring a time-varying speed of light and the gravitational constant. The governing field equations generally simplify into two-dimensional systems, subject to analysis through dynamical systems techniques within phase space and the application of asymptotic splitting methods. In the broader context, our findings reveal the existence of initially expanding closed models that undergo subsequent recollapse towards a future singularity, and open universes exhibiting perpetual expansion into the future. The specific characteristics of these singularities are also expounded upon.

</details>


### [193] [Analytical Study of Deflection Angle and Time Delay in Kerr Spacetime with Modified Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16842)
*Takamasa Kanai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了克尔时空中高阶曲率有效场论修正下光子传播定律改变所导致的引力透镜效应，解析推导了偏折角和传播时延，并指出这些修正与黑洞自旋相关，在强场区域（尤其是光子区附近）可能对可观测效应产生显著影响，为探测紫外引力修正提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在克尔时空背景下，探索高阶曲率有效场论修正对光子传播的影响，量化标准引力透镜理论的偏离，为检验紫外引力修正提供理论框架。

Method: 采用变形色散关系描述修正的光子传播定律，在旋转（克尔）背景下解析推导了零测地线的偏折角和传播时延。

Result: 修正后的传播导致偏折角和时延相对于标准克尔预言出现明确且可计算的偏差，这些修正与黑洞自旋存在非平凡依赖关系，并在强场区域（尤其是光子区附近）更为显著。

Conclusion: 建立了量化标准光子传播偏离的系统框架，指出相对论性图像分离和时延等可观测量为在强场环境中探测引力的紫外修正提供了潜在途径。

Abstract: We investigate gravitational lensing in Kerr spacetime in the presence of a modified photon propagation law arising from higher-curvature effective field theory corrections. Adopting a deformed dispersion relation, we analytically derive the deflection angle and the propagation time delay for null trajectories in a rotating background.
  We show that the modified propagation leads to explicit and calculable deviations from the standard Kerr predictions, affecting both the bending angle and the time delay. These corrections exhibit a nontrivial dependence on the black hole spin and are expected to become more relevant in the strong-field regime. In particular, near the photon region-where the spin-dependent geometry influences photon trajectories-such effects may play a role in shaping observable propagation features.
  Our results establish a concrete and systematic framework to quantify deviations from standard photon propagation in gravitational lensing. They further indicate that observables such as relativistic image separations and time delays provide a potential avenue to probe ultraviolet corrections to gravity in strong-field environments.

</details>


### [194] [Perturbations of black holes in mass-varying massive gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17046)
*De-Jun Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于特征分解和矩阵微扰理论，提出了一种适用于大质量引力及其修改理论的扰动分析方法，并在质量变化大质量引力中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大质量引力扰动分析方法在某些看似奇异的参数区域失效，需要一种更普适的框架来处理基理论及其各种修改版本中的扰动问题。

Method: 利用特征分解和矩阵微扰理论，构建了一个替代性扰动分析框架；将该方法应用于质量变化大质量引力，计算了所有常标量Schwarzschild-(A)dS黑洞解的扰动。

Result: 在$β=α^2$条件下，这些黑洞的张量扇区约化为广义相对论情形；标量扇区在适当参数选择下无快子不稳定性；一般参数解与$β=α^2$解的解流形在扰动消失的特定轨迹上相交。

Conclusion: 提出的扰动分析方法在看似奇异的参数区域仍然有效，揭示了质量变化大质量引力中黑洞解的深层结构关系。

Abstract: Based on eigendecomposition and matrix perturbation theory, we propose an alternative method for analyzing perturbations in massive gravity that is applicable to both the base theory and its various modifications. We demonstrate the validity of this approach even in seemingly singular regimes. Applying this framework to mass-varying massive gravity, we compute perturbations for all constant-scalar Schwarzschild-(A)dS black hole solutions. We show that, under the condition $β= α^2$, the tensor sector of these black holes reduces to that of general relativity, while the scalar sector remains free from tachyonic instabilities given appropriate parameter choices. A deeper relationship is also revealed between the solutions with generic parameters and those with $β= α^2$: their solution manifolds intersect at a specific locus where the perturbation vanishes.

</details>


### [195] [Probing Nonlinear Logarithmic Kalb-Ramond Black Holes: Particle Dynamics, Epicyclic Oscillations and Thermodynamic Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17198)
*Aftab Ansari,Rajesh Kumar,Praveen Kumar Dhankar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类新型对数Kalb-Ramond黑洞，该黑洞由非线性对数耦合场生成。通过有效势形式分析了测试粒子的运动，研究了圆形赤道测地线的稳定性、最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）以及本轮频率（径向、垂直、方位角）和近日点进动。还分析了霍金温度和能量发射率，揭示了非线性对数耦合对黑洞时空几何、轨道动力学和热力学行为的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的线性耦合框架在黑洞强场区域中不再充分有效，非线性效应变得显著。作者旨在研究非线性对数耦合对Kalb-Ramond黑洞时空几何的影响，并探索其在天体物理观测中的可观测特征。

Method: 采用有效势形式分析测试粒子运动；推导圆形赤道测地线的守恒能量和角动量；计算本轮频率和近日点进动；研究霍金温度和能量发射率。

Result: ISCO位置和圆形轨道稳定性强烈依赖模型参数Q、l和β；非线性对数修正显著改变准周期振荡可观测量；非线性耦合对黑洞热力学行为和蒸发特性也产生独特影响。

Conclusion: 对数Kalb-Ramond黑洞参数是轨道动力学、振荡特性和热演化的关键调控因子，为通过强场天体物理现象探测非线性KR引力提供了统一框架。

Abstract: In contrast to conventional linear coupling frameworks, the proposed work investigated the nonlinear effects that become more significant in the strong field regime of a black hole. We have investigated a new class of Kalb Ramond black holes generated by a nonlinear logarithmic coupling of the field, referred to as a Logarithmic Kalb Ramond black hole. The logarithmic coupling introduces strong-field modifications to the spacetime geometry, leading to significant departures from the Schwarzschild and Reissner Nordstrom BHs. We analyze the motion of test particles using the effective potential formalism and derive the conserved energy and angular momentum for circular equatorial geodesics. The stability of circular orbits and the location of the innermost stable circular orbit are examined, revealing a strong dependence on the model parameters Q, l, and $β$. The epicyclic frequencies (radial, vertical, and azimuthal) together with the associated periastron precession demonstrate that nonlinear logarithmic corrections can substantially modify quasi periodic oscillation observables. We further investigate the Hawking temperature and energy emission rate, which show that the nonlinear coupling also impacts distinct imprints on the thermodynamic behavior and evaporation characteristics of the black hole. Our results also identify the BH parameters as key regulators of the orbital dynamics, oscillatory properties, and thermal evolution, providing a unified framework for probing nonlinear KR gravity through strong-field astrophysical phenomena.

</details>


### [196] [Axial Obstructions to Rotating Bumblebee Vacuum Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17223)
*Minyong Guo,Zhong-Ying Fan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发现两种导致常模向量真空无法存在正则旋转解的一般性障碍：一是双曲分叉面极点处的水平与轴向不变性迫使对称继承性1-形式为零，与严格非零常模矛盾；二是沿旋转轴的锥度变化会产生曲率奇异性。将这些结果应用于爱因斯坦-大黄蜂引力中的Kerr-disformal解族，发现其向量真空因非零常模在轴上不光滑，且位置依赖的锥度导致极轴上出现真实曲率奇点。仅某非极端分支在轴外区域存在正则外视界，定义了非极地区域的精确局部旋转解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究洛伦兹破缺引力理论（如爱因斯坦-大黄蜂引力）中旋转黑洞解的存在性，特别关注常模向量真空在旋转情形下是否可能保持正则性。

Method: 通过微分几何分析，识别旋转常模向量真空的正则性障碍；然后以Kerr-disformal解族为具体案例进行验证。

Result: 发现两个一般性障碍：极点处对称性迫使向量场为零与常模矛盾；轴向锥度变化导致曲率奇点。Kerr-disformal解在轴上不光滑且存在真实奇点，仅非极端分支在轴外区域存在正则视界。

Conclusion: 爱因斯坦-大黄蜂引力中的旋转常模向量真空解在极轴上普遍存在奇异性，无法实现全局正则的旋转黑洞解。

Abstract: We identify two general obstructions to a regular rotating constant-norm vector vacua. Firstly, at the poles of a nondegenerate bifurcation surface, horizon and axial invariance force every smooth symmetry-inheriting one-form to vanish, contradicting a strictly nonzero constant norm. Secondly, we show that a conicity varying along a rotation axis generates a singular curvature that diverges as the inverse proper distance from the axis. We apply these results to a general three parameter family Kerr-disformal solution in Einstein--bumblebee gravity. Consequently, its vector vacuum is not smooth on the axes because of a nonzero constant norm, and the position-dependent conicity leads to genuine curvature singularities on the polar axes. A distinguished nonextremal branch nevertheless admits a regular outer Killing horizon away from the axes and defines an exact local rotating solution in the nonpolar region.

</details>


### [197] [Probing Primordial Cosmology Through BBN Observational Constraints Under Extended Gravitational Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17252)
*Abdul Malik Sultan,Manahil Ali,Muhammad Israr Aslam,Nazek Alessa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(R,G,T)引力框架下，利用大爆炸核合成（BBN）对早期宇宙的严格约束，分析了四个代表性模型，通过氦-4丰度Y_p和|ΔT_f/T_f|观测限值约束模型参数，结果表明该引力理论与观测到的原始轻元素丰度一致，允许显著偏离标准宇宙学的参数空间存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验f(R,G,T)引力框架的宇宙学后果，利用大爆炸核合成这一早期宇宙最可靠探针，验证该修正引力理论是否与标准宇宙学图像及观测到的轻元素丰度兼容。

Method: 选取四个代表性f(R,G,T)引力模型，利用BBN观测限值（|ΔT_f/T_f|和原始氦质量分数Y_p）对模型参数空间进行约束分析，确定允许的参数区域。

Result: 获得了每个模型的允许参数区间，表明f(R,G,T)引力的广泛参数区域能满足BBN约束，显著偏离标准宇宙学的参数空间仍与核合成观测兼容。

Conclusion: f(R,G,T)修正引力理论与早期宇宙原始轻元素丰度观测结果一致，不影响已建立的早期宇宙图像和轻核素丰度。

Abstract: In this article, We investigate the cosmological consequences of a recently developed $f(R,G,\mathcal{T})$ gravitational framework, in which the action is formulated as a general function of the Ricci scalar $R$, the Gauss-Bonnet invariant $G$, and the trace of the energy-momentum tensor $\mathcal{T}$. As one of the most reliable probes of the physical conditions in the early universe, Big Bang nucleosynthesis offers a stringent framework for testing deviations from standard cosmology. We consider four representative models that are analyzed and constrained using observational limits on $\left|ΔT_f/T_f\right|$ and the primordial helium mass fraction $Y_p$. The bounds obtained identify the allowed parameter regions for each model and demonstrate that significant departures from standard cosmology are compatible with nucleosynthesis observations. Our analysis shows that broad regions of the parameter space satisfy existing nucleosynthesis constraints, indicating the consistency of $f(R,G,\mathcal{T})$ gravity with the observed primordial light-element abundances and the established picture of the early universe preserving the observed abundances of light nuclei.

</details>


### [198] [Spontaneous scalarization around a black hole in a dark matter halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17367)
*M. Navid Gasemi Zad,H. Mohseni Sadjadi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，在暗物质与标量场共形耦合的标量-张量理论中，被Hernquist暗物质晕包围的黑洞可以发生自发标量化，获得非平凡标量场构型。暗物质晕的天体物理特性直接决定了中心黑洞周围标量场构型的类量子谱，且共形耦合常数被离散化，仅由晕的致密度参数决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质晕与黑洞之间的标量场耦合机制，研究天体物理环境如何影响黑洞的标量化过程。

Method: 基于标量-张量理论，考虑暗物质与标量场的共形耦合，采用Hernquist暗物质晕模型，分析中心黑洞周围标量场构型的自发标量化行为。

Result: 黑洞能获得非平凡标量场构型；暗物质晕特性决定标量场类量子谱；共形耦合常数离散化并由晕致密度参数决定。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕的天体物理性质与黑洞标量化之间存在深层联系，共形耦合常数的离散化揭示了宏观天体物理参数与微观场论参数之间的新颖对应关系。

Abstract: We demonstrate that in a scalar-tensor theory where dark matter is conformally coupled to a scalar field, a black hole surrounded by a Hernquist dark matter halo can undergo spontaneous scalarization, acquiring a nontrivial scalar field configuration. The astrophysical properties of the dark matter halo directly dictate the quantum-like spectrum of scalar field configurations around the central black hole. Remarkably, the conformal coupling constant becomes discretized and is determined solely by the halo's compactness parameter.

</details>


### [199] [Gravitational wave propagation in Hořava-Lifshitz gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17431)
*A. A. Araújo Filho,J. L. A. Silva,N. Heidari,Jie Zhu,Iarley P. Lobo,V. B. Bezerra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hořava-Lifshitz引力理论中宇称守恒的红外截断下的引力波产生与传播，其修正表现为色散关系ω²=k²+αk⁴。研究发现高阶空间导数修正保持标准引力波的+和×极化模式，不引入极化混合、螺旋性分裂或引力双折射。通过构建修正波算子的推迟格林函数，推导了辐射区波形的α一阶修正，信号呈现频率依赖的振幅重正化和色散传播相位。应用于准圆轨道双黑洞系统后，得到了极化波形、能量通量、总光度和绝热啁啾演化。关键修正满足Δh_A/h_A^GR=-8π²αf²和ΔP/P_GR=Δf·/f·_GR=-16π²αf²，累积相位相当于相对论第三后牛顿阶贡献。通过映射到LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA色散参数化，从GWTC-4.0后验分布得到α在90%置信度下的约束区间为-6.2×10² eV⁻²<α<1.9×10² eV⁻²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Hořava-Lifshitz引力理论中宇称守恒的红外截断对引力波产生和传播的影响，并通过引力波观测数据对该理论的参数α进行约束。

Method: 采用横向无迹规范，构建修正波算子的推迟格林函数，推导α一阶的辐射区波形；将其应用于准圆轨道双黑洞系统，计算极化波形、能量通量、光度和啁啾演化；将系数α映射到LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA修正色散参数化，利用GWTC-4.0后验数据给出约束。

Result: 高阶修正保持标准+和×极化，不引入额外极化效应；修正导致频率依赖的振幅重正化(Δh_A/h_A^GR=-8π²αf²)和功率/啁啾演化修正(ΔP/P_GR=Δf·/f·_GR=-16π²αf²)；累积相位相当于3PN阶贡献；GWTC-4.0给出α在90%置信度下为-6.2×10² eV⁻²<α<1.9×10² eV⁻²。

Conclusion: Hořava-Lifshitz引力的宇称守恒红外截断对引力波波形产生可观测的修正，当前LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA数据可对参数α给出有效约束，为进一步检验该引力理论提供了观测窗口。

Abstract: We investigate the generation and propagation of gravitational waves in the leading parity-even infrared truncation of Hořava-Lifshitz gravity, characterized by the modified tensor dispersion relation $ω^{2}=k^{2}+αk^{4}$. Working in the transverse-traceless sector, we show that the higher-spatial-derivative correction preserves the conventional plus and cross polarizations and introduces neither polarization mixing, helicity splitting, nor gravitational birefringence. We construct the retarded Green function of the modified wave operator and derive the radiation-zone waveform to first order in $α$. The resulting signal exhibits a frequency-dependent amplitude renormalization together with a dispersive propagation phase that accumulates over the source-observer distance. We apply the formalism to a binary black hole system in a quasi-circular orbit and obtain the polarization waveforms for an arbitrary observation direction. We further derive the corresponding energy flux, total luminosity, and adiabatic chirp evolution. In terms of the observed gravitational wave frequency $f$, the leading corrections satisfy $Δh_{A}/h_{A}^{\mathrm{GR}}=-8π^{2}αf^{2}$ and $ΔP/P_{\mathrm{GR}} =Δ\dot{f}/\dot{f}_{\mathrm{GR}} =-16π^{2}αf^{2}$, while the accumulated generation phase has the frequency dependence of a relative third post-Newtonian contribution. By mapping the Hořava-Lifshitz coefficient to the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA modified-dispersion parametrization, we obtain $-6.2\times10^{2}\,\mathrm{eV}^{-2} <α< 1.9\times10^{2}\,\mathrm{eV}^{-2}$ at $90\%$ credibility from the GWTC-4.0 posterior.

</details>


### [200] [The impact of physically motivated calibration errors on search pipeline detection parameters for broadband burst Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17439)
*Milan Wils,Brad Ratto,Jeffrey S. Kissel,Tjonnie G. F. Li,Marek J. Szczepańczyk,Gabriele Vedovato,Michele Zanolin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了引力波探测器校准误差对爆发信号搜索的影响，发现虽然校准误差会改变检测统计量，但对探测效率的影响不足1%，在当前探测器灵敏度下影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的爆发信号搜索未充分考虑校准误差的频率依赖性，特别是对核塌缩超新星这类宽频带、时频形态复杂的信号，需要系统评估真实校准误差的影响。

Method: 结合解析建模与大规模注入研究，首先通过一阶估计量化频率相关幅度和相位误差对相干网络SNR和相关系数的影响，再通过注入实验验证理论预测。

Result: 校准误差确实改变了检测统计量，但主要贡献来自间接改变事件选择的时间-频率像素数量；所有测试波形的探测效率随距离变化差异小于1%，爆炸能量极限仍由天体物理不确定性主导。

Conclusion: 在当前探测器灵敏度下，真实校准误差对宽带引力波爆发信号的可探测性影响极小，参数估计的影响留待未来研究。

Abstract: Imperfections in the calibration of gravitational wave observatories introduce frequency dependent amplitude and phase errors on the measured GW signal. Previous unmodelled burst searches have approximated these effects using prescriptions such as a uniform amplitude rescaling or a constant time shift, which do not capture the frequency-dependent structure of calibration errors. This limitation is problematic for core-collapse supernovae, whose predicted GW signals occupy a wide frequency band and exhibit complex time-frequency morphology. In this work, we investigate how realistic calibration errors affect burst search pipelines by combining analytical modelling with large-scale injection campaigns. First-order estimates are derived to quantify how frequency-dependent amplitude and phase errors influence detection statistics such as the coherent network SNR and the correlation coefficient. These calculations predict that the relative impact on the coherent network SNR scales with the signal strength until it reaches an asymptotic value. The effect on the correlation coefficient is most pronounced near the detection threshold and is entirely suppressed at high SNR ratio. Injection studies confirm that calibration errors do modify the detection statistics, but show that the dominant contribution arises indirectly through changes in the number of time-frequency pixels selected in an event. Despite these measurable variations, detection efficiencies as a function of distance differ by less than one percent across all tested waveforms, and explosion-energy limits remain dominated by astrophysical uncertainties rather than calibration uncertainty. These results demonstrate that, at current detector sensitivity, realistic calibration errors have minimal impact on the detectability of broadband GW burst signals. The impact of calibration errors on parameter estimation is left for future work.

</details>


### [201] [Shadow of the generalized Vaidya black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17483)
*Vitalii Vertogradov,Ali Övgün*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究广义Vaidya黑洞（Husain解）的阴影，通过共形静态变换推导零测地线的有效势和同宿光子曲面，分析了状态方程参数α对阴影大小的影响，并推导了时变情况下的准静态演化方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义Vaidya黑洞（Husain解）的阴影特性，特别是额外物质的状态方程参数α如何影响黑洞阴影的大小，以及时变情况下阴影的演化规律。

Method: 采用共形静态变换方法，将Husain度规中具有同宿Killing矢量的自相似类变换为共形静态形式，推导零测地线的有效势和同宿光子曲面，再变换回原坐标；对时变情形推导准静态慢演化方程。

Result: 弱能量条件分支中，参数α决定阴影相对于Vaidya情形的大小：0≤α<1/2分支增大光子球和阴影半径，1/2<α≤1分支减小它们；时变情形下阴影的增大或收缩由光子区域的局部有效流入决定，而非Ṁ和Ṇ的符号单独决定。

Conclusion: 建立了广义Vaidya黑洞阴影与状态方程参数的定量关系，揭示了阴影演化的局部有效流入判据，为理解动态黑洞阴影的物理机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the shadow of the generalized Vaidya black hole described by the Husain solution. Assuming a barotropic equation of state for the additional matter sector, we first identify the self-similar class admitting a homothetic Killing vector and transform the geometry to a conformally static form. This allows us to derive the effective potential for null geodesics in the conformally static representative, determine the corresponding homothetic photon surface, and then transform the result back to the original generalized Vaidya coordinates. We show that, within the weak-energy-condition branches, the parameter $α$ controlling the equation of state determines whether the shadow is enlarged or reduced relative to the Vaidya case: the branch $0\leqα<1/2$ increases the photon-sphere and shadow radii, whereas the branch $1/2<α\leq 1$ decreases them. We then turn to the genuinely time-dependent Husain metric and derive quasistatic slow-evolution equations for the instantaneous photon surface and critical impact parameter. In particular, we show that the growth or contraction of the shadow is governed by the local effective influx in the photon region rather than by the signs of $\dot M$ and $\dot N$ separately. For the charge-like branch, this yields a transparent criterion for when accretion enlarges the shadow and when rapid charge growth instead causes it to shrink.

</details>


### [202] [Evaporation and fate of covariant quantum black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17520)
*Li-Shuai Wang,Xiangdong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了协变量子黑洞对不同自旋粒子的蒸发过程，发现其霍金辐射和质量损失率与史瓦西黑洞不同，且依赖于发射粒子的自旋，表明仅考虑无质量标量场情况可能不足，并为未来检验圈量子引力提供了可能途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多的现象和理论问题表明量子引力理论是必要的，因此需要研究量子黑洞的蒸发特性以深入理解量子引力效应。

Method: 研究了协变量子黑洞对不同自旋粒子的蒸发过程，并将结果与史瓦西黑洞情形进行对比分析。

Result: 协变量子黑洞的霍金辐射和质量损失率与史瓦西黑洞不同，且依赖于发射粒子的自旋。

Conclusion: 仅考虑无质量标量场情形来描述黑洞蒸发可能是不充分的；这些结果为未来检验圈量子引力提供了潜在方法。

Abstract: A growing number of phenomena and theoretical problems indicate that quantum gravity theory is necessary. In this paper, we investigate the evaporation of covariant quantum BHs for particles with different spins and compare the results with the Schwarzschild case. Our results show that the Hawking radiation and mass loss rate of covariant quantum BHs differ from those of Schwarzschild BHs and they depend on the spins of the emitted particles. Therefore, these results suggest that it may be insufficient to consider only BH evaporation in the massless scalar field case and may provide a possible way to test loop quantum gravity in the future.

</details>


### [203] [Massive Compact Stars Beyond the General Relativity Limit in Finslerian Gravity: A Possible Explanation for the GW190814 Secondary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17609)
*Praveen J,Rajesh Kumar,Sadaf Fatima,S K Narasimhamurthy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Finsler引力框架下，利用Heintzmann IIa引力势和线性状态方程构建各向异性致密星模型，成功将最大质量提升至2.67 M⊙，为GW190814事件中质量处于中子星-黑洞质量间隙的天体提供了解释，并分析了转动惯量、质量-半径等可观测量与GR的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW190814事件发现了一个质量为2.5-2.67 M⊙的致密天体，处于传统中子星最大质量与黑洞最小质量之间的间隙中，挑战了标准恒星演化模型。本文旨在探索Finsler修正引力理论能否无需引入黑洞形成即可容纳这类超大质量致密星。

Method: 在Finsler引力框架下，采用Heintzmann IIa引力势描述致密星内部结构，假设线性状态方程并引入各向异性压强，构建静态球对称致密星模型。通过求解修正的引力场方程得到质量-半径关系、转动惯量-质量关系等可观测量，并与GR对应结果进行对比，同时进行稳定性检验。

Result: Finsler引力修正可显著提高致密星的最大质量至2.67 M⊙，能够解释GW190814的次级天体；转动惯量相比GR结果增大，表明更强的旋转支撑和内部质量分布重构。模型通过了稳定性判据检验，物理上自洽。

Conclusion: Finsler引力提供了一种替代黑洞解释的可行方案，能够在不违背广义相对论基本预测（存在质量上限）的前提下，容纳位于质量间隙中的超高质量致密天体，对理解中子星-黑洞质量间隙和致密双星并合产物具有重要启示。

Abstract: The existence of an upper mass limit for compact stars is one of the fundamental predictions of general relativity (GR), with important implications for the outcome of compact binary mergers and the nature of the proposed neutron star black hole mass gap. The LIGO Virgo collaboration announced the discovery of a compact binary merger, GW190814, containing a compact star with mass 2.5 to 2.67 $M_\odot$ [R. Abbott et al.(2020) ApJ Lett., 896, L44], which provided an exciting new stimulus to the ongoing debate on whether a gap exists between the maximum mass of NS and the minimum mass of black hole. Such GW detection has also challenged conventional stellar models and renewed interest in exploring whether modified theories of gravity can accommodate such ultra-massive compact objects without invoking black hole formation. The present work investigated the structure and physical properties of compact stars within the framework of Finslerian gravity, employing the Heintzmann IIa gravitational potentials and showed that the our model can substantially enhance the maximum mass of compact stars upto $2.67 M_{\odot}$, thereby offering a plausible explanation for massive compact objects such as the secondary component of GW190814. In addition, the moment of inertia is found to increase, indicating stronger rotational support and a redistribution of the internal mass profile. The analysis focuses on key astrophysical observables, including the mass radius and moment of inertia mass relations, and a detailed comparison with the GR counterpart is performed. Assuming a linear equation of state, we construct a class of physically viable anisotropic stellar models and analyze their behavior under the influence of Finslerian corrections. The physical viability of the model is rigorously tested through stability criteria.

</details>


### [204] [Harmonic oscillation and orbital morphology for spinning charged quantum corrected black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17658)
*Rameez Khalid,Muhammad Yasir,Shahid Qaisar,Faisal Javed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究围绕旋转带电爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-狄拉顿（EMd）量子修正黑洞的测试粒子动力学行为，分析了自旋参数、量子修正参数和电荷参数对有效势、有效力、圆形轨道稳定性以及振荡频率（径向、垂直、角向）和进动频率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究旋转带电EMd量子修正黑洞的测试粒子动力学行为，揭示量子修正和电荷参数对强引力场中圆形轨道稳定性与结构的影响机制。

Method: 基于EMd时空几何，通过分析有效势和有效力判定圆形轨道的稳定性，计算径向、垂直和角向振荡频率以及近心点进动和Lense-Thirring进动频率。

Result: 自旋参数决定时空旋转特性，量子修正和电荷参数在事件视界附近引入对Kerr-Newman几何的额外偏差，改变了圆形轨道的位置和稳定性，且频谱与粒子运动有效关联。

Conclusion: 研究结果为通过未来高精度天体物理观测全面描述旋转EMd量子修正黑洞的轨道动力学提供了理论依据。

Abstract: This study investigates the dynamical behavior of particle's test around a spinning charged Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton (EMd) quantum corrected black hole (QCBH) by examining the effects of spinning parameter $a$, quantum correction parameter $b$, and charge parameter $Q$. Using EMd spacetime geometry, we analyze the effective potential and effective force to determine the stability and structure of the circular orbits in a strong gravitational field. Furthermore, we calculate the oscillation frequencies radial $(Ω_r)$, vertical $(Ω_θ)$, and angular $(Ω_φ)$, as well as the corresponding Periapsis and Lense-Thirring precession frequencies. The spin parameter primarily determines the spinning properties of spacetime, while the quantum correction and charge particle introduce additional biases to Kerr-Newman geometry, particularly near the event horizon. These corrections alter the position and stability of the circular orbits, and the spectrum effectively associates with particle's motion. The results provide a comprehensive description of the orbital dynamics of spinning EMd QCBH through future high-precision astrophysical observations.

</details>


### [205] [Angular momentum flux through post-Newtonian order for noncircular nonspinning black-hole binaries in Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17887)
*Qi Tan,Peng-Cheng Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文计算了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-狄拉顿理论中无自旋双黑洞系统在一般非圆轨道上的瞬时角动量通量，达到了相对论第一后牛顿阶精度，并分别得到了标量、电磁和张量通道的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-狄拉顿理论中的双黑洞系统提供完整的耗散信息，特别是角动量通量的后牛顿计算，以支持未来轨道平均偏心演化和波形定相研究。

Method: 在爱因斯坦框架下，采用松弛场方程直接积分方法，构造波区中标量、电磁和张量引力场，在质心系中表达源多极矩，并保留波区中$1/R^2$的有限距离修正项。

Result: 分别得到了标量、电磁和张量通道对角动量通量的贡献；标量和电磁通道始于偶极辐射，张量通道始于四极辐射；恢复了准圆平衡关系、已知广义相对论极限以及标量和电磁偶极抑制极限。

Conclusion: 与已知能量通量结果共同为爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-狄拉顿理论中偏心演化和波形定相的进一步研究提供了必要的耗散信息。

Abstract: We compute the instantaneous angular momentum flux from nonspinning black hole binaries in Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory for generic noncircular orbits up to relative first post-Newtonian order. Working in the Einstein frame and using the direct integration of the relaxed field equations approach, we construct the scalar, electromagnetic, and tensor gravitational fields in the wave zone and express the required source multipole moments in the center-of-mass frame. In addition to the leading $1/R$ radiative fields, we retain the next-to-leading $1/R^2$ terms in the wave-zone fields, which describe finite-distance corrections and do not contribute to the flux at null infinity. We obtain separately the scalar, electromagnetic, and tensor contributions to the angular momentum flux. The scalar and electromagnetic channels begin with dipole radiation, while the tensor channel begins at quadrupole order. Our results recover the quasicircular balance relation, the known general-relativistic limit, and the expected scalar- and electromagnetic-dipole suppression limits. Together with the previously known energy flux, these results provide the dissipative information required for future studies of orbit-averaged eccentric evolution and waveform phasing in Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory.

</details>


### [206] [Defocusing dark energy: Raychaudhuri diagnostics beyond $w<-1/3$ and the phantom divide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18008)
*Özgür Akarsu,Antonio De Felice,N. Merve Uzun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出在广义相对论中，宇宙加速由共动汇的超曲面时间型发散（即负总主动引力质量密度 $\mathcal{M}_{\rm tot}<0$）驱动。对于符号变化的暗能量（如 $\Lambda_{\rm s}$CDM 模型），应使用符号化的密度 $\rho_{\rm de}$ 和两个分支无关组合 $\mathcal{I}_{\rm de}$、$\mathcal{M}_{\rm de}$ 替代传统的状态方程参数 $w_{\rm de}$。证明了当暗能量分量单独守恒且密度经历有限奇次阶光滑穿越时，$\mathcal{I}_{\rm de}$ 和 $\mathcal{M}_{\rm de}$ 在穿越点附近为负，而 $w_{\rm de}$ 产生运动学极点。推导了加速穿越的精确条件，并指出在总 NEC 满足下减速参数有一或三个变号零点。平滑 $\Lambda_{\rm s}$CDM 剖面、指数红外 $f(T)$ 模型和最小幻影膜模型验证了这些结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统使用 $w_{\rm de}<-1/3$ 作为暗能量排斥判据在 $\rho_{\rm de}$ 变号时失效（不等式反转，比值变量在零点无定义），因此需要更稳健的物理量来刻画符号变化暗能量（如 $\Lambda_{\rm s}$CDM）的排斥效应和加速动力学。

Method: 理论推导与数值验证。基于Raychaudhuri方程，定义符号化密度$\rho_{\rm de}$、正規零能条件边界$\mathcal{I}_{\rm de}=\rho_{\rm de}+p_{\rm de}$和主动引力质量$\mathcal{M}_{\rm de}=\rho_{\rm de}+3p_{\rm de}$，证明其替代$w_{\rm de}$的普适性质。对有限奇次阶光滑穿越的暗能量分量，解析推导$w_{\rm de}$极点的留数、$\mathcal{I}_{\rm de}$和$\mathcal{M}_{\rm de}$的符号行为，以及加速穿越的精确条件。在$\Lambda_{\rm s}$CDM、$f(T)$和幻影膜模型中进行验证。

Result: (1) 证明对有限奇次阶$n$的光滑穿越，$\mathcal{I}_{\rm de}$和$\mathcal{M}_{\rm de}$在穿越点附近为负，穿越点处非正；$w_{\rm de}$产生运动学极点，留数为$n(1+z_\dagger)/3$。(2) 若高红移处$\mathcal{M}_{\rm de}>0$，连续性要求至少存在一个排斥边界$z_{\rm rep}>z_\dagger$，即$\rho_{\rm de}<0$时扇区已呈排斥性。(3) 导出穿越点处$\rho_{\rm de}'(z_\dagger)$的精确加速范围，满足总NEC。(4) 单脉冲和驻点假设下，减速参数有一或三个变号零点。

Conclusion: 晚时期宇宙学推断应围绕$(\rho,p,\mathcal{I},\mathcal{M})$而非$w$进行组织，以正确处理符号变化暗能量中的排斥效应和加速动力学。

Abstract: In general relativity, cosmic acceleration is timelike defocusing of the comoving congruence and requires a negative total active gravitational mass density, $\mathcal{M}_{\rm tot}=ρ_{\rm tot}+3p_{\rm tot}<0$. The criterion $w\equiv p/ρ<-1/3$ diagnoses sector repulsion only for $ρ>0$: the inequality reverses for $ρ<0$, and ratio variables are ill-defined at $ρ=0$ even when the stress-energy tensor is finite. For sign-changing effective dark energy (DE), as in $Λ_{\rm s}$CDM-type histories, we instead use the signed density $ρ_{\rm de}$ and two branch-independent combinations. The regular null energy condition (NEC) boundary $\mathcal{I}_{\rm de}=ρ_{\rm de}+p_{\rm de}=0$ replaces the phantom divide $w_{\rm de}=-1$, while $\mathcal{M}_{\rm de}=ρ_{\rm de}+3p_{\rm de}<0$ governs sector-level Raychaudhuri repulsion. For a separately conserved DE sector with a smooth negative-to-positive density crossing of finite odd order $n$ at $z_\dagger$, we prove that $\mathcal{I}_{\rm de}$ and $\mathcal{M}_{\rm de}$ are negative in a punctured neighborhood and non-positive at the crossing, while $w_{\rm de}$ develops a kinematic pole with universal residue $n(1+z_\dagger)/3$. If $\mathcal{M}_{\rm de}>0$ at some sufficiently high redshift, continuity requires at least one repulsion boundary $z_{\rm rep}>z_\dagger$: the sector is already repulsive while $ρ_{\rm de}<0$. We derive the exact range of $ρ_{\rm de}'(z_\dagger)$ for acceleration at the crossing with the total NEC satisfied. Under the stated single-impulse and stationary-point assumptions, the deceleration parameter has one or three sign-changing zeros. A smooth $Λ_{\rm s}$CDM profile, an exponential infrared $f(T)$ model, and the minimal phantom brane illustrate the results. These results motivate organizing late-time inference around $(ρ,p,\mathcal{I},\mathcal{M})$ rather than around $w$ alone.

</details>


### [207] [Post-Newtonian Global Conservation Laws in the Presence of Sensitive Bodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18145)
*Timothy Clifton,Asta Heinesen,Oliver Pitt*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文扩展了后牛顿（PPN）框架下的半保守和全保守引力理论，使其适用于具有环境敏感性的致密天体（即违反强等效原理的情形），证明了在存在敏感性的情况下仍可存在全局守恒量，并推导了其显式形式，同时给出了敏感体存在时PPN度规系数的新表达方式，并通过标量-张量理论验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPN形式体系可对弱场引力进行理论无关检验，但未能有效处理违反强等效原理的理论中致密天体对环境敏感的情形，因此需要扩展半保守和全保守引力理论的概念以涵盖此类情况。

Method: 将半保守和全保守引力理论的概念扩展到包含环境敏感质量的致密天体情形，推导存在敏感性时全局守恒量的存在条件及显式形式，重新定义敏感体存在时PPN度规系数的表达方式，并与标量-张量引力理论中的已知结果进行对比验证。

Result: 证明了在存在环境敏感性的情况下仍可存在全局守恒量，并给出了其显式形式；发现了敏感体存在时PPN度规系数的新写法；通过标量-张量理论验证了方法的适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作将PPN框架拓展至违反强等效原理的理论，为更广泛的引力理论检验提供了理论基础，特别是涉及致密天体的系统中全局守恒律的有效性得以保留。

Abstract: The parameterized post-Newtonian (PPN) approach is the state of the art formalism for performing theory independent tests of weak-field gravity, and for constraining possible deviations from Einstein's theory. Within this framework, global conservation laws are useful for the calculation of dynamics and for giving meaning to parameters. In this paper we extend the concept of semi-conservative and fully-conservative theories of gravity to include situations in which compact astrophysical bodies are modeled as masses that are sensitive to their local environment, as relevant for theories that violate the strong equivalence principle. We find that globally conserved quantities can still exist in the presence of such sensitivities, and find their explicit forms when they do. We identify new ways of writing the coefficients that enter into the PPN metric when a theory of gravity admits conserved quantities in the presence of a sensitive body, and demonstrate the applicability of our approach by comparing it to known results in scalar-tensor theories of gravity.

</details>


### [208] [Search for continuous gravitational waves from the pulsar J0435+3233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18219)
*Brian McGloughlin,Jing Ming,Maria Alessandra Papa,Kartikey Sharma,Reinhard Prix,Benjamin Steltner,Heinz-Bernd Eggenstein,Na Wang,Jianping Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用LIGO O4a公开数据搜索来自毫秒脉冲星J0435+3233的连续引力波。J0435+3233自旋下变频率异常大（>10^{-12} Hz/s），是潜在强引力波源。在自转频率两倍处、窄带及r模信号搜索均未探测到信号。95%置信度下引力波振幅上限h0比自旋下限低约14倍，椭圆率上限约束在10^{-8}量级，首次在物理感兴趣范围内将自旋下限超越一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: J0435+3233是迄今唯一在亚10ms自旋周期范围内自旋下变频率超过10^{-12} Hz/s的毫秒脉冲星，其巨大的自旋下变频率意味着能量损失可能通过引力辐射主导，因此是寻找连续引力波的极佳候选体。

Method: 使用LIGO O4a公开数据，在三个目标频段进行搜索：恰好两倍自转频率、该频率附近窄带、以及对应r模信号的频率。通过贝叶斯或频率域方法设定95%置信度上限。

Result: 未探测到连续引力波信号。95%置信度下引力波振幅上限h0比自旋下限低约14倍，椭圆率上限约束在10^{-8}量级（低10^{-8}区域）。

Conclusion: J0435+3233是首个自旋下限被超越一个数量级以上、且椭圆率被约束到物理感兴趣的低10^{-8}范围的引力波源，表明该源可能不是单纯由引力波辐射主导自旋下变频。

Abstract: We perform a search for continuous gravitational waves from J0435+3233 using LIGO O4a public data. J0435+3233 is unique among millisecond pulsars as it exhibits an exceptionally large spin-down and marks the first pulsar observed to date with a spin-down larger than $10^{-12}$ Hz/s in the sub $10$ ms spin period range, making it a potentially strong source of continuous gravitational waves. We target signals at exactly twice the rotation frequency, a narrow band around this frequency, and also signals corresponding to r-modes. Our results are consistent with a non-detection. Our most stringent upper limit on the intrinsic gravitational wave amplitude is $h_0=\targetedULninetyfive$, at the 95\% confidence level, which is a factor of $\approx14$ times lower than the spin-down limit. With an estimated source distance of $1.2$ kpc our upper limit on the gravitational wave amplitude excludes source ellipticities greater than $\targetedellipULninetyfive$, making this the first source for which the spin-down upper limit is beaten by over an order of magnitude {\it{and}} the ellipticity is constrained to a physically interesting range, in the low $10^{-8}$ region.

</details>
