### [209] [The exceptional series through the fifth tensor power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16353)
*Bruce Westbury*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文为例外单李代数的伴随表示的前五个张量幂给出了统一结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究例外单李代数的张量幂结构，特别是前五个张量幂的统一描述，有助于深入理解其表示理论和代数结构。

Method: 通过构造统一的结构框架，系统地分析了例外单李代数伴随表示的前五个张量幂的分解与组成。

Result: 成功给出了前五个张量幂的统一结构描述。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解例外单李代数的表示结构提供了统一的视角，并为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We give a uniform structure for the first five tensor powers of the adjoint representation of an exceptional simple Lie algebra.

</details>


### [210] [Twisted super Yangians of quasi-split type A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16594)
*Kang Lu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入了拟分裂A型扭曲超Yangian $Y^{\mathfrak{s}}_{\imath}$的Drinfeld当前表示，证明了其与通过R矩阵定义的扭曲超Yangian $\mathscr{SY}^{\mathfrak{s}}$同构，并建立了PBW定理及中心描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将拟分裂A型扭曲Yangian的Drinfeld当前表示推广到超情形，建立不同对称奇偶序列下代数之间的同构关系，并推广已知的非超拟分裂A型结果。

Method: 通过Gauss分解方法建立Drinfeld当前表示与R矩阵表示之间的同构关系，利用对称奇偶序列的代数结构证明同构性，并借助Gaussian生成元描述中心。

Result: 证明了$Y^{\mathfrak{s}}_{\imath}$与$\mathscr{SY}^{\mathfrak{s}}$同构；不同对称奇偶序列但相同$\mathfrak{m}$和$\mathfrak{n}$的代数同构；建立了PBW定理；描述了$\mathscr{Y}^{\mathfrak{s}}$的中心。

Conclusion: 成功将拟分裂A型扭曲Yangian的Drinfeld表示推广到超代数情形，为后续研究提供了基础工具。

Abstract: We introduce the twisted super Yangian $Y^{\mathfrak{s}}_{\imath}$ of quasi-split type A in the Drinfeld current presentation for an arbitrary symmetric parity sequence $\mathfrak{s}$. We prove via Gauss decomposition that $Y^{\mathfrak{s}}_{\imath}$ is isomorphic to the special twisted super Yangian $\mathscr{SY}^{\mathfrak{s}}$, a subalgebra of the twisted super Yangian $\mathscr{Y}^{\mathfrak{s}}$ previously defined via the R-matrix presentation. As a corollary, we show that the algebras $Y^{\mathfrak{s}}_{\imath}$ corresponding to different symmetric parity sequences with the same $\mathfrak{m}$ and $\mathfrak{n}$ are isomorphic. We also establish a PBW theorem for $Y^{\mathfrak{s}}_{\imath}$ and describe the center of $\mathscr{Y}^{\mathfrak{s}}$ in terms of Gaussian generators, thereby generalizing known results for the nonsuper quasi-split type A case.

</details>


### [211] [Tensor Powers of Indecomposable Modules for $F\frak{S}_p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16626)
*Manzu Kua,Kay Jin Lim*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文基于arXiv:2603.11533中给出的显式张量积公式（模投射），研究群代数F\mathfrak{S}_p上的模的张量幂，给出了特定情况下不可分解直和的组合描述，并分析了当素数趋于无穷时这些重数的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先前工作arXiv:2603.11533已给出模投射的张量积公式的基础上，进一步研究该公式在张量幂中的应用，特别是探索模的张量幂分解中不可分解直和项的重数的组合结构与渐近性质。

Method: 利用arXiv:2603.11533中的显式张量积公式，对特定情形下的模张量幂进行分解，通过组合方法描述不可分解直和项的重数，并分析素数趋于无穷时的渐近行为。

Result: 在特定情形下，给出了不可分解直和项重数的组合描述，并得到了这些重数在素数趋于无穷时的渐近结果。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了模张量积公式的应用，深化了对对称群模表示中张量幂分解结构的理解，尤其揭示了素数增大时的渐近规律。

Abstract: In the earlier paper arXiv:2603.11533, the authors gave an explicit tensor product formula, modulo projectives, for modules over the group algebra $F\frak{S}_p$. In this paper, we use this formula to study tensor powers of such modules. In certain cases, we give combinatorial descriptions of the multiplicities of the indecomposable summands appearing in their direct-sum decompositions. We also study the asymptotic behaviour of these multiplicities as the underlying prime tends to infinity.

</details>


### [212] [Cyclotomic character fields and sets of primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16732)
*Eugenio Giannelli,J. Miquel Martínez,Carolina Vallejo*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于π={2, q}（q为奇素数），任意有限群G的主p-块（p∈π）总含有一个非平凡不可约特征标，其次数不被2或q整除，且值域包含在q次分圆扩张中。该结果统一并推广了Navarro-Tiep与Giannelli-Hung-Schaeffer Fry-Vallejo的结论到主块情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已知的关于群特征标次数与值域的限制结果（Navarro-Tiep和Giannelli等的工作）从整体群推广到主p-块（principal p-block）的局部设定，探究主块中是否存在满足特定算术和域条件的非平凡不可约特征标。

Method: 利用有限群模表示论中的块论（block theory）、特征标理论以及分圆域上的算术性质，结合前人在整体群上的技术方法，将其适配到主块框架中完成证明。

Result: 在π={2, q}且q为奇素数的条件下，有限群G的主p-块（p∈π）必存在非平凡不可约特征标，其次数不被2和q整除，且值域在Q(ζ_q)（q次分圆域）中。

Conclusion: 该结果统一了Navarro-Tiep和Giannelli-Hung-Schaeffer Fry-Vallejo的结论在主块语境下的版本，揭示了主块特征标在次数和域结构上受素数2和q约束的深层规律。

Abstract: Let $π=\{ 2, q \}$ where $q$ is an odd prime. Let $G$ be a finite group of order divisible by a prime $p \in π$. We show that the principal $p$-block of $G$ contains a nontrivial irreducible character of degree not divisible by $2$ nor $q$ and with field of values contained in the $q$th cyclotomic extension. This statement simultaneously provides a principal block version of results of Navarro--Tiep and Giannelli--Hung--Schaeffer Fry--Vallejo.

</details>


### [213] [Clifford and Weyl algebras in symmetric tensor categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16910)
*Pavel Etingof*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在对称张量范畴中研究 Clifford 和 Weyl 代数，建立了非退化形式下的单性和 Azumaya 性质，在 Verlinde 范畴中具体计算，并引入辛 Witt 群作为 Brauer 群的子群，最终对有限群表示范畴给出了用第二 Stiefel-Whitney 类表达的显式描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般对称张量范畴中系统地建立 Clifford 和 Weyl 代数的结构理论（单性、Azumaya 性质），并利用它们研究 Brauer 群的结构，特别是引入并刻画辛 Witt 子群。

Method: 利用对称张量范畴的范畴论框架，在非退化（斜）对称双线性型条件下分析 Clifford/Weyl 代数的结构；在 Verlinde 范畴 Ver_p 中进行具体计算；结合 Frobenius 正合性条件证明 Azumaya 性质；通过第二 Stiefel-Whitney 类将辛 Witt 群与正交表示相联系。

Result: 1）在特征≠2的代数闭域上，对称张量范畴中带有非退化（斜）对称型的对象的 Clifford 和 Weyl 代数满足单性和 Azumaya 性质；2）在 Verlinde 范畴中具体计算了这些代数；3）对 Frobenius 正合范畴，有限对称代数的辛对象的 Weyl 代数是 Azumaya 的；4）引入辛 Witt 群 SW(C) 作为 Brauer 群的子群；5）对 G 为有限群且阶与特征互素的情形，SW(Rep(G)⊠sVec) 可用正交表示的第二 Stiefel-Whitney 类表示。

Conclusion: 将 Clifford/Weyl 代数理论从向量空间推广到一般对称张量范畴，建立了其结构定理，并利用它们揭示了 Brauer 群中辛 Witt 子群的结构，对有限群表示范畴给出了具体的同调刻画。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal C$ be a symmetric tensor category over an algebraically closed field $\mathbf k$ of characteristic $\ne 2$. We study Clifford and Weyl algebras of objects of $\mathcal C$ with a (skew-)symmetric bilinear form. When the form is non-degenerate, we establish simplicity and the Azumaya property for such algebras under suitable assumptions. We also compute Clifford and Weyl algebras in the Verlinde category ${\rm Ver}_p$ and use them to prove that if $\mathcal C$ is Frobenius exact then the Weyl algebra of a symplectic object of $\mathcal C$ with finite symmetric algebra is Azumaya. Using this, we introduce the symplectic Witt group $\mathcal S\mathcal W(\mathcal C)$, the subgroup of the Brauer group ${\rm Br}(\mathcal C)$ consisting of Morita classes of such Azumaya algebras, and when $\mathcal C={\rm Rep}(G)\boxtimes{\rm sVec}$ for a finite group $G$ of order coprime to ${\rm char}(\mathbf k)$, express $\mathcal S\mathcal W(\mathcal C)$ in terms of second Stiefel-Whitney classes of orthogonal representations of $G$.

</details>


### [214] [Branching rule for $SL_{n+m}(\mathbb{C})\supset SL_n(\mathbb{C})\times SL_m(\mathbb{C})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16762)
*Andrei Gornitskii*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了 $SL_{n+m}(\mathbb{C}) \supset SL_n(\mathbb{C}) \times SL_m(\mathbb{C})$ 的 branching 问题，通过半群 $\Sigma_{n,m}$ 描述了相应的分支规则，该半群由某个多面体锥中的整点构成，并给出了定义该锥的不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典李群约化中分支规则是核心问题。$SL_{n+m}$ 限制到 $SL_n \times SL_m$ 的分支规则虽有经典结果（如 Littlewood-Richardson 规则），但作者希望以一种新的、基于半群与多面体锥的视角来统一描述，揭示其组合几何结构。

Method: 引入半群 $\Sigma_{n,m} \subset \Lambda^+ \times \mathbb{Z}^N$，其中 $\Lambda^+$ 是 $SL_{n+m}$  dominant weights 的半群，$N$ 是 $SL_{n+m}$ 极大幂幺子群的维数。对每个 dominant weight $\lambda \in \Lambda^+$，$\Sigma_{n,m}$ 中具有 dominant weight $\lambda$ 的元素参数化 $V(\lambda)|_{SL_n \times SL_m}$ 中的不可约表示。进一步将 $\Sigma_{n,m}$ 刻画为某个多面体锥中的整点半群，并推导出定义该锥的显式不等式。

Result: 得到了 $\Sigma_{n,m}$ 作为多面体锥中整点半群的精确描述，并找到了定义该锥的一组不等式，从而给出了 $SL_{n+m} \downarrow SL_n \times SL_m$ 分支规则的一个新的组合几何刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作为 $SL_{n+m}$ 到 $SL_n \times SL_m$ 的分支问题提供了一种基于半群与多面体锥的简洁描述，推广了经典的分支规则，并为研究更一般约化对的分支问题提供了框架。

Abstract: We study the branching problem for the pair $SL_{n+m}(\mathbb{C})\supset SL_n(\mathbb{C})\times SL_m(\mathbb{C})$. We describe the corresponding branching rule in terms of a semigroup $Σ_{n,m}\subsetΛ^{+}\times\mathbb{Z}^N$, where $Λ^{+}$ is the semigroup of dominant weights of $SL_{n+m}$, and $N$ is the dimension of maximal unipotent subgroup in $SL_{n+m}$. Let $V(λ)$ be the irreducible representation of $SL_{n+m}$ with the highest weight $λ$. For every dominant weight $λ\inΛ^{+}$ the set of all $σ\inΣ_{n,m}$ with dominant weight $λ$ parametrizes the irreducible representations in the restriction $V(λ)|_{SL_n\times SL_m}$ of $V(λ)$ to $SL_{n}\times SL_{m}$. We describe the semigroup $Σ_{n,m}$ as the semigroup of integral points in some polyhedral cone and we find the inequalities defining this cone.

</details>


### [215] [Iwahori component of the Gelfand--Graev representation for reductive groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17163)
*Yi Luo*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于p-adic域上的连通约化群G，Gelfand-Graev表示中Iwahori不动子空间作为Iwahori-Hecke代数模同构于H ⊗_{H_W0} sgn，将Chan-Savin定理从分裂群推广到所有连通约化群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Chan-Savin关于分裂群的Gelfand-Graev表示中Iwahori不动子空间结构的定理推广到更一般的连通约化群上。

Method: 利用Iwahori-Hecke代数H及其有限子代数H_W0（对应于相对Weyl群）的模结构，分析深度为零的非退化特征下I-不动向量的空间结构，建立与张量积H ⊗_{H_W0} sgn的同构。

Result: 对于任意连通约化群G，Gelfand-Graev表示中I-不动子空间作为H-模同构于H ⊗_{H_W0} sgn。

Conclusion: 成功将Chan-Savin定理从分裂群推广到所有连通约化群，统一了该领域的重要结果。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected reductive group over a $p$-adic field $F$, $U$ the unipotent radical of a minimal parabolic subgroup, $ψ$ a depth-zero non-degenerate character of $U(F)$, and $I$ an Iwahori subgroup of $G(F)$. We show that, as a module over the Iwahori-Hecke algebra ${H}$, the space of $I$-fixed vectors in the Gelfand-Graev representation $\mathrm{ind}_U^Gψ$ is isomorphic to ${H} \otimes_{{H}_{W_0}} \mathrm{sgn}$. Here $\mathrm{sgn}$ is the sign representation of the finite Hecke subalgebra ${H}_{W_0}$ attached to the relative Weyl group. This extends the theorem of Chan-Savin from split groups to all connected reductive groups.

</details>


### [216] [On a super-Virasoro group, a semigroup of annuli, and Gauss--Berezin integral operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17168)
*Yury A. Neretin*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Neveu-Schwarz李超代数的Grassmann化及其对应的超群NS(𝒜)，并构造了其复化半群Γ(𝒜)，该半群元素是带有接触结构的1|1维超环，在参数不等式条件下证明了NS(𝒜)的酉表示可延拓至Γ(𝒜)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在显式刻画Neveu-Schwarz超群的结构，并建立其酉表示到复化半群的延拓理论，为超对称共形场论中的表示论提供数学基础。

Method: 采用Grassmann代数生成元方法，通过张量积构造李超代数的Grassmann化，进而定义超群；利用接触结构定义超环的复化半群，并研究表示延拓条件。

Result: 给出了NS(𝒜)超群的多种显式描述；构造了复化半群Γ(𝒜)；在特定参数不等式下建立了酉表示从NS(𝒜)到Γ(𝒜)的延拓。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了Neveu-Schwarz超群及其复化半群结构，建立了表示延拓定理，无需读者具备李超代数和超群的先验知识。

Abstract: Denote by ${\mathcal A}$ the Grassmann algebra with a countable number of generators, by ${\mathcal A}_{\overline 0}$, ${\mathcal A}_{\overline 1}$ its even and odd parts. We consider supergroups as groups over $\mathcal A$.
  Consider the Neveu--Schwarz Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{ns}= \mathfrak{ns}_{\overline 0}\oplus \mathfrak{ns}_{\overline 1}$. Consider its Grassmannization $\mathfrak{ns}(\mathcal{A}):= (\mathfrak{ns}_{\overline 0}\otimes {\mathcal A}_{\overline 0}) \oplus (\mathfrak{ns}_{\overline 1}\otimes {\mathcal A}_{\overline 1})$ and the corresponding supergroup $\mathrm{NS}(\mathcal {A})$. We describe this group explicitly in different ways. Next, we define a complexification $Γ({\mathcal A})$ of $\mathrm{NS}({\mathcal A})$. It is a semigroup, whose elements are superannuli of dimension $1|1$ equipped with contact structures; the multiplication is gluing of such superannuli. Under some inequalities for parameters, we show that unitary representations of $\mathrm{NS}({\mathcal A})$ admit extensions to representations of $Γ(\mathcal {A})$. We do not assume that the reader has prior knowledge of Lie superalgebras and supergroups.

</details>


### [217] [Nilpotent quiver varieties with multiplicities and symmetrisable crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17301)
*Victoria Hoskins,Joshua Jackson,Tanguy Vernet*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构建了带多重边的Nakajima幂零箭图变体的类似物，通过发展新的稳定性条件并利用Hecke对应关系，将Saito关于Kac-Moody代数不可约最高权表示的Kashiwara晶体的几何构造从对称情形推广到可对称化情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Saito的Kashiwara晶体几何构造从对称Kac-Moody代数推广到更一般的可对称化Kac-Moody代数。

Method: 1) 构建带多重边的箭图的Nakajima幂零箭图变体的类似物；2) 发展带多重边的框架幂零箭图表示的新稳定性条件；3) 利用这些新稳定性条件与Hecke对应之间的相互作用。

Result: 成功构造了新的箭图变体，并将Saito的几何构造从对称情形扩展到可对称化情形。

Conclusion: 通过引入带多重边的幂零箭图变体，实现了Kashiwara晶体几何构造的推广，为更广泛类型的Kac-Moody代数提供了几何实现。

Abstract: We construct analogues of Nakajima's nilpotent quiver varieties for quivers with multiplicities, by developing new stability conditions for framed nilpotent quiver representations with multiplicities and exploiting their interplay with Hecke correspondences. Using these new quiver varieties, we extend Saito's geometric construction of Kashiwara crystals of irreducible highest-weight representations of Kac--Moody algebras from the symmetric to the symmetrisable case.

</details>


### [218] [Model structures arising from weak cotorsion pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17322)
*Ramin Ebrahimi,Rasool Hafezi,Jiaqun Wei*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在阿贝尔范畴中定义了弱投射模型结构，证明了其与左弱余挠对的一一对应，这是Beligiannis-Reiten对应的推广。特别地，证明了支撑τ-倾斜模能诱导模型结构，并建立了支撑τ-倾斜模与某类模型结构之间的双射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倾斜模可诱导余挠对，从而获得模型结构的同伦解释；但支撑τ-倾斜模只能诱导弱余挠对，经典Beligiannis-Reiten对应不再适用。需要建立适用于支撑τ-倾斜模的推广理论。

Method: 定义了弱投射模型结构，给出了其与满足温和条件的左弱余挠对之间的双射关系；随后利用支撑τ-倾斜模诱导的左弱余挠对构造对应的模型结构。

Result: 建立了弱投射模型结构与左弱余挠对的一一对应（推广了Beligiannis-Reiten对应）；证明了支撑τ-倾斜模可以诱导模型结构；建立了支撑τ-倾斜模与某类模型结构之间的双射。

Conclusion: 从τ-倾斜理论的视角和哲学出发，将Beligiannis-Reiten对应推广到弱投射模型结构情形，为支撑τ-倾斜模提供了模型结构层面的同伦论解释。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{A}$ be an abelian category. Beligiannis and Reiten proved that there is a bijective correspondence between so-called projective model structures on $\mathcal{A}$ and hereditary cotorsion pairs in $\mathcal{A}$ with a contravariantly finite core.
  It is well-known that, tilting modules induce cotorsion pairs, so we may have a homotopicl interpretation of tilting modules. But a recent generalization of tilting modules, support $τ$-tilting modules, induce weak cotorsion pairs.
  In this paper, we define weak projective model structures and prove that there is a bijective correspondence between weak projective model structures and left weak cotorsion pairs satisfying some mild conditions. This is a generalization of Beligiannis-Reiten correspondence from the perspective and philosophy of $τ$-tilting theory. In particular, we prove that any support $τ$-tilting module induce a model structure, and there is bijective correspondence between support $τ$-tilting modules and a certain class of model structures.

</details>


### [219] [A Serre-type criterion for $n$-Gorenstein rings and its application to Nakayama algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17497)
*Dawei Shen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对左右Noetherian环，提出了一个用不可分解内射模的首次出现和平坦维数表示的Serre型条件$(G_n)$，并证明$R$是$n$-Gorenstein当且仅当它满足$(G_n)$。随后将该准则应用于Nakayama代数，证明了syzygy filtration保持Auslander-Gorenstein性质，并在2-Gorenstein Nakayama代数类中反射该性质。结合Klász等人的结果，证明了奇数级的单模在其级数上是正则的，解决了他们的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一种新的Serre型条件来刻画n-Gorenstein环，并应用于解决Nakayama代数中关于Auslander-Gorenstein性质的猜想。

Method: 提出了用不可分解内射模的首次出现和平坦维数定义的Serre型条件$(G_n)$；通过syzygy filtration对Nakayama代数进行研究。

Result: 证明了$R$是$n$-Gorenstein当且仅当满足$(G_n)$；syzygy filtration保持Auslander-Gorenstein性质并在2-Gorenstein Nakayama代数中反射该性质；奇数级单模在其级数上正则。

Conclusion: 解决了Klász、Kleinau和Marczinzik提出的关于Auslander-Gorenstein Nakayama代数中奇数级单模正则性的猜想。

Abstract: Let $R$ be a left and right Noetherian ring. We introduce a Serre-type condition $(G_n)$, formulated in terms of the first occurrence and the flat dimension of indecomposable injective modules, and prove that $R$ is $n$-Gorenstein if and only if it satisfies $(G_n)$. We then apply the criterion to Nakayama algebras via syzygy filtration. It is shown that syzygy filtration preserves the Auslander-Gorenstein property and reflects it within the class of $2$-Gorenstein Nakayama algebras. Combined with a result of Klász, Kleinau and Marczinzik, this shows that simple modules of odd grade over Auslander-Gorenstein Nakayama algebras are regular in their grade, thereby settling their conjecture.

</details>


### [220] [Space of norms on locally algebraic representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17605)
*Alexandre Pyvovarov*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究p-adic群表示上拓展Goldman-Iwahori距离的性质，证明了有界轨道等价于存在G-不变范数，并给出了不变范数存在的积分代数和类型-Hecke条件，最后对GL_n(F)上的BS(r)表示进行了特化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在p-adic约化群G的表示论中，刻画局部代数表示V=π_sm⊗_E σ_alg上的G-不变范数的存在性条件是一个基础性问题。不变范数的存在与表示的酉化、模空间的几何紧化等密切相关。

Method: 通过固定参考范数α_0，构造有限距离分量N_{α_0}(V)作为广义Bruhat-Tits建筑的投射极限；定义均匀上确界度量（ℓ^∞型），直接证明轨道有界当且仅当分量包含G-不变范数（作为轨道的逐点上确界）；提出积分群代数和类型-Hecke条件作为不变范数存在的必要条件。

Result: 1. N_{α_0}(V)在均匀上确界度量下是完备的，但一般不是CAT(0)空间。2. G-轨道有界当且仅当N_{α_0}(V)包含G-不变范数，且该不变范数等于轨道的逐点上确界。3. 给出了不变范数存在的积分群代数与类型-Hecke条件。4. 对GL_n(F)上的特殊表示BS(r)=π_gen(r)⊗_E π_alg(r)进行了具体分析。

Conclusion: 建立了一般p-adic约化群表示上不变范数存在性的充要条件（轨道有界性），并给出了可操作的代数判据，为后续研究p-adic群表示的酉化理论和建筑几何提供了新工具。

Abstract: Let $F$ and $E$ be finite extensions of $\mathbb Q_p$, let $\mathbb G$ be a reductive group over $F$, and put $G=\mathbb G(F)$. Let $V$ be a locally algebraic representation of the form $V=π_{\mathrm{sm}}\otimes_Eσ_{\mathrm{alg}}$, where $π_{\mathrm{sm}}$ is smooth admissible and $σ_{\mathrm{alg}}$ is finite-dimensional algebraic. We study the extended Goldman--Iwahori distance on the set of non-Archimedean norms on $V$. After fixing a reference norm $α_0$, its finite-distance component $\mathscr N_{α_0}(V)$ is the bounded projective limit of the extended Bruhat--Tits buildings attached to $V_K=π_{\mathrm{sm}}^K\otimes_Eσ_{\mathrm{alg}}$. It is complete for the resulting uniform sup metric; this metric is of $\ell^\infty$ type and is generally not CAT(0). We prove directly that a $G$-orbit in $\mathscr N_{α_0}(V)$ is bounded if and only if this component contains a $G$-invariant norm. The invariant norm is the pointwise supremum of the orbit. We formulate an integral group-algebra and type-Hecke condition necessary for an invariant norm. For $G=GL_n(F)$ we specialise to $V=\operatorname{BS}(r)=π_{\mathrm{gen}}(r)\otimes_E π_{\mathrm{alg}}(r)$.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [$0$-cycles and sheaves on abelian surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16364)
*Giovanni Mongardi,Gianluca Pacienza,Laura Pertusi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种在阿贝尔曲面 $A$ 的 Chow 环上的过滤（受 O'Grady 对 K3 曲面的过滤启发），给出了其几何描述，并证明了该过滤与 $A$ 的广义 Kummer 簇中点的有理轨道深度相关。提出了关于该阿贝尔曲面上层的第二陈类的一个猜想，并提供了部分证据及一些应用证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 O'Grady 在 K3 曲面上过滤工作的启发，作者希望在阿贝尔曲面的 Chow 环上建立类似的过滤结构，并探索其与广义 Kummer 簇几何性质之间的深层联系。

Method: 通过类比 K3 曲面的 O'Grady 过滤，在阿贝尔曲面的 Chow 环上定义了一种过滤；利用几何描述刻画该过滤；通过分析广义 Kummer 簇中点的有理轨道来建立过滤与轨道之间的关联；提出关于第二陈类的猜想并给出部分验证和证明。

Result: 成功建立了阿贝尔曲面 Chow 环上的过滤，给出了其几何描述，并证明了该过滤与广义 Kummer 簇中点的有理轨道之间的深刻联系。对于第二陈类的猜想提供了部分证据，并证明了该猜想的一些应用。

Conclusion: 该工作将 O'Grady 过滤的思想推广到了阿贝尔曲面，揭示了 Chow 环过滤与广义 Kummer 簇轨道结构之间的内在联系。关于第二陈类的猜想若被完全证明，将进一步推动对该类曲面向量丛或层的理解。

Abstract: We introduce a filtration on the Chow ring of an abelian surface $A$, inspired by O'Grady's filtration on K3 surfaces. We give a geometric description of the filtration, and we prove that it is deeply linked with the rational orbit of points in the generalized Kummer variety of $A$. We propose a conjecture on the second Chern class of sheaves on this abelian surface, and we provide some evidence for the conjecture and prove some of its applications.

</details>


### [222] [Fields of Moduli of Smooth Cubic Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16519)
*Tianzhi Yang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特征0域上，所有光滑三次曲面都在其模域上存在模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机是理解代数簇在什么条件下可以在其模域上定义模型，这是一个基本且重要的问题。

Method: 论文在特征0的代数闭域上，通过分析光滑三次曲面的自同构群和模域的性质，证明了该存在性结论。

Result: 主要结果是：在特征0的条件下，每个光滑三次曲面都在其模域上存在一个模型。

Conclusion: 结论表明，对于光滑三次曲面这一特定类型的代数簇，模域上模型的存在性在特征0下是普遍成立的。

Abstract: The field of moduli of a variety $X$ over an algebraically closed field $K$ is defined as the fixed field of those automorphisms $σ$ of $K$ for which $X\simeq X^σ$. A fundamental question is under what conditions a variety admits a model over its field of moduli. We show that, in characteristic $0$, every smooth cubic surface has a model over its field of moduli.

</details>


### [223] [Finite Group Reduction of the DR/DZ Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16607)
*Si-Qi Liu,Youjin Zhang,Tianwen Zhong*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了半单CohFT的Dubrovin-Zhang/双分支层次结构的有限群约化保持双哈密顿结构和tau结构。应用于$\mathbb{P}^1_{2,2,2,2}$的orbifold Gromov-Witten理论，发现Hurwitz空间$M_{1;1}$上的Frobenius流形结构可关联两个不同的可积层次，其中一个等价于亏格1的拓扑递归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究半单CohFT的Dubrovin-Zhang/双分支层次结构的有限群约化是否保持其双哈密顿结构和tau结构，并探索由此产生的可积层次与Frobenius流形结构之间的联系。

Method: 通过有限群约化方法，将半单CohFT的层次结构进行约化，并验证约化后结构的保持性；具体应用于$\mathbb{P}^1_{2,2,2,2}$的orbifold Gromov-Witten理论，分析Hurwitz空间$M_{1;1}$上的Frobenius流形结构。

Result: 有限群约化保持了半单CohFT层次结构的双哈密顿性质和tau结构；在$\mathbb{P}^1_{2,2,2,2}$的orbifold Gromov-Witten理论中，Hurwitz空间$M_{1;1}$上的Frobenius流形可导出两个不同的可积层次，其中一个与亏格1的拓扑递归等价。

Conclusion: 有限群约化对半单CohFT层次结构的结构保持性成立，且该结果揭示了Hurwitz空间上Frobenius流形与多个可积层次及拓扑递归之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We show that the finite group reduction of the Dubrovin-Zhang/Double Ramification hierarchy associated to a semisimple CohFT preserves its bihamiltonian structure and its tau structure. By applying this result to the orbifold Gromov-Witten theory with the target $\mathbb{P}^1_{2,2,2,2}$, we see that there are two different and interesting integrable hierarchies that can be associated to the Frobenius manifold structure on the Hurwitz space $M_{1;1}$. One of them is equivalent to the genus $1$ topological recursion.

</details>


### [224] [Non-general type surfaces in P4, an update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16753)
*Hirotachi Abo,Kristian Ranestad,Frank-Olaf Schreyer*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文综述了P4中非一般型光滑曲面的分类现状，以及经典和计算机辅助构造方法，证明了Hartshorne-Lichtenbaum猜想：一般代数曲面无法嵌入P4，且Ellingsrud和Peskine给出了P4中光滑有理曲面（及非一般型曲面）的度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Hartshorne-Lichtenbaum猜想，即一般代数曲面不能嵌入P4，并为P4中光滑有理曲面（及非一般型曲面）建立度界。

Method: 通过综述分类现状，结合经典构造与计算机辅助构造方法，对P4中非一般型光滑曲面进行系统研究。

Result: 证实了一般代数曲面不能嵌入P4，并给出了P4中光滑有理曲面及非一般型曲面的度界。

Conclusion: Hartshorne-Lichtenbaum猜想成立，且P4中非一般型光滑曲面的分类与构造取得重要进展。

Abstract: A general algebraic surface cannot be embedded in P4. Proving a conjecture by Hartshorne and Lichtenbaum, Ellingsrud and Peskine showed that there is a degree bound for smooth rational surfaces in P4, and in fact for surfaces not of general type. We give a survey of the classification status and of classical and computer-aided constructions of smooth non-general type surfaces in P4.

</details>


### [225] [Explicit LCP of MDS Codes and LCD Codes on Hyperelliptic Curves via Mumford Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16945)
*Adler Marques,Yuri da Silva,Saeed Tafazolian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了亏格 g≥2 的超椭圆曲线上具有互补性质的代数几何码。通过 Mumford 表示的次数刻画了 g 和 g-1 次非特殊除子，利用雅可比簇上的多项式算术构造了线性互补对（LCP），并在 2-挠条件下将其转化为线性互补对偶（LCD）码。在最大超椭圆曲线上给出了 [2q,q,q+1]_{q^2} 的 MDS LCD 码显式例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典困难的几何问题（非特殊除子的刻画）简化为单变量多项式的单次数测试，并利用超椭圆曲线的代数结构构造具有互补性质的优良码（LCP 和 LCD 码），特别是 MDS 码。

Method: 利用 Mumford 表示的多项式次数来刻画非特殊除子；在雅可比簇上进行多项式算术构造 LCP；通过 2-挠条件（等价于 Mumford 多项式的可整除条件）获得显式乘子，将 LCP 转化为 LCD 码；在最大超椭圆曲线 y²=x^q+x 上具体实现。

Result: 给出了非特殊除子的 Mumford 次数刻画；建立了 LCP 成为 MDS 的 Mumford 次数判据；在 2-挠条件下得到了 LCD 码的显式构造；在最大超椭圆曲线上给出了 q=4,5,7 时参数为 [2q,q,q+1]_{q²} 的 MDS LCD 码计算实例，并猜想对所有 q≥4 成立。

Conclusion: 通过 Mumford 表示将非特殊除子的几何问题代数化，建立了超椭圆曲线上构造 MDS LCD 码的系统框架，并在最大超椭圆曲线上验证了有效性。

Abstract: We study algebraic geometry codes on hyperelliptic curves of genus $g \geq 2$ with complementarity properties. Our first contribution is a characterization of non-special divisors of degree $g$ and $g-1$ via the polynomial degrees of their reduced Mumford representation, reducing a classical hard geometric problem to a single-degree test on univariate polynomials. Using this, we construct Linear Complementary Pairs (LCP) of codes via polynomial arithmetic on the Jacobian and provide a criterion in terms of Mumford degrees for the resulting codes to be Maximum Distance Separable (MDS). Under a $2$-torsion condition in the Jacobian, equivalently a divisibility condition on the Mumford polynomials, we obtain explicit multipliers that turn these pairs into Linear Complementary Dual (LCD) codes. Finally, we apply this framework to the maximal hyperelliptic curve $\mathcal{X} \colon y^2 = x^q + x$ over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ and give explicit examples of MDS LCD codes with parameters $[2q,q,q+1]_{q^2}$ for $q = 4, 5, 7$, verified computationally; we conjecture, with heuristic support, that such codes exist for all $q \geq 4$.

</details>


### [226] [Coincident Poisson structures on principal-bundle moduli spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17433)
*Alexandru Chirvasitu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于简约复代数群$\mathbb{G}$及其线性约化仿射群概形$\mathbb{K}$的作用，由$(\mathbb{K},\mathfrak{g})$-不变非退化对称双线性型诱导的$\mathfrak{p}^*$与$\mathfrak{p}$的扩张，与通过$\mathbb{K}$-不变Cartan/Borel对及相同双线性型从标准双代数结构得到的扩张是$\mathbb{K}$-等变同构的。这进而识别了椭圆曲线上主$\mathbb{P}$-丛模空间光滑点集上的两种Poisson结构（Balduzzi定义与Feigin-Odesskii定义），验证了Feigin-Odesskii关于双代数诱导辛叶与沿$\mathbb{P}\le\mathbb{G}$遗忘结构后互相同构的丛轨道的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Balduzzi和Feigin-Odesskii在椭圆曲线主丛模空间上定义的两类Poisson结构之间的等价性，验证Feigin-Odesskii对双代数诱导辛叶的几何识别。

Method: 通过将椭圆曲线$E$上的丛扩张与$\mathfrak{p}$-模范畴中的扩张建立对应，利用$(\mathbb{K},\mathfrak{g})$-不变对称非退化双线性型在$\mathbb{K}$-不变抛物子群$\mathbb{P}$的Levi分解下诱导的扩张与标准双代数结构扩张的$\mathbb{K}$-等变同构。

Result: 证明了两种扩张的$\mathbb{K}$-等变同构性，进而识别了主$\mathbb{P}$-丛模空间上的两种Poisson结构，并验证了Feigin-Odesskii关于辛叶的几何描述。

Conclusion: 本文建立了在椭圆曲线主丛模空间上两类Poisson结构的等价性，统一了不同构造途径，并给出了双代数诱导辛叶的几何解释。

Abstract: Consider a reductive complex algebraic group $\mathbb{G}$ equipped with an action by a linearly reductive affine group scheme $\mathbb{K}$. The extension of $\mathfrak{p}^*$ by $\mathfrak{p}$ induced by an $(\mathbb{K},\mathfrak{g})$-invariant symmetric non-degenerate bilinear form on $\mathfrak{g}:=Lie(\mathbb{G})$, for a $\mathbb{K}$-invariant parabolic $\mathbb{P}\le \mathbb{G}$, is $\mathbb{K}$-equivariantly isomorphic to the extension obtained via the standard bialgebra structure attached to a $\mathbb{K}$-invariant Cartan/Borel pair $\mathbb{H}\le \mathbb{B}\le \mathbb{P}\le \mathbb{G}$ and the same bilinear form. Associating bundle extensions on an elliptic curve $E$ to said $\mathfrak{p}$-module extensions, this identifies Poisson structures on the smooth locus of the principal-$\mathbb{P}$-bundle moduli space over $E$ respectively defined by Balduzzi (using the former extension) and Feigin-Odesskii (via the standard bialgebra structure). This in particular verifies Feigin-Odesskii's identification of the bialgebra-induced symplectic leaves with the loci of bundles mutually isomorphic after forgetting structure along $\mathbb{P}\le \mathbb{G}$.

</details>


### [227] [Characteristic cycles of constructible sheaves and microlocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17026)
*Takeshi Saito*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究在正特征域光滑代数簇上，可构造层的奇异支撑与特征循环的代数构造，探讨了类比超越情形（Kashiwara-Schapira）的微局部化方法在代数框架下的适用性，并获得了部分正面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正特征域上，可构造层的奇异支撑和特征循环已有定义，但能否通过类似于Kashiwara-Schapira在超越几何中所使用的微局部化方法在代数框架下构造这些对象，是一个尚未完全解决的问题。本文旨在研究这一方法在代数环境中的可行性。

Method: 采用微局部化技术，尝试将Kashiwara-Schapira在超越情形的构造方法移植到正特征域的代数几何框架中，分析其适用条件和障碍，并证明部分正面结果。

Result: 证明了在代数设定下，类似Kashiwara-Schapira的微局部化方法能够部分实现奇异支撑和特征循环的构造。

Conclusion: 在正特征域光滑代数簇上，利用微局部化进行奇异支撑与特征循环的代数构造是部分可行的，为后续完全解决这一问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: For a constructible sheaf on a smooth scheme over a field of positive characteristic, the singular support and the characteristic cycle are defined on the cotangent bundle. We study if a construction using the microlocalization similar to that in a transcendental setting due to Kashiwara--Schapira works in the algebraic setting and prove partial positive results.

</details>


### [228] [Expressivity of Shallow Neural Networks Over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17090)
*Maksym Zubkov,Carol Wu,Shiwei Yang,Param Mody,Yifei Chen*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上具有单项式激活函数的浅层多项式神经网络的表达能力，通过神经流形的基数来量化表达能力，并推导了上下界。研究还发现了特征零域与有限特征域在神经流形上的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解有限域上多项式神经网络的表达能力，通过计数神经流形中的有理点来量化网络的表达 richness。

Method: 将神经流形定义为从网络权重到多项式环乘积的映射的像，通过有限域上有理点的计数（与Weil猜想相关）推导表达能力上下界，并比较不同特征域下的神经流形差异。

Result: 推导了神经流形基数的自然上下界，并发现某些架构在特征零域与有限特征域下的神经流形存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 域特征在神经网络表达能力中扮演关键角色，有限域上的表达能力分析需要借助代数几何工具如Weil猜想。

Abstract: We study the expressivity of shallow polynomial neural networks (PNNs) with monomial activation functions over finite fields. For a given architecture, we define a neuromanifold as the image of the map from all possible network weights into the product of polynomial rings. We quantify the expressivity by the cardinality of the neuromanifold, and derive a natural lower and upper bound. This leads to counting rational points over finite fields, a problem closely linked to the Weil conjectures. Finally, we present an architecture that exhibits a striking difference in the neuromanifolds when considered over a characteristic zero versus a finite-characteristic field, illustrating the critical role of field characteristic in the notion of expressivity.

</details>


### [229] [Regions represented as foliated forms and natural smooth maps onto them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17180)
*Naoki Kitazawa*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了由超曲面包围的区域及其到单位球面的自然光滑映射（特别是特殊通用映射和矩映射），重点讨论了这些区域通过一维函数族及其零集形成的叶状结构，以及通过规范投影复合得到的显式函数的拓扑/组合性质，最后探讨了此类函数的一阶导数和临界集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者对超曲面包围的区域及其上的自然光滑映射感兴趣，特别关注特殊通用映射和矩映射，旨在从可微映射的奇点理论角度理解这些区域的叶状结构和相关函数的拓扑性质。

Method: 通过一维函数族及其零集对区域进行叶状结构分析，利用规范投影的复合构造显式函数，并研究这些函数的拓扑与组合性质；作为新挑战，进一步分析函数的一阶导数及其临界集。

Result: 给出了关于超曲面包围区域上的光滑映射（特殊通用映射、矩映射）的叶状结构描述，以及通过规范投影复合所得函数的性质；特别地，讨论了函数一阶导数的临界集。

Conclusion: 该工作属于可微映射奇点理论及其在微分拓扑和实几何中的应用，通过一维函数族和零集的研究为理解超曲面区域的叶状结构和相关映射提供了新视角。

Abstract: The author is interested in regions surrounded by hypersurfaces and natural smooth maps onto them respecting the canonical projections of the unit spheres and so-called special generic maps and moment maps, more generally. We consider situations where these regions are foliated via 1-dimensional families of functions and their zero sets (smoothly).
  We including the author are also interested in explicit and nice functions obtained by composing the canonical projections and their topological or combinatorial properties. This is of singularity theory of differentiable maps and applications to differential topology and various real geometry. Explicitly, here, as a new challenge, we discuss the 1st derivative of such a function and critical sets of this.

</details>


### [230] [Torsors over the universal Jacobian over $\mathcal{M}_g$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17206)
*Qixiao Ma*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于光滑亏格g≥4的曲线族，其相对雅可比簇下的每个挠子都与相对皮卡概形的某个连通分支同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解光滑曲线族上相对雅可比簇挠子与相对皮卡概形连通分支之间的结构关系。

Method: 通过对光滑亏格g曲线族（g≥4）进行代数几何分析，比较相对雅可比簇挠子与相对皮卡概形的连通分支结构。

Result: 对于g≥4，每个相对雅可比簇的挠子均同构于相对皮卡概形的某个连通分支。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了在足够高的亏格条件下，相对雅可比簇挠子与相对皮卡概形之间的紧密对应关系。

Abstract: We consider the universal family of smooth genus-$g$ curves over $\mathbb{C}$. We show that for $g\geq4$, every torsor under the relative Jacobian is isomorphic to a connected component of the relative Picard scheme.

</details>


### [231] [Algebraic Knots and their Universal K^MW_2-Coverings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17716)
*Matthias Wendt,Thor Wittich*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在特征非2的基域（包括代数闭域）上，为$\mathbb{A}^1 \hookrightarrow \mathbb{A}^3$闭嵌入的补空间构造了万有阿贝尔$\underline{\operatorname{K}}^{\operatorname{MW}}_2$-覆盖，并通过动机同伦理论将纽结理论思想提升到代数几何中，得到了此类嵌入的可纠正性不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将纽结理论中的思想（如环绕数、可纠正性等）通过动机同伦理论提升到代数几何中，从而为代数闭嵌入（特别是$\mathbb{A}^1 \hookrightarrow \mathbb{A}^3$）建立新的不变量。

Method: 在特征非2的基域上，利用动机同伦理论和$\underline{\operatorname{K}}^{\operatorname{MW}}_2$（Milnor-Witt K-理论层），构造万有阿贝尔覆盖；通过该覆盖提取嵌入的不变量。

Result: 成功构造了$\mathbb{A}^1 \hookrightarrow \mathbb{A}^3$闭嵌入补空间的万有阿贝尔$\underline{\operatorname{K}}^{\operatorname{MW}}_2$-覆盖，并由此导出了一个可纠正性不变量。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了代数几何与纽结理论之间新的联系，为代数嵌入的分类提供了动机同伦理论视角下的新工具。

Abstract: Over suitable base fields $k$ of characteristic not $2$, including algebraically closed ones, we construct universal abelian $\underline{\operatorname{K}}^{\operatorname{MW}}_2$-coverings for complements of closed embeddings $\mathbb{A}^1 \hookrightarrow \mathbb{A}^3$. Using these, we obtain a rectifiability invariant of such embeddings by lifting knot-theoretic ideas to algebraic geometry via motivic homotopy theory.

</details>


### [232] [The Miracle of Flatness in Algebraic Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17406)
*Ivan Zelich*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了代数几何中平坦性的多个方面：半环上的平坦性及相关结果、忠实平坦环映射的可下降性及反例构造，以及极大平展位点的仿射性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解代数几何中平坦性概念在不同语境下的性质，特别是半环上的平坦性、忠实平坦映射的可下降性问题，以及极大平展位点的仿射性。

Method: 利用同伦论模型结构研究半环上的导出群化函子；通过模论中的非消没杯积构造高指数的不可下降忠实平坦环映射；利用Artin-Rees引理的变体、D-模刻画和倾斜对应证明极大平展位点的仿射性。

Result: 证明了半环上的离散性结果及fppf代数性质；构造了不可下降的忠实平坦环映射并揭示了下降指数与基数的精确关系；在完全一般性下证明了极大平展位点的仿射性定理。

Conclusion: 本文系统推进了代数几何中平坦性理论的研究，在三个核心方向上取得了重要进展：半环平坦性、可下降性与极大平展位点的仿射性。

Abstract: This thesis studies various aspects of flatness in algebraic geometry. We first study flatness in the context of semi-rings. We prove some discreteness results for a derived groupification functor with respect to the homotopy theoretic model structure on simplicial semirings, compare the Zariski and fppf topology, and study fppf algebras over the positive reals. We then study descendibility properties of faithfully flat ring maps; we in particular construct a non-descendable faithfully flat ring map, and then construct examples demonstrating the precise the relationship between the exponent of descendibility and cardinality by developing a rather general method to convert module-theoretic non-vanishing "cup-products" to descendable faithfully flat ring maps with sufficiently high exponent. Finally, we prove the affineness of the maximal etale locus of morphism $X \to Y$ of schemes with $X$ and $Y$ locally Noetherian schemes, $X$ normal and $Y$ regular in fully generality. Some notable aspects of this chapter is a variant of Artin-Rees' lemma that holds for non-Noetherian rings, a characterization of $D$-modules over unramified regular local rings (similar to those obtained by O. Gabber and W. Zhang), and Tor-independence result for global sections of étale schemes which we prove by a variant of the tilting correspondence which doesn't require completion.

</details>


### [233] [The Springer Geometry of Specht Polynomials and Schubert cycle positivity for two row Springer fiber components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17487)
*Hunter Spink,Vasu Tewari*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了A型Springer表示可通过Specht多项式在完全旗簇的同调中几何实现，将Specht模的生成元与一族不相交的Levi-Richardson簇的类等同，该簇族退化为相应的Springer纤维。对于两行划分，给出了显式组合展开，解决了Springer的Schubert正性问题，证明了Precup和Sabando-Alvarez的两个猜想，并将Springer基与web基等同。作为应用，推导了旗簇Poisson退化轨迹分量的Schubert循环展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Springer表示与Specht多项式之间的几何联系，解决Springer关于Springer纤维分量的Schubert正性问题，并为相关猜想提供证明。

Method: 通过Specht多项式在完全旗簇同调中几何实现A型Springer表示，将Specht模生成元与Levi-Richardson簇的类等同，并通过退化到Springer纤维实现正性展开。对两行划分给出组合显式展开。

Result: 解决了Springer关于两行纤维的Schubert正性问题，证明了Precup和Sabando-Alvarez的两个猜想，将Springer基与web基等同，推导了Poisson退化轨迹分量的Schubert循环展开。

Conclusion: Springer表示与Specht模之间存在深刻的几何联系，通过Schubert循环、Joseph多项式和Specht多项式构成的正性展开链，为Springer纤维分量的正性问题提供了完整解答。

Abstract: We show that the type A Springer representation is realized geometrically in the homology of the complete flag variety by Specht polynomials. For any partition, we identify the classical Specht polynomial generators of the Specht module with the classes of a family of disjoint Levi--Richardson varieties, and this family degenerates to the corresponding Springer fiber. This factors Springer's Schubert positivity problem for Springer fiber components through a chain of positive expansions, from Specht polynomials through the Joseph polynomials to the Schubert cycles.
  For two-row partitions we make each of these expansions combinatorially explicit, giving manifestly nonnegative Schubert cycle expansions of both the Levi-Richardson cycles and the Springer fiber components. This resolves Springer's question for two-row fibers and proves two conjectures of Precup and Sabando-Alvarez, and identifies the Springer basis with the web basis for two-row Specht modules. As an application, we deduce the Schubert cycle expansions of the components of the Poisson degeneracy locus of the flag variety.

</details>


### [234] [$Θ$-reductivity and $S$-completeness for adjoint Fano foliated structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17878)
*Theodoros Stylianos Papazachariou*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了t-K-半稳定伴随Fano叶状结构的模问题的Θ-约化性和S-完备性的取值判据，发展了混合Ding理论，证明了伴随叶状结构的任意理想伴随倒置，建立了相对提取和有限生成定理，并应用于证明t-K-多稳定退化的唯一性、自同构群的约化性及t-K-稳定情形下自同构群的有限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立t-K-半稳定伴随Fano叶状结构的模空间的紧化理论，为其几何性质提供严格的取值判据基础。

Method: 发展了任意线性有界乘法滤子的混合Ding理论；证明了伴随叶状结构下任意理想的伴随倒置定理；建立了相对提取和有限生成定理。

Result: 证明了Θ-约化性和S-完备性的取值判据；证明了t-K-多稳定退化的唯一性；证明了自同构群的约化性；在t-K-稳定情形下证明了自同构群的有限性。

Conclusion: 该工作为伴随Fano叶状结构的模问题提供了完整的理论框架，所得结果对理解叶状结构模空间的紧化与稳定性具有重要意义。

Abstract: We prove the valuative criteria of $Θ$-reductivity and $S$-completeness for the moduli problem of $t$-K-semistable adjoint Fano foliated structures. We develop a mixed Ding theory for arbitrary linearly bounded multiplicative filtrations, prove inversion of adjunction with arbitrary ideals for adjoint foliated structures, and establish a relative extraction and finite generation theorem. Together, these results yield the required relative extension theorems for families. As applications, we prove uniqueness of $t$-K-polystable degenerations, reductivity of the automorphism group of $t$-K-polystable adjoint Fano foliated structures, and finiteness of the automorphism group in the $t$-K-stable case.

</details>


### [235] [Białynicki-Birula Decompositions of Nakajima Quiver Varieties, Quiver Chains and Star-Shaped Quivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17891)
*Juan Sebastian Numpaque-Roa*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Nakajima箭图簇上由双箭图反向箭头缩放诱导的自然C*-作用的Białynicki-Birula分解，将不动点用称为箭图链的辅助箭图的表示描述，并分析了吸引纤维的维数及相应的动机分解，最后对星形箭图特殊情况计算了完整Nakajima箭图簇的动机类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Nakajima箭图簇上自然C*-作用的不动点结构及其Białynicki-Birula分解的几何性质。

Method: 利用双箭图反向箭头缩放定义C*-作用，通过辅助箭图（箭图链）的带关系表示刻画不动点，研究Białynicki-Birula分解的吸引纤维维数和动机分解，并特化到星形箭图进行分类和计算。

Result: 不动点由箭图链表示描述；得到了吸引纤维的维数；建立了以箭图链模空间表达的动机分解；对星形箭图完成了不动点分支分类并计算了完整动机类。

Conclusion: 本文系统给出了Nakajima箭图簇在该C*-作用下的Białynicki-Birula分解的几何描述，并通过星形箭图特例展示了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies the Białynicki--Birula decomposition associated with the natural $\mathbb C^*$-action on Nakajima quiver varieties given by scaling the maps along the reversed arrows of the doubled quiver. Fixed points of this action are described in terms of representations with relations of auxiliary quivers, called quiver chains. Several aspects of the geometry of the resulting Białynicki--Birula decomposition are also investigated, including the dimensions of their attracting fibers and the corresponding motivic decomposition of the Nakajima quiver variety in terms of quiver-chain moduli spaces. Finally, the general framework is specialized to star-shaped quivers, where, for particular dimension vectors, the fixed-locus components are classified and the motivic class of the full Nakajima quiver variety is computed in a suitable localization of the Grothendieck ring of varieties.

</details>


### [236] [Degenerating Discriminants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17966)
*Viktoriia Borovik,Clara Briand*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了射影对偶簇与判别式在平坦退化（特别是Gröbner退化）下的行为，证明了余法丛在该退化下具有对偶坐标的反向权重结构，并利用Whitney分层和Sabbah公式描述了退化特殊纤维的不可约分支和重数，进而推广到Grassmannian中的高阶关联超曲面。应用包括孤立奇点超曲面的经典公式、一般完全交与互易线性空间的退化分析，以及参数化多项式系统混合判别式的理论关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解射影对偶簇和判别式在退化过程中的结构变化，建立从退化特殊纤维恢复极限对偶超曲面的代数几何框架，并将结果推广到更高阶的关联超曲面。

Method: 利用Gröbner退化构造余法丛的退化（在对偶坐标上赋予反向权重），结合Whitney分层与Sabbah公式分析特殊纤维的不可约分支与重数，再将结论推广至Grassmannian中的高阶关联超曲面场景。

Result: 建立了余法丛在Gröbner退化下的对偶权重结构，给出了极限对偶超曲面的不可约分支与重数描述；将结果推广到Grassmannian中的高阶关联超曲面；在孤立奇点超曲面、一般完全交、互易线性空间及混合判别式等场景中得到应用。

Conclusion: 该工作为射影对偶性与判别式理论在退化情形下的行为提供了系统的方法，并展示了其在代数几何与多项式系统分析中的广泛适用性。

Abstract: We study the behavior of projective dual varieties and discriminants under flat degenerations. For Gröbner degenerations, we show that the conormal variety admits a Gröbner degeneration with opposite weights on the dual coordinates. Using Whitney stratifications and Sabbah's formula, we describe the irreducible components and multiplicities of the special fiber of this degeneration, and hence of the limiting dual hypersurface. We then extend these results to higher associated hypersurfaces in Grassmannians. As applications, we recover classical formulas for hypersurfaces with isolated singularities, analyze degenerations of generic complete intersections and reciprocal linear spaces, and relate the theory to mixed discriminants of parametrized polynomial systems.

</details>


### [237] [Resolution of the Prym map in genus 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18134)
*Dmitry Zakharov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用热带三角曲线构造描述了Prym周期映射$\overline{R}_4\dashrightarrow\overline{A}_3$的极小环面解消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Prym周期映射的紧化与解消问题，特别是模空间$\overline{R}_4$到$\overline{A}_3$的映射行为。

Method: 采用热带三角曲线构造（tropical trigonal construction）作为主要工具。

Result: 给出了Prym周期映射$\overline{R}_4\dashrightarrow\overline{A}_3$的极小环面解消的具体描述。

Conclusion: 热带三角曲线构造有效地描述了该映射的环面解消，有助于理解相关模空间的几何结构。

Abstract: We use the tropical trigonal construction to describe the minimal toroidal resolution of the Prym period map $\overline{R}_4\dashrightarrow\overline{A}_3$.

</details>


### [238] [Integral Weyl Invariants in Chow Characteristic Images of Spin and Special Clifford Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18188)
*Sanghoon Baek*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分裂旋量群Spin(n)（n≥7）的Benson-Wood生成集中递归定义的整值Weyl不变量q_i属于Chow特征像的条件，发现仅当n=10, i=3时成立。类似地，特殊Clifford群Γ⁺(n)的递归不变量f_i中仅f_2（Γ⁺(7)）属于该像。在复数域上，分类空间BG的整数上同调的无挠商中对应q_i的类恰好在(n,i)=(10,3)时是代数的。所有非例外类在添加任意挠类后仍为非代数的，可由Bockstein-Steenrod运算检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Karpenko的Steenrod理论障碍推广到分裂旋量群Spin(n)（n≥7），分类Benson-Wood生成集中哪些递归定义的整值Weyl不变量q_i属于Chow特征像，并得到特殊Clifford群Γ⁺(n)的类似分类，进而确定这些类在整数上同调中何时为代数类。

Method: 利用扩展的Steenrod理论障碍方法，结合Benson-Wood生成集和递归定义的整值Weyl不变量，对Spin(n)和Γ⁺(n)进行分类分析，并通过Bockstein-Steenrod运算检测非代数性。

Result: 在Benson-Wood生成集中，仅q_3（Spin(10)）属于Chow特征像；在Γ⁺(n)的有限生成范围内，仅f_2（Γ⁺(7)）属于该像。在复数域上，BG的整数上同调无挠商中对应q_i的类恰在(10,3)时代数。所有非例外类添加任意挠类后仍非代数。

Conclusion: 成功分类了Spin(n)和Γ⁺(n)中属于Chow特征像的递归不变量，确定了其代数性的精确条件，并给出了非代数性的Bockstein-Steenrod运算检测方法。

Abstract: Let $G=\Spin(n)$ be the split spin group over an arbitrary field, with $n\ge7$. Extending a Steenrod-theoretic obstruction of Karpenko, we classify the recursively defined integral Weyl invariants $q_i$ in the Benson--Wood generating set that lie in the Chow characteristic image: the only such invariant is $q_3$ for $\Spin(10)$. We obtain the analogous classification for the recursive invariants $f_i$ of the special Clifford group $Γ^+(n)$: in their finite generating range, the only such invariant is $f_2$ for $Γ^+(7)$. Over $\mathbb C$, the class corresponding to $q_i$ in the torsion-free quotient of the integral cohomology of the classifying space $BG$ is algebraic precisely when $(n,i)=(10,3)$. For each $n$, a single smooth projective approximation simultaneously realizes all the corresponding classes in the finite range. Every nonexceptional class remains nonalgebraic after the addition of any torsion class, as detected by a Bockstein--Steenrod operation.

</details>
